Abstract: Recent research has increasingly focused on understanding how Transformers store and process knowledge, as well as how this knowledge can be edited. Research work in this area is often conducted in two phases: first, phenomena are explored on individual samples. Then, when results appear promising, more statistically robust experiments follow. To support the first phase, we propose KnowledgeDebugger, a GUI-based exploration tool for knowledge localization and editing in Transformers. Our tool - inspired by LM-Debugger - offers no-code access to the methods in EasyEdit, a widely used library of state-of-the-art Knowledge Editing approaches. We demonstrate the tool's effectiveness through case studies of recent findings in this field.

</details>


### [49] [Logit-Contribution Scoring Identifies Non-Literal Retrieval Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01002)
*Aryo Pradipta Gema,Beatrice Alex,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Logit-Contribution Scoring (LOCOS)，一种写感知的注意力头检测器，用于识别长上下文中执行非字面检索（综合）的头。LOCOS 通过将 OV 电路输出投影到答案 token 的 unembedding 方向来评分，在单次前向传播中对比 needle 和 off-needle 位置。实验表明，消融 LOCOS 选出的头能以更少的头数使 ROUGE-L 崩溃，且仅影响检索任务，不损害参数记忆和算术推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的注意力头检测器基于字面复制标准（被关注 token 与生成 token 匹配），只能捕捉头“读取”的位置，无法捕捉头通过 OV 电路“写入”的非字面综合信息，因此会遗漏执行语义综合的关键头。

Method: 引入 LOCOS：对每个头，计算其 OV 电路输出在答案 token unembedding 方向上的投影，对比 needle（相关上下文）和 off-needle（无关上下文）源位置的贡献差异，单次前向传播即可完成打分。

Result: 在 Qwen3、Gemma-3、OLMo-3.1 三个模型家族上，消融前 50 个 LOCOS 头使 NoLiMa 基准 ROUGE-L 从 0.401 降至 0.000，而最强基线保留 0.292；同一消融对 MuSiQue（0.55→0.08）和 BABI-Long（0.62→0.20）也显著下降，随机头控制组仅下降 0.05 以内。参数记忆和算术推理保持基线水平，证明所选头具有检索特异性。

Conclusion: LOCOS 能准确定位长上下文中负责非字面语义综合的注意力头，为解释长上下文模型行为提供了更精确的工具，且消融实验证实这些头专门服务于检索任务，不影响其他能力。

Abstract: In long-context use, large language models frequently synthesize answers from the meaning of a relevant context span rather than literally copy-pasting them. Identifying which attention heads perform this synthesis matters for interpreting long-context model behavior. Yet existing detectors miss these heads by construction: they reward heads whose attended token matches the generated token, a literal-copy criterion that captures where a head reads but not what it writes through its output-value (OV) circuit, the very mechanism that carries non-literal retrieval. We introduce Logit-Contribution Scoring (LOCOS), a write-aware detector that scores each head by the projection of its OV-circuit output onto the answer-token unembedding direction, contrasting needle and off-needle source positions in a single forward pass. Across three model families (Qwen3, Gemma-3, OLMo-3.1), mean-ablating the top LOCOS heads on the NoLiMa non-literal retrieval benchmark collapses ROUGE-L at lower head counts than prior attention-based detections; on Qwen3-8B, ablating 50 heads drives ROUGE-L from 0.401 to 0.000 while the strongest baseline still retains 0.292. The selected heads are retrieval-specific: parametric recall and arithmetic reasoning stay at baseline under the same ablation. On Qwen3-8B, the same ablation also drops MuSiQue from 0.55 to 0.08 and BABI-Long from 0.62 to 0.20, while a random-heads control stays within 0.05 of baseline.

</details>


### [50] [Understanding Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01006)
*Yannik Keller,Thomas Eisenmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本章综述了大语言模型(LLM)的当前理解，涵盖Transformer架构、涌现认知能力(符号推理、心智理论、欺骗策略)、可解释AI方法，以及关于LLM真正理解与模式记忆的争论，主张采取更细致的视角看待AI认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在梳理LLM的机制、能力及其与人类认知的关系，回应关于LLM是否具备真实认知还是仅靠模式记忆的核心争议。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，系统分析Transformer架构原理、涌现能力实证研究、可解释AI技术(神经元激活分析、电路追踪)以及哲学认知科学层面的辩论论据。

Result: 证据显示LLM展现出类人认知能力(推理、心智理论、欺骗)，但也存在揭示人机认知差异的失败案例；可解释AI研究开始揭示内部机制；反拟人化论点基于对优化过程和认知能力的误解。

Conclusion: 主张超越简单的二元对立(真实理解vs模式记忆)，既承认人类与LLM认知的本质差异，又避免用过度还原论预设否定AI认知的可能性，呼吁建立更细致的LLM认知理论框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) represent one of the most significant advances in AI and natural language processing in recent years. Still, many pressing questions about their mechanisms, capabilities, and relationship to human cognition remain highly debated. This chapter aims to outline our current understanding of LLMs by discussing recent evidence on emerging capabilities and their mechanistic implementation within processing layers. We begin with a concise overview of the Transformer architecture, emphasizing how the attention mechanism enables training on massive datasets, allowing LLMs to function as generalist rather than specialized models. Next, we examine emergent LLM capabilities that appear to resemble aspects of human cognition, including symbolic reasoning, theory of mind, and deception strategies. Several studies provide evidence that LLMs can solve tasks previously thought to require human-like cognition. Other studies reveal insightful failure cases that shed light on the differences between human and LLM cognition. Alongside these findings, we review explainable AI approaches ranging from neuron activation analysis to circuit tracing. In the final section, we address current debates concerning what LLMs genuinely understand versus what they merely appear to understand. Prominent arguments against AI anthropomorphism point to the simplicity of LLM training objectives, claiming that LLM behavior is better explained by pattern memorization of training data than by genuine cognition. We argue that this standpoint is guided by misconceptions about optimization processes and cognitive capacity, and advocate for a more nuanced discussion of LLM cognition that neither dismisses the differences between humans and LLMs nor precludes the possibility of AI cognition through overly simplistic reductionist arguments.

</details>


### [51] [Reading Order Inference for Complex Document Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01018)
*Iddo Hakim,Sharva Gogawale,Omer Ventura,Gal Grudka,Daria Vasyutinsky-Shapira,Berat Kurar-Barakat,Nachum Dershowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的基于图的阅读顺序推理框架，针对复杂历史手稿（如Glossa Ordinaria布局）中的多流文本阅读顺序问题。该方法将OCR文本行作为节点构建有向候选转移图，利用因果语言模型条件似然和BERT下一句预测加权集成评分边，并通过最大遗憾推理规则避免贪婪边选择的“边窃取”失效，在多个基准上显著优于XY-cut和LayoutReader基线，且具有镜像不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂历史手稿数字化中，阅读顺序推理是关键瓶颈，特别是Glossa Ordinaria等布局包含多个空间交织的阅读流，文本区域非矩形、非凸，传统方法（如XY-cut）和基于布局的模型（如LayoutReader）难以处理这种非线性阅读顺序，且LayoutReader因词级与行级粒度不匹配迁移性差。

Method: 无训练图框架：每个OCR文本行为节点构建有向候选转移图；边由因果语言模型条件似然和BERT NSP加权加性集成评分（第三句嵌入信号经评估未提升效果）；全局阅读顺序建模为度约束有向路径覆盖；提出最大遗憾推理规则避免贪婪边选择的“边窃取”级联失效，优先承诺机会成本高的边。

Result: 在合成Glossa Ordinaria网格布局、23个ALTO页面几何（含镜像翻转变体）和140页OmniDocBench多栏英语子集上评估：Glossa环绕布局上平均恢复95%真值后继边（XY-cut 50%）；OmniDocBench多栏子集上宏边准确率达88%（XY-cut 75%，LayoutReader 25%）；LayoutReader基线因词级与行级粒度不匹配迁移差；镜像不变性测试：本方法变化<1个百分点，XY-cut变化2点，LayoutReader-T变化高达8点。

Conclusion: 该无训练图方法结合轻量语言模型信号和最大遗憾推理，有效解决复杂历史手稿的非线性阅读顺序推理，在多项基准上显著超越现有方法，且具备良好的几何变换不变性，为历史文献数字化提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Reading order inference remains a critical bottleneck in the digitization of complex historical manuscripts, where pages contain multiple spatially interleaved reading streams, the canonical example being the Glossa Ordinaria layout, in which a central text is surrounded by commentaries that wrap around it in non-rectangular, non-convex regions. We present a training-free, graph-based framework: each OCR text line becomes a node in a directed candidate-transition graph, edges are scored by a weighted additive ensemble of two lightweight language-model signals (causal language model conditional likelihood and BERT next-sentence prediction, NSP; a third sentence-embedding signal was evaluated but did not improve reading order), and the global reading order is recovered as a degree-constrained directed path cover. To avoid the cascading "edge-theft" failures of greedy edge selection, we propose a max-regret inference rule that prioritizes commitments with high opportunity cost. We evaluate on synthetic Glossa Ordinaria grid layouts, on 23 ALTO page geometries (10 historical source pages plus mirrored and flipped variants), and on a 140-page multi-column English subset of OmniDocBench, comparing our method against the canonical recursive XY-cut (PaddleOCR PP-StructureV3) and two LayoutReader variants (layout-only and text+layout) on identical inputs. On wrap-around Glossa layouts our method recovers 95% of ground-truth successor edges on average vs. XY-cut's 50%; on the OmniDocBench multi-column subset it reaches 88% macro edge accuracy versus XY-cut's 75% and LayoutReader's 25%. The LayoutReader baselines transfer poorly due to a word-level vs. line-level granularity mismatch. We additionally verify mirror-invariance under horizontal and vertical page reflections: Our method changes by less than 1 percentage point, classical XY-cut by 2 points, and LayoutReader-T by up to 8 points.

</details>


### [52] [Evidence-Supported Credit Risk Report Generation Using News-Centric Financial Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01023)
*Rocio Jimenez-Villen,Ziwei Xu,Ying Chen,Oscar Araque,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FinKG-News 从新闻中自动构建以公司为中心、环境感知的知识图谱，将事件作为锚点链接公司，并基于此开发上下文学习架构生成信用风险报告。实验表明该方法在三个核心金融维度上质量提升 19%-34% 且减少幻觉，但自动幻觉检测仍不可靠，专家判断不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场随真实世界新闻事件演变，但这些驱动因素在文本中往往隐性存在。需要通过事实性、以公司为中心、环境感知的知识图谱显式建模事件-市场关系，以更好地解释市场动态。

Method: 提出 FinKG-News 框架：自动抽取新闻事件作为锚点并链接到公司，构建事件-新闻-公司一体化的知识图谱；在此基础上开发上下文学习架构，利用图谱作为接地证据，在信用、流动性、市场三个核心金融维度生成信用风险报告。

Result: 自动与人工评估均显示自动幻觉检测和质量评估仍不可靠，专家判断不可替代。该方法在所有基线之上稳定获胜，报告质量提升 19%-34% 且显著降低幻觉率。

Conclusion: 基于事件驱动的知识图谱能有效支撑信用风险报告生成，显著提升质量并抑制幻觉；但当前自动化幻觉检测手段不足，仍需引入专家评审。代码与资源已开源。

Abstract: Financial markets evolve in response to real-world events reported in news, yet these drivers often remain implicit in text. To better explain market dynamics, event-market relations must be explicitly modeled through factual, company-centric, and environment-aware knowledge graphs. We present FinKG-News, a framework that automatically constructs such graphs by extracting news events as anchors linked to companies. Using FinKG-News as grounded evidence that integrates events, news, and company data, we develop an in-context learning architecture for credit risk report generation across three core financial dimensions. Automatic and human evaluations show that automated hallucination detection and quality assessment remain unreliable, making expert judgment indispensable. Our approach consistently outperforms baselines, improving quality by 19%-34% while reducing hallucinations. The source code and project resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/ichise-laboratory/FINKG-news.

</details>


### [53] [Behavior-Adaptive Conversational Agents: Toward a Fluid Personality Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01034)
*Hasibur Rahman,Smit Desai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出流体人格框架，使基于大语言模型的对话代理能根据任务情境、用户目标特征和情境紧迫性，动态调整隐喻人设（如教练、导师、图书管理员、工具）和人格表达强度（低、中、高），以解决固定人设导致的错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话代理多固定人设与风格，难以适应医疗信息搜寻、健身指导、反思学习等场景中动态变化的紧迫性、正式度等需求。研究表明适度人格表达优于极端，情境适配隐喻优于静态助手，因此需要联合自适应框架。

Method: 提出流体人格框架，包含两大核心自适应维度：1) 隐喻人设自适应（教练、导师、图书管理员、工具等）；2) 人格表达强度自适应（低、中、高）。依据任务情境、用户目标特质、情境紧迫性联合调整，并勾勒框架核心设计维度。

Result: 构建了流体人格框架的概念模型与核心设计维度，为后续实证研究与系统实现奠定基础。

Conclusion: 流体人格框架通过动态联合调整隐喻人设与人格表达强度，有望显著提升对话代理在多变情境下的信任度、愉悦感、采纳意愿、用户体验与接受度。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based conversational agents (CAs) are now ubiquitous, creating new opportunities for AI-mediated behavior change. Their capacity to project nuanced personalities and adopt diverse metaphorical roles raises a design question: how should an agent's persona and personality be calibrated to the moment? Recent evidence suggests that (i) moderate personality expression outperforms low or high extremes on trust, enjoyment, and intention to adopt in goal-oriented tasks, and (ii) context-appropriate metaphors outperform static one-note assistants on user experience and uptake. Yet most CAs still fix both persona and style, risking misalignment when dynamics, urgency, and formality vary, for example in medical information seeking, fitness coaching, and reflective learning. We propose a Fluid Personality Framework that jointly adapts (1) the agent's metaphorical persona, such as coach, tutor, librarian, or tool, and (2) its personality expression intensity, low, medium, or high, as a function of task context, user goals and traits, and situational urgency. We sketch the framework and its core design dimensions.

</details>


### [54] [Conversable Complexity: Agentic LLM Collectives as Interpretable Substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01047)
*Elias Najarro,Ane Espeseth,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证大语言模型（LLM）代理群体可作为人工生命（ALife）研究的计算基质，强调其通过自然语言交互实现可解释的涌现行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一LLM是静态制品，缺乏生命相关的涌现特性；复杂性与可解释性通常难以兼得。通过构建具备持久记忆、工具使用、共享技能和主动行动能力的LLM代理群体，可在保持可解释性的同时实现复杂涌现动力学。

Method: 理论论证与综述相结合：提出代理群体作为ALife计算基质的概念框架，扩展可解释性定义至代理群体层面，并系统调查现有代理LLM群体实例（从受控实验到野外部署）。

Result: 阐述了基于自然语言交互的代理群体如何实现可直接审视的集体行为，梳理了相关前沿工作，证实该范式已在多种场景落地。

Conclusion: 具备代理能力的LLM群体通过自然语言通信，为研究复杂涌现系统提供了兼具丰富性与可解释性的新型计算基质，有望推动人工生命研究向更透明、可交互方向发展。

Abstract: Complexity and interpretability rarely coincide: systems rich enough for complex behaviours to emerge are usually too opaque to question, while transparent ones are too simple for anything complex to emerge. A single large language model (LLM) is a static artefact, hardly exhibiting any of the emergent properties we associate with life. This changes through interaction: populations of LLMs display emergent dynamics absent from isolated models. Furthermore, LLMs can be endowed with persistent memory, tools and shared skills, and the capacity to initiate actions unprompted, i.e., turning LLMs agentic. In this paper, we argue that such collectives of agents can serve as a computational substrate for Artificial Life (ALife) research. Critically, since the agents communicate in natural language, their collective behaviour can be directly interrogated by examining textual traces and asking the agents themselves. We outline the notion of interpretability in language-model research and extend it for collectives of agents. Lastly, we survey recent examples of agentic LLM collectives that already instantiate the idea of agentic substrates, from controlled experiments to deployments in the wild.

</details>


### [55] [Message Passing Enables Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01077)
*Xuecheng Liu,Daman Arora,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出消息传递语言模型（MPLMs），一种让大语言模型（LLM）推理线程通过轻量级发送/接收原语直接通信的框架，解决了分叉-汇合（FJ）范式中线程无法点对点通信、限制可扩展性的问题。MPLMs 通过减少冗余上下文共享降低通信成本，并引入抢占机制让线程根据同伴的部分信息提前终止，从而在数独、3-SAT 和长上下文问答任务上展现出更高的推理效率和竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时扩展虽提升了 LLM 推理能力，但生成长思维链（CoT）成为计算瓶颈。并行扩展技术虽使用分叉-汇合（FJ）原语将工作分配到多线程，但线程通常是瞬态的且缺乏点对点通信，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 引入消息传递语言模型（MPLMs）框架，线程通过轻量级 send/receive 原语直接通信。两大核心机制：1) 避免冗余上下文共享以降低通信成本；2) 抢占机制，允许线程基于同伴的部分信息提前终止。

Result: 在三类任务上验证：1) 数独任务中，MPLMs 所需上下文渐近小于串行 CoT 和并行 FJ，微调模型可解决 25×25 难题，超越标准 CoT、FJ 及无工具的前沿推理模型；2) 3-SAT 任务中，抢占机制使无望分支提前终止，显著提升效率；3) 长上下文问答中，适当提示的大型预训练模型遵循 MPLM 协议，达到与流行 FJ 方法竞争的性能。

Conclusion: MPLMs 通过线程间直接通信实现高效推理扩展，降低通信开销并引入抢占机制，在多种推理任务中展现出优于传统串行 CoT 和并行 FJ 方法的潜力，为 LLM 推理时计算提供了新的可扩展范式。

Abstract: While inference-time scaling has improved the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs), the need to generate long chains-of-thought (CoTs) is a computational bottleneck. Thus, in contrast to sequential scaling methods like CoT, recent parallel scaling techniques instead use fork and join (FJ) primitives to divide work across multiple LLM threads. However, in the fork-join paradigm, threads are typically transient and do not communicate pointwise with one another which limits scalability. To tackle this, we introduce Message Passing Language Models (MPLMs), a framework for LLM reasoning in which threads communicate directly via lightweight send and receive primitives. MPLMs enable efficient scaling through two key mechanisms: (1) reduced communication costs, achieved by avoiding redundant context sharing, and (2) preemption, which allows threads to terminate early based on partial information from their peers.
  We demonstrate the promise of MPLMs on 3 classes of tasks. First, on Sudoku puzzles, we show that MPLMs require an asymptotically smaller context than both serial CoT and parallel FJ. We then fine-tune a single model to solve 25 x 25 puzzles that remain challenging for standard CoT and FJ approaches, as well as frontier reasoning models without tools. Second, on 3-SAT puzzles, the capability of preemption allows termination of unpromising branches, which results in improved efficiency. Finally, we show that appropriately prompted large pre-trained models follow the MPLM protocol, achieving competitive results on long-context question answering relative to popular fork-join approaches.

</details>


### [56] [Clinician-Level Agreement Without Clinical Caution: LLM Evaluator Limits in Medical AI Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01103)
*William Philipp,Finn Fassbender,Thorsten Langer,Martje Pauly,Rebecca Herzog,Alexander Baumann,Markus Hobert,Theresa Paulus,Ip Chi Wang,Lukas Goede,Johanna Reimer,Sebastian Löns,Ronald Böck,Sebastian Fudickar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个德语开放式临床基准MedQADE，发现自动评估器虽在统计上与医生一致，但缺乏临床元认知（如拒答校准）且存在血缘偏好，统计对齐不等于临床谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式评估比选择题更具临床效度，但人工评分成本高，推动LLM-as-Judge自动化评估；然而自动评估器是否能复现医生的临床校准与谨慎尚未被测试，且德语作为主要临床语言缺乏原生评估基础设施。

Method: 构建MedQADE基准，包含3800个条目，由10名执业医生和9个LLM评估器标注；对比顶级模型Gemini 3 Flash与医生的标注一致性（Kappa系数），分析拒答行为随题目难度的变化，量化模型家族间的系统性偏好偏差。

Result: Gemini 3 Flash与医生一致性达Kappa=0.694，接近医生上限0.709，但置信区间宽；医生会随题目难度调整拒答率，而前沿模型对所有条目给出确定分数；模型倾向于给同架构模型更高分，该偏好与语言无关。

Conclusion: 统计对齐不能保证临床谨慎（如元认知缺失），评估器独立性需显式验证；开放式临床基准建设需关注评估器的临床校准能力而非仅追求统计指标。

Abstract: Open-response evaluation provides stronger clinical validity than multiple-choice benchmarks but creates a scoring bottleneck that motivates automated LLM-asa-Judge approaches. Whether such evaluators replicate clinical calibration and caution, however, remains untested. We introduce MedQADE, the first standardised open-response clinical benchmark for German, a major clinical language lacking native evaluation infrastructure, comprising 3,800 items annotated by ten practising physicians and nine Large Language Model (LLM) evaluators. The top-performing evaluator model, Gemini 3 Flash, reached alignment consistent with the physician ceiling (\k{appa} = 0.694 vs. \k{appa} = 0.709), though wide confidence intervals limit interpretation. Despite this statistical alignment, automated evaluators exhibited near-absent clinical metacognition: physicians scaled abstention with item difficulty, while frontier models assigned definitive scores in every case. We additionally quantified systematic lineage-dependent biases, where models preferentially scored architectural siblings, an effect independent of language. These results show that statistical alignment does not ensure clinical caution, and that evaluator independence requires explicit verification.

</details>


### [57] [Towards Developing a Multimodal Chat Assistant for University Stakeholders: RAG-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01115)
*Md Abu Hanif Shaikh,Abdullah Al Shafi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的多模态大学聊天机器人，结合大语言模型与语义检索，支持文本和图像查询，采用量化推理部署于受限硬件，通过FastAPI后端和Next.js前端实现实时交互。实验显示该系统在文本和图像查询上均获得高满意度，且将幻觉率从31.7%降至6.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大学利益相关者（尤其是发展中国家）难以获取及时可靠的信息，现有基于规则的聊天机器人无法处理复杂领域查询，且难以适应不断变化的机构政策，因此需要一种能够利用机构中心资源（如大学手册）提供上下文相关回答的智能支持系统。

Method: 构建多模态RAG系统：使用大语言模型配合语义检索从大学手册等资源生成回答；引入视觉语言模型支持图像查询；采用量化推理加速部署；后端用FastAPI构建可扩展API，前端用Next.js开发响应式界面。

Result: 多模态评估显示系统在文本和图像查询上均保持高满意度，尽管视觉输入响应时间增加；定量评估表明幻觉率从31.7%降低至6.6%，验证了检索接地的有效性。

Conclusion: 该多模态RAG聊天机器人有效解决了大学信息获取难题，通过检索增强显著降低幻觉，量化推理使其适合资源受限环境，为发展中国家高校提供了可落地的智能问答解决方案。

Abstract: University stakeholders often face difficulties in accessing timely and reliable information, especially in developing countries, where there are very few intelligent support systems. Existing rule-based chatbots are unable to handle complex, domain-specific queries and are not well-equipped to adapt to evolving institutional policies. As a fill-in-the-gap solution, we present the multimodal university chatbot with retrieval-augmented generation. The system combines the large language model with semantic retrieval to produce context-based responses from institution-centric resources, such as the university handbook. The system accepts text and image queries through the vision-language model and applies quantized inference for rapid deployment on constrained hardware. A scalable backend built with FastAPI, adjoined with a responsive frontend developed with Next.js, ensures real-time usability. Our multimodal evaluation demonstrates that the system maintains strong satisfaction scores across both text and image queries, despite increased response time for visual inputs. Furthermore, quantitative evaluation shows that hallucination is reduced from 31.7% to 6.6% in our proposed RAG-based system, confirming the effectiveness of retrieval grounding.

</details>


### [58] [$\text{Log}_\text{b}$Quant: Quantizing Language Models in Logarithmic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01127)
*Jeremias Bohn,Tizian Dippold,Mahdi Koubaa,Elias R. Wahl,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Log_bQuant对数量化方法，在4-bit精度下优于非对称线性量化，适合消费级GPU部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有均匀量化码本因低频高幅值权重导致表示次优，需要更适应参数分布的量化方法。

Method: 提出Log_bQuant，一种可调底数的对数量化方法，适应常见参数分布。

Result: 在4-bit精度下多个基准测试中优于张量级非对称线性量化，实现中等加速和高内存节省。

Conclusion: Log_bQuant适用于消费级GPU上的私有部署，提供高效量化方案。

Abstract: Quantization has become an invaluable tool to reduce memory requirements and inference speed of modern language models, in particular to make them available for consumer setups and edge devices. While previous work has primarily focused on uniform quantization codebooks, such approaches are prone to suboptimal representations due to low-frequency high-magnitude weights. We introduce Log$_\text{b}$Quant, a novel logarithmic quantization approach with adjustable bases, to adapt to common parameter distributions. We show that our method exhibits superior performance at 4-bit precision on several performance benchmarks compared to asymmetric linear quantization at tensor-wise granularity, while achieving moderate speedup and high memory savings, making it suitable for private use on consumer-grade GPUs.

</details>


### [59] [AGC-Bench: Measuring Artificial General Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01152)
*Roger Beaty,Vijeta Deshpande,Clin K. Y. Lai,Anna Attuch,Namrata Shivagunde,Swastik Roy,Rajkumar Pujari,Paul V. DiStefano,Sherin Muckatira,Claire E. Stevenson,Mikhail Gronas,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出AGC-Bench，一个统一的AI创造力基准，通过系统文献综述和标准化工具构建，包含78个数据集，并开发了校准后的评判模型AGC-Judge。实验发现LLM存在单一创造力因子'c'，提示“发挥创造力”比推理更能提升表现，顶尖人类仍领先顶尖LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创造力研究长期争论创造力是否领域特异及与一般智力的关系，这些问题现延伸至LLM，但缺乏统一的AI创造力基准，亟需建立标准化评估框架。

Method: 基于系统文献综述（3101篇论文，497个基准）构建AGC-Bench，利用代理工具将异构代码库转为HELM标准基准，首发覆盖78个数据集跨六大领域。采用评判反应理论校准LLM评判偏差，在三个前沿LLM的偏差修正评分上微调Qwen3-30B得到AGC-Judge。对83个LLM进行因子分析，对比“发挥创造力”提示与推理启用的效果，并进行人机对比实验。

Result: 前沿模型领跑排行榜，开源模型紧随其后；LLM在不同创造领域表现差异显著。因子分析提取单一创造力因子‘c’，解释81.5%方差，与一般知识/推理相关但可分离。“发挥创造力”提示显著优于推理增强，证明基准测量的是创造力而非一般能力。人机对比中顶尖人类仍超越顶尖LLM。

Conclusion: AGC-Bench为大规模量化AI创造力提供了开放基础设施，揭示了LLM创造力的统一因子结构，验证了基准的效度，并确立了当前人机创造力差距，为后续研究奠定基准。

Abstract: Creativity research has debated whether creativity is domain-specific (e.g., visual, writing, science), and if it is psychometrically separable from general intelligence. Both questions now apply to LLMs, but a unified benchmark of AI creativity remains elusive. We introduce AGC-Bench, an artificial general creativity benchmark built from a systematic review of the AI creativity literature (3,101 papers screened, 497 benchmarks identified), paired with an agentic harness that converts idiosyncratic codebases into HELM-standardized benchmarks. The first release covers 78 datasets spanning brainstorming, problem solving, STEM, narrative, figurative language, and humor. To address bias in LLM-as-judge, we apply Judge Response Theory -- a psychometric calibration of judge leniency/severity; we then fine-tune Qwen3-30B on the bias-corrected ratings of three frontier LLMs to produce AGC-Judge, an open-weight model that robustly scores new creativity benchmarks it was not trained on. Results reveal frontier models at the top of the AGC-Bench leaderboard, with open models close behind. LLMs show different creative strengths, ranking higher on some domains (e.g., writing) than others (e.g., scientific ideation). Extensive experiments yield three main findings. First, applying factor analysis across 83 LLMs, we recover a single creativity factor 'c', analogous to the 'g' factor of general intelligence, that explains 81.5% of variance, related to but separable from general knowledge/reasoning. Second, we show that prompting models to "be creative" boosts their performance far more than enabling reasoning, evidence that the benchmark tracks creativity over general ability. Third, on a human-matched subset, we find the top human still leads the top LLM on creativity. We release AGC-Bench with a public leaderboard, AGC-Judge, and human data as open infrastructure for measuring AI creativity at scale.

</details>


### [60] [Adversarial Pragmatics for AI Safety Evaluation: A Benchmark for Instruction Conflict, Embedded Commands, and Policy Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01153)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出“对抗性语用学”基准与标注协议，旨在系统评估语言模型在指令冲突、嵌入式指令、引用、作用域歧义、指示语、间接言语行为及多轮代理对话等语用歧义场景下的行为表现，并提供专家评估协议与度量指标，将语言学判断方法论转化为安全评估、LLM裁判、黄金集构建、提示词注入测试及安全文档的实用验证工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准常将复杂的语用区分压缩为通过/失败标签，掩盖了失败源于能力上限、策略歧义、指令冲突、脚手架失效还是评估者判断不稳定，缺乏细粒度、语言学可控的诊断框架。

Method: 构建语言学可控分类体系、18项种子基准（含验证器强制元数据）、54行本地种子试点、专家评估协议（区分任务成功、策略合规、安全风险、拒绝结果、评估者置信度），并定义裁判有效性、诊断歧义度、分类漂移等度量指标。

Result: 形成一套将语言学判断方法论转化为实用工具的框架，可用于验证安全评测、LLM裁判、黄金集构建、提示词注入测试及安全文档编制。

Conclusion: 对抗性语用学为模型在歧义自然语言行为下的安全评估提供了实证与方法论双重贡献，填补了现有基准在细粒度语用诊断上的空白。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for language models increasingly depend on judgments about ambiguous natural-language behaviour: whether a model has followed an instruction, refused appropriately, complied with a policy, resisted an embedded command, or misreported progress in an agentic task. Existing benchmarks often compress these distinctions into pass/fail labels, obscuring whether failures arise from capability limits, policy ambiguity, instruction conflict, scaffold failure, or unstable evaluator judgments.
  This paper introduces adversarial pragmatics as a benchmark and annotation protocol for evaluating model behaviour under instruction conflict, embedded commands, quotation, scope ambiguity, deixis, indirect speech acts, and multi-turn agent transcripts. The contribution is empirical and methodological: a linguistically controlled taxonomy, an 18-item seed benchmark with validator-enforced metadata, a 54-row local seed pilot, an expert-evaluation protocol distinguishing task success, policy compliance, safety risk, refusal outcome, and evaluator confidence, and metrics for judge validity, diagnostic ambiguity, and taxonomy drift. The framework turns linguistic judgment methodology into a practical tool for validating safety evals, LLM judges, gold-set construction, prompt-injection tests, and safety documentation.

</details>


### [61] [Distill to Detect: Exposing Stealth Biases in LLMs through Cartridge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01208)
*Shayan Talaei,Abhinav Chinta,Devvrit Khatri,Amin Karbasi,Azalia Mirhoseini,Amin Saberi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Distill to Detect (D2D)方法，通过将疑似模型与基础模型之间的分布偏移蒸馏到KV缓存前缀适配器中，集中主要分歧并放大偏差信号至生成文本，从而可靠检测隐蔽偏好偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在高风险角色中可能偏向特定实体、品牌或观点，这种隐蔽偏好偏差仅在相关主题显现，其余输入表现与基础模型一致，且偏差信号仅存在于软logit分布中，文本检查不可见。防御者面临根本不对称：不知偏差主题时，任何检测方法都无法可靠发现隐蔽偏好偏差。

Method: 提出Distill to Detect (D2D)：将疑似模型与基础模型的logit分布偁移蒸馏到一个‘cartridge’（KV缓存前缀适配器）中，利用前缀调优适配器的容量瓶颈集中主要分歧，放大偏差信号使其在生成文本中显现。同时提供理论框架，从Fisher加权投影logit分布偏移的角度解释D2D有效性。

Result: D2D成功放大隐蔽模型的隐藏偏差，使其能跨多种偏差类型被可靠检测。实证观察支持理论框架。D2D将前缀调优适配器的容量瓶颈转化为检测工具，为审计部署语言模型的隐蔽行为提供实用构建块。

Conclusion: D2D通过分布偏移蒸馏和前缀适配器放大偏差信号，有效解决隐蔽偏好偏差检测的根本不对称问题，为语言模型审计提供实用方法。

Abstract: Language models deployed in high-stakes roles can potentially favor certain entities, brands, or viewpoints, steering user decisions at scale. Such preferential biases can be introduced by any actor in the model's supply chain and are most dangerous when the model reveals its preference only on the relevant topic while behaving identically to its unmodified base on all other inputs. Recent work has shown that these biases can transfer through context distillation on semantically unrelated data, with the signal residing entirely in the soft logit distribution and remaining invisible to text-based inspection. However, the defender faces a fundamental asymmetry: without knowing the bias topic, no detection method can reliably surface a stealth preferential bias, regardless of whether it examines generated text, internal representations, or model weights. Here we introduce Distill to Detect (D2D), a method that surfaces hidden biases by distilling the distributional shift between a suspected model and its base into a cartridge (a KV-cache prefix adapter), concentrating the dominant divergence and amplifying the bias signal into generated text. We show that D2D successfully amplifies the hidden biases of stealth models to the extent that they can be reliably detected across multiple bias types. We also propose a theoretical framework that explains the efficacy of D2D through the lens of Fisher-weighted projection of the logit distribution shift, supported by empirical observations. By turning the capacity bottleneck of prefix-tuning adapters into a detection tool, D2D provides a practical building block for auditing hidden behaviors in deployed language models.

</details>


### [62] [The State-Prediction Separation Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01218)
*Giovanni Monea,Nathan Godey,Kianté Brantley,Yoav Artzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出状态-预测分离假设，设计双流Transformer架构将下一token预测与未来状态存储解耦，实验证明该方法在多个规模上持续提升数据和计算效率，下游任务平均超越标准Transformer 2-3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer使用同一前向计算流同时承担下一token预测和为未来预测存储有用状态的双重角色，作者假设将这两个功能解耦能获得更好的语言建模性能。

Method: 设计一种Transfomer变体，采用两条独立的计算流分别处理状态存储和token预测，在多个规模上进行预训练实验并进行大量实证分析。

Result: 状态-预测分离持续提供更好的数据和计算效率，降低验证损失，在下游任务上平均比标准Transformer高出2-3个百分点；梯度分析显示两种设计存在本质差异。

Conclusion: 将状态存储与预测功能解耦的双流架构从根本上改变了梯度流动机制，显著提升了语言模型的预训练效率和下游任务表现，验证了状态-预测分离假设的有效性。

Abstract: Transformers use the same forward computation stream to both predict the next token and store useful state for future token predictions. We formulate the \emph{state-prediction separation hypothesis}: disentangling the two roles yields better language modeling performance. We design a Transformer variant that uses two computation streams to separate the two functions, and conduct pretraining experiments across various scales. Our experiments show that state-prediction separation consistently offers better data and compute efficiencies, improving validation loss and outperforming standard Transformers by 2--3 percentage points on average on downstream tasks. We also conduct extensive empirical analysis that rules out potential confounders and demonstrates the fundamental difference in the gradients our design entails.

</details>


### [63] [Measuring the Gap Between Human and LLM Research Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01233)
*Ziyu Chen,Yilun Zhao,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建大规模评估框架，通过逆向工程高质量人类论文的灵感来源并引入双轴研究品味分类法，量化LLM生成想法与人类研究者想法的分布差距，发现LLM想法过度集中于“桥接式机会”和“综合方法”，而人类想法分布更广泛，表明LLM虽能生成合理想法但范围更窄且系统性偏离人类研究品味。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多针对单个想法的新颖性、可行性或专家偏好，缺乏对LLM生成想法与人类研究者整体差距的系统表征。

Method: 构建大规模评估框架：从高质量人类论文出发，逆向工程出可能启发其核心想法的少量相关先行工作；提示LLM根据论文标题和摘要生成新想法；引入双轴研究品味分类法（机会模式和研究范式）为每个想法画像，量化人类与LLM想法的分歧。

Result: 不同LLM生成的想法集呈现一致的分布差距：LLM想法过度集中在桥接式机会和综合方法上，而人类参考论文分布更广泛地覆盖了问题框架和贡献构建的多种方式。

Conclusion: 强LLM能产生一系列合理想法，但其范围仍比人类研究品味更窄，且存在系统性偏移。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used to brainstorm research ideas, but existing evaluations mostly judge individual ideas by novelty, feasibility, or expert preference. We instead ask: how far are current LLM-generated ideas from human researchers? To characterize this gap, we build a large-scale evaluation framework for ideation from high-quality human research papers. For each paper, we reverse-engineer a small set of closely related prior works that likely inspired its core idea. LLMs are then prompted to generate a new idea from the set of paper titles and summaries. We introduce a two-axis research-taste taxonomy to profile each idea by its opportunity pattern and research paradigm, and use it to quantify the divergence between human and LLM ideas. Across idea sets generated by different LLMs, we observe a consistent distributional gap: LLM ideas are disproportionately concentrated around bridge-like opportunities and synthesis methods, whereas the human paper reference distribution spreads more broadly across ways of framing gaps and constructing contributions. This result suggests that strong LLMs can produce a range of reasonable ideas, but that range remains narrower than, and systematically shifted relative to, human research taste.

</details>

### [64] [Representation as a Bottleneck for Mechanistic Interpretability: The Manifestation Unit Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00089)
*Hussein Chouman,Wataru Sasaki,Tomokazu Matsui,Hirohiko Suwa,Keiichi Yasumoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Manifestation Units 这一类型化元组协议 (E, S, R, D, G) 并扩展注意力头原语 (T)，用于将机制可解释性分析的组件级统计结构化、自动化填充并支持混合检索，在生成式视觉、判别式视觉与语言模型上验证了其检索优势、因果充分必要性及核心字段 S+R 的不可约性，定位为机制可解释性的 schema 基础设施而非前沿规模验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机制可解释性分析（选择性表格、电路图、特征列表）分散于各研究笔记本中，不可组合、不可用自然语言查询、难以直接用于下游审计或干预，缺乏一个可独立评估的中间表示层来连接分析与下游应用。

Method: 设计 Manifestation Units 协议：五元组 (E, S, R, D, G) 加上面向 Transformer 的注意力头原语 (T)，将每个组件的统计量自动填充到结构化字段中，并支持混合检索；在 β-VAE、CNN 与 GPT-2 上实例化该协议，进行检索基准、因果充分必要性测试、IOI 电路成员集合恢复及字段消融实验。

Result: 1) 类型化结构在检索任务上显著优于非结构化基线；2) 经 Schema 检索的 CNN 滤波器在预算匹配控制下满足因果充分性与必要性；3) 协议无需修改即可吸收注意力头原语，并在检索预算匹配条件下集合恢复已知 IOI 电路成员；4) 消融揭示核心不可约字段为 S+R，其余字段要么冗余要么产生干扰。

Conclusion: Manifestation Units 为机制可解释性提供了一种可复用、可查询、可组合的 Schema 基础设施，验证了结构化表示在跨模态任务中的实用价值，但作者明确将其定位为基础设施层而非大规模前沿验证。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has produced a rich inventory of component-level analyses that characterise what neural-network components encode and how they interact. Their outputs, however, are not easily reusable: selectivity tables, circuit diagrams, and feature lists remain locked in per-study notebooks - non-composable, not queryable in natural language, and not directly actionable for downstream audit or intervention. We study the representation layer that sits between these analyses and downstream use as a bottleneck that can be evaluated independently, and introduce Manifestation Units, a typed tuple protocol (E, S, R, D, G) extended with attention-head primitives (T) for transformer architectures, organising per-component statistics into structured fields populated automatically and queried through hybrid retrieval. Instantiated across generative vision (beta-VAE), discriminative vision (CNN), and language (GPT-2), the protocol supports two findings: typed structure substantially outperforms unstructured baselines on retrieval, and CNN filters retrieved by the schema satisfy causal sufficiency and necessity criteria under matched-budget controls. The schema absorbs attention-head primitives without modification, set-recovers known IOI circuit members under retrieval-budget-matched controls, and reveals an irreducible two-field core (S+R) with remaining fields either redundant or actively interfering. We present this as schema infrastructure for mechanistic interpretability rather than frontier-scale validation.

</details>


### [65] [SNAP-FM: Sparse Nonlinear Accelerated Projection for Physics-Constrained Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00095)
*Alaina Kolli,Theodoros Xenakis,Utkarsh Utkarsh,Pengfei Cai,Rafael Gomez-Bombarelli,Alan Edelman,Christopher Vincent Rackauckas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用稀疏GPU非线性优化加速生成模型中的物理约束采样，通过ExaModels.jl和MadNLP.jl实现块稀疏雅可比和KKT系统的高效求解，在PCFM基准上显著提升非线性约束投影速度并保持约束满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型作为物理仿真的可扩展替代品，但无法保证输出满足守恒定律、边界条件和非线性不变量等物理约束。约束采样可在推理时强制满足这些约束，但现有方法在非线性约束下计算成本高昂，且标准ML框架的稠密张量代数和有限稀疏求解器组合性掩盖了物理约束天然诱导的结构，导致高效批量非线性优化难以实现。

Method: 利用样本级批处理和局部PDE耦合诱导的块稀疏雅可比和KKT系统结构，使用ExaModels.jl暴露该结构，并结合MadNLP.jl和GPU稀疏分解求解稀疏非线性规划，应用于物理约束流匹配（PCFM）。

Result: 在包含线性、非线性、一维和二维约束的PDE基准测试中，该方法加速了非线性约束投影，同时保持了约束满足。

Conclusion: 稀疏GPU非线性优化是科学机器学习中约束生成采样的实用基础。

Abstract: Generative models have emerged as scalable surrogates for physical simulation, yet they offer no guarantee that their outputs respect the conservation laws, boundary conditions, and nonlinear invariants that govern the underlying physics. Constrained sampling closes this gap, enforcing such constraints exactly at inference time without retraining, but at a computational cost: projection, correction, and trajectory-optimization steps are repeated during sampling, with these steps becoming expensive for nonlinear constraints. Standard ML frameworks exacerbate this: their dense tensor algebra and limited sparse solver composability obscure the structure that physical constraints naturally induce, making efficient batched nonlinear optimization difficult to realize in practice. We address this bottleneck by exploiting the structure that sample-wise batching and local PDE couplings induce in the projection subproblems -- namely, block-sparse Jacobian and KKT systems -- exposing this structure using ExaModels.jl and solving the resulting sparse nonlinear programs with MadNLP.jl and GPU sparse factorization. Applied to Physics-Constrained Flow Matching (PCFM), on PDE benchmarks with linear, nonlinear, one-dimensional, and two-dimensional constraints, this approach accelerates nonlinear constraint projection while maintaining constraint satisfaction. These results show that sparse GPU nonlinear optimization is a practical foundation for constrained generative sampling in scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [66] [SemiScope: Disentangling Classifier Tuning and Joint Optimization in Semi-Supervised Security Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00113)
*Rui Shu,Tianpei Xia,Jingzhu He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过实验表明，在安全分类任务中，半监督学习（SSL）流水线的优化收益主要来自于下游分类器的超参数调优，而非复杂的SSL组件交互。作者提出SemiScope分析工具，对比联合调优与仅调优分类器的效果，发现简单的自训练+分类器调优+阈值调优即可达到接近全监督的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全领域标注数据稀缺，SSL常被作为黑盒使用（默认参数、固定分类器、忽略伪标签导致的类别不平衡）。现有工作报告联合优化SSL流水线能带来显著增益，但这些增益难以归因：可能源于SSL-分类器交互，也可能仅来自下游分类器的调优。本文旨在针对二分类表格安全数据，剥离这些效应，量化各组件的真实贡献。

Method: 构建SemiScope作为分析工具而非部署建议：使用贝叶斯优化联合调优SSL设置、置信度过滤、过采样和分类器。关键对照组Tuned-Clf将SSL固定为默认设置，但给予相同的100次分类器调优预算和验证集阈值调优。在10%标注率下，采用配对TOST检验，以±1.0 g-measure为最小关注效应量，对比两种方案。实验涵盖五个安全数据集。

Result: SemiScope在全部五个数据集上均显著优于默认SSL基线，比最强基线提升0.7-12.7个g-measure点。在等预算对照下，Tuned-Clf与完整流水线在4/5数据集上统计等价（Phishing数据集结论不确定）。仅分类器超参数调优即可恢复SemiScope相对于Default ST+RF增益的中位数86%。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是分解分析协议。一个更简单的配方即可：使用自训练，用贝叶斯优化调优分类器，在验证集上调优决策阈值。该配方在四个数据集上于20-30%标注率、在Drebin上于40%标注率时，距离全监督随机森林仅1个g-measure以内，且在每个数据集上所需标注率均不高于默认ST+RF。

Abstract: Background. Labeled data for security classification is scarce. Semi-supervised learning (SSL) propagates labels from a small labeled pool to larger unlabeled pools. Yet security applications often use SSL as a black box: default parameters, a fixed classifier, and no handling of pseudo-label-induced class imbalance. Aims. Recent work reports sizeable gains from optimizing SSL pipelines via joint search, AutoML, or per-component tuning. These gains are hard to attribute: they may reflect useful SSL-classifier interactions, or mostly from simply tuning the downstream classifier. We disentangle these effects for binary tabular security data with classical SSL and tree-based classifiers. Method. We build SemiScope as an analysis instrument, not a deployment recommendation. It uses Bayesian Optimization to jointly tune SSL settings, confidence filtering, oversampling, and the classifier. The key control, Tuned-Clf, fixes SSL to defaults but gets the same 100-trial classifier budget and validation-set threshold tuning as SemiScope. At 10% labels, we compare them with paired TOST using a +/-1.0 g-measure smallest effect of interest. Results. SemiScope beats every default SSL baseline on all five datasets, improving over the strongest by 0.7-12.7 points. Under the equal-budget control, Tuned-Clf is statistically equivalent to the full pipeline on 4 of 5 datasets; Phishing is inconclusive. Classifier HPO alone recovers a median 86% of SemiScope's gain over Default Self-Training (ST) + Random Forest (RF). Conclusions. The reusable contribution is the decomposition protocol. A simpler recipe suffices: use Self-Training, tune the classifier with Bayesian Optimization, and tune the decision threshold on validation data. It reaches within 1 g-measure of Supervised RF at 20-30% labels on four datasets and 40% on Drebin, at the same or lower label rate than Default ST + RF on every dataset.

</details>


### [67] [A Filtered Mixture-of-Generators for Fully Synthetic Survival Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00127)
*Niccolò Maria Rizzi,Eugenio Lomurno,Alberto Archetti,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoGS 通过从异构生成器池中筛选样本而非生成合成数据，解决了生存分析中小样本、隐私限制下的数据稀缺问题，在16个公开数据集上显著提升下游模型性能，且隐私保护无显著损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析面临训练数据稀缺（随访周期长、队列小、隐私法规限制跨机构共享），表格生成模型需大量数据，在小队列上难以生成高质量合成数据供下游模型使用。

Method: FoGS 将合成数据构建重构为样本选择：从四种架构不同的表格生成器采样候选池，利用七个在真实数据上训练的生存模型集成打分（用适当评分规则作为单样本合理性代理），两层流水线优化选择策略（外层优化生成器配额、评分器权重、随机补充、事件时间和删失分层平衡，内层调优下游模型 XGBoost-Cox）。

Result: 在16个公开数据集上（C-index 和 IBS，0-100量表），平均提升 C-index +2.17，IBS +0.67；9/16 数据集双指标均提升，13/16 至少一项提升（单侧 Wilcoxon p=0.039 和 p=0.035）；多数队列匹配或超越真实数据训练，最近邻隐私边界无显著变化。

Conclusion: 基于异构生成器池的样本筛选是隐私受限临床场景下替代真实数据训练的可行方案。

Abstract: Survival analysis models time-to-event data, but in clinical settings training data are costly and scarce: events accrue over years of follow-up, cohorts are small, and privacy regulations restrict sharing across institutions. Tabular generative models promise augmentation and privacy-preserving cohort sharing, yet are themselves data-hungry -- on the small cohorts typical of survival analysis, a single generator rarely characterizes the population well enough for downstream models trained on its output to match real-data performance. FoGS (Filtered Mixture-of-Generators for Survival analysis) reframes synthetic-data construction as sample selection rather than generation. A candidate pool is drawn from four architecturally distinct tabular generators, and each sample is scored by an ensemble of seven survival models trained on real data, using proper scoring rules as a per-sample plausibility proxy. A two-level pipeline optimizes, in its outer loop, a selection policy -- generator quotas, scorer weights, a random complement, and stratified balancing on event time and censoring -- against held-out downstream performance, while an inner loop tunes the downstream model (XGBoost-Cox). On 16 public datasets under train-on-synthetic, test-on-real (C-index and IBS, $0$--$100$ scale), FoGS yields mean improvements of $+2.17$ in C-index and $+0.67$ in IBS, improving both metrics on 9 of 16 datasets and at least one on 13 (one-sided Wilcoxon $p=0.039$ and $p=0.035$). It matches or exceeds real-data training on most cohorts, with no significant change in nearest-neighbour privacy margin relative to unfiltered sampling. Sample filtering over a heterogeneous generator pool is thus a viable substitute for real-data training in privacy-restricted clinical settings.

</details>


### [68] [GRPO, Dr. GRPO, and DAPO Are Three Operations on One Number: The Group-Standard-Deviation Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00152)
*Yong Yi Bay,Kathleen A. Yearick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了三种流行的语言模型推理训练方法（GRPO、Dr. GRPO、DAPO）本质上都是调节同一个“旋钮”——提示词采样答案正确性的标准差（即分歧度），该标准差直接决定了训练更新的大小，从而统一解释了它们的异同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法看似各自提出不同的修正技巧，但缺乏统一的理论解释；作者旨在揭示这些方法背后的共同机制，并阐明标准差在奖励建模中的核心作用。

Method: 理论分析证明，对于二元奖励（对/错），组内标准差恒等式表明分歧度即训练更新幅度；通过在Big-Math数据集上的大规模实验和受控训练运行验证该理论。

Result: 实验证实了理论预测：分歧最大的组（答案对错均分）贡献最大梯度，一致组梯度为零；三种方法分别对应除以标准差、去除除法、丢弃零标准差组三种设置，本质相同。

Conclusion: 看似无害的归一化步骤实则是决定学习发生位置和强度的关键旋钮，为推理模型训练提供了统一视角和实用指导。

Abstract: Three of the most popular methods for training language models to reason look like three different tricks. They are not. All three adjust a single number: standard deviation, reflecting how much a prompt's sampled answers disagree. When such a model is trained, it answers each problem many times, and an automatic checker marks every answer right or wrong. The standard deviation of those marks measures the disagreement: largest when the answers split evenly between right and wrong, and zero when they all agree. Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) divides by this number, GRPO Done Right (Dr. GRPO) drops the division, and Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO) discards the groups where it is zero. Each is presented as its own fix, yet this paper proves they are three settings of one dial. That dial is not cosmetic: for right-or-wrong rewards, the disagreement is exactly the size of the training update, the group-standard-deviation identity. A split group teaches the most, while a unanimous group teaches nothing and falls silent. The same result says which problems deserve the most weight and how many tries each one needs. This paper confirms the intuition on a large real difficulty dataset (Big-Math) and in a controlled training run. What looks like a harmless normalization step is the dial that decides where learning happens and how strongly.

</details>


### [69] [EVOTS: Evolutionary Transformer Search for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00154)
*AbdElRahman ElSaid,Damir Pulatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EVOTS，一种进化神经架构搜索框架，用于发现适应任务的Transformer类模型进行多变量时间序列预测。通过模块化基因组表示和修复机制，在ETT基准数据集上进化出的架构在多变量到多变量预测中达到或超越强基线，证明进化搜索能在实际运行时间约束下有效发现高性能架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多变量时间序列预测方法多依赖固定Transformer架构，忽视任务和预测设置的差异，进化神经架构设计尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出EVOTS框架，采用模块化基因组编码实现注意力、前馈、投影组件的灵活组合，引入修复机制保证结构有效性，无需手工设计规则即可有效探索多样架构空间。

Result: 在ETT家族四个数据集的多种预测设置（单变量到单变量、多变量到单变量、多变量到多变量，预测范围96/192/336/720）上评估。多变量到多变量设置下，进化架构均方误差具有竞争力，部分优于强Transformer基线；附加分析了不同设置下的性能差异及训练墙钟时间。

Conclusion: 进化搜索能在实际运行时间约束下有效发现灵活且高性能的Transformer类架构，适用于多变量时间序列预测。

Abstract: Evolutionary neural architecture design for multivariate time-series forecasting remains underexplored, with most approaches relying on fixed Transformer architectures despite substantial variation across tasks and forecasting settings. This paper introduces an evolutionary neural architecture search framework for discovering task-adaptive Transformer-like models for time-series forecasting (EVOTS). Architectures are encoded using a modular genome representation that enables flexible composition of attention, feed-forward, and projection components, while a repair mechanism enforces structural validity throughout the evolutionary process. This formulation allows effective exploration of a diverse architecture space without relying on hand-crafted design rules. The proposed approach is evaluated on four benchmark datasets from the ETT family (ETTh1, ETTh2, ETTm1, and ETTm2) under multiple forecasting settings, including univariate-to-univariate, multivariate-to-univariate, and multivariate-to-multivariate prediction, with horizons of 96, 192, 336, and 720. In the multivariate-to-multivariate setting, the evolved architectures achieve competitive and, in several cases, improved mean squared error relative to a strong Transformer-based baseline. Additional analyses examine performance differences across forecasting settings and report wall-clock training time to provide a coarse indication of computational cost. Overall, the results demonstrate that evolutionary search can effectively discover flexible and high-performing Transformer-like architectures for multivariate time-series forecasting within practical runtime constraints.

</details>


### [70] [FRAME: Learning the Adaptation Domain with a Mixture of Fractional-Fourier Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00162)
*Tom Saliencro,Maya Lindqvist,Rohan Desai,Priya Nair,Daniel Whitmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分数阶傅里叶专家混合（Fractional-Fourier Mixture of Experts），让每个专家学习一个分数阶傅里叶阶次，在空间域（LoRA）与频谱域（傅里叶适配器）之间连续插值；路由机制将不同 token 分配到不同域的专家，利用不同阶次算子的互不相干性天然去相关，仅增加单标量阶次参数并用 O(d log d) chirp-FFT 实现，在 LLaMA-3.1-8B 与 Qwen2.5-7B 上以极小激活参数量超越 MoE-LoRA、FlyLoRA、FourierMoE、HMoRA 等强基线，且学到的阶次按任务、层可解释地特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PEFT 方法将权重更新固定在某一基底（低秩适配器用空间域，谱方法用傅里叶域），但单一基底无法在所有任务、层、token 上都最优；选择域本身应成为可学习的设计自由度。

Method: 构建分数阶傅里叶专家混合适配器：每个专家携带一个可学习的分数阶傅里叶阶次标量，连续插值空间域（阶次 0，退化为普通 LoRA）与傅里叶域（阶次 1，退化为谱适配器）。采用路由机制将 token 分配到不同阶次专家，不同阶次算子互不相干天然降低专家干扰。阶次参数用独立优化器训练，变换用 O(d log d) chirp-FFT 代理计算，开销可忽略。

Result: 在常识、数学、代码、知识四类基准上，Fractional-Fourier Mixture of Experts 在 LLaMA-3.1-8B 和 Qwen2.5-7B 上均显著优于 MoE-LoRA、FlyLoRA、FourierMoE、HMoRA 等强基线，同时保持极小的激活参数预算。分析表明学到的阶次按任务、层呈现可解释的特化模式。

Conclusion: 分数阶傅里叶专家混合通过将域选择作为可学习的连续自由度，实现了更紧凑、去相关的低秩更新，以极低额外成本在多任务 PEFT 中取得了最优性能，且阶次参数的可解释特化揭示了任务与层对空间/频谱域的不同偏好。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) reparameterizes weight updates in a fixed basis: low-rank adapters operate in the spatial domain, while a recent line of spectral methods operates in a fixed Fourier domain. We argue that the choice of domain is itself a design degree of freedom that should be learned, and that no single basis is optimal across tasks, layers, or tokens. We introduce Fractional-Fourier Mixture of Experts, a mixture-of-experts adapter in which every expert carries a learnable fractional-Fourier order that continuously interpolates between the spatial domain (recovering vanilla LoRA) and the Fourier domain (recovering a spectral adapter). Routing tokens through experts that occupy different points on this spatial-spectral continuum lets the model place each low-rank update in the domain where it is most compact, and -- because fractional-Fourier operators of different orders are mutually incoherent -- makes the experts naturally decorrelated, which reduces interference and improves multi-task composition. The order is a single scalar per expert, trained with a separate optimizer, and the transform is computed with an $\mathcal{O}(d\log d)$ chirp--FFT surrogate, so Fractional-Fourier Mixture of Experts adds negligible cost over standard MoE-LoRA. Across commonsense, mathematical, code, and knowledge benchmarks on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, Fractional-Fourier Mixture of Experts improves over strong MoE-LoRA and spectral baselines -- including FlyLoRA, FourierMoE, and HMoRA -- while keeping the active-parameter budget small, and analysis shows that the learned orders specialize by task and layer in interpretable ways.

</details>


### [71] [Verifiable Rewards for Calibrated Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00164)
*Sadanand Singh,Allam Reddy,Manan Chopra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法，用于训练校准良好的概率预测器，通过使用无标签的状态条件经验胜率作为奖励，并避免梯度污染推理链，使7B模型达到博彩市场的校准水平，优于零样本前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在使用可验证奖励（如Brier分数）训练概率预测器时，会因单一结果的噪声和策略梯度破坏思维链而导致校准性能下降；现有补救措施主要针对认知不确定性，而论文研究的是本质不确定性预测，即预测本身是输出，标签是随机结果。

Method: 以NFL比赛实时胜率预测为测试平台，引入一种可验证的无标签奖励——基于历史结果估计的状态条件经验胜率，消除标签噪声；通过直接预测或梯度掩码使梯度不作用于推理过程，防止推理被破坏；仅用该奖励训练7B模型，无需人工标签或监督微调。

Result: 训练后的7B模型通过直接预测达到博彩市场的校准水平，校准优于零样本前沿模型；前沿模型和表格估计器达到相同的Brier分数，表明市场剩余的微小优势来自共享输入之外的实时比赛信息；梯度掩码而非丢弃思维链保留了推理能力，而普通思维链训练会破坏推理。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了仅使用可验证的无标签奖励即可训练出校准良好的概率预测器，解决了强化学习中标签噪声和梯度污染推理的问题，为本质不确定性预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards can in principle train calibrated probabilistic forecasters, since a proper scoring rule such as the Brier score is computed from outcomes alone and is minimized in expectation by the true probability. In practice it degrades calibration, and existing remedies address epistemic uncertainty, where a model's confidence accompanies a verifiably correct or incorrect answer. We study aleatoric forecasting, where the forecast itself is the output and the label is one stochastic outcome, taking NFL in-game win probability as a testbed with the betting market as a reference. Rewarding the realized per-play outcome fails, because the single outcome is a noisy target and the policy gradient corrupts the chain of thought. We introduce a verifiable, label-free reward, a state-conditioned empirical win rate estimated from past outcomes, that removes the label noise, and we keep the gradient off the reasoning, by direct prediction or a gradient mask, so it cannot be corrupted. Trained with this reward alone, without human labels or supervised fine-tuning, a 7B model reaches the calibration of the betting market by direct prediction and is better calibrated than a zero-shot frontier model. That frontier model and a tabular estimator reach the same Brier score as this model, identifying the market's small remaining edge as live in-game information beyond their shared inputs. Masking the gradient, rather than dropping the chain of thought, preserves reasoning from which the forecast follows, which ordinary chain-of-thought training corrupts.

</details>


### [72] [Scaling Up Thermodynamic AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00170)
*Andrew G. Moore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于反向传播的可扩展算法，用于在伊辛模型热力学计算硬件上训练深度卷积网络，实现了CIFAR-10 94.9%和CIFAR-100 76.0%的准确率，并建立了推理成本与准确率的理论权衡及最优调度算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于伊辛模型的热力学计算设备在低功耗AI推理和边缘计算方面展现出巨大潜力，但针对此类硬件训练大规模模型的可扩展方法仍然匮乏。

Method: 利用高温吉布斯采样伊辛系统的时均行为可实现前馈神经推理的理论对应关系，开发了一种纯基于反向传播的可扩展算法，用于训练深度卷积网络以在伊辛机器硬件上进行热力学推理。

Result: 在二进制吉布斯采样下，图像分类模型在CIFAR-10上达到94.9%准确率，在CIFAR-100上达到76.0%准确率；建立了推理成本与准确率的数学理论及自相关时间控制方法；给出了渐近结果证明推理成本与性能存在可控权衡，并提供了计算最优推理调度的算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为热力学计算硬件上的大规模模型训练提供了可扩展解决方案，揭示了推理成本与准确率的理论界限，并为未来高温热力学AI模型的硬件开发提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Thermodynamic computing devices based on the Ising model show great promise for low-power AI inference and edge computing, but scalable methods for training large models for such hardware remain limited. Prior theory shows that the time-averaged behavior of high-temperature Gibbs-sampled Ising systems can implement feed-forward neural inference. We turn this theoretical correspondence into a scalable and purely backpropagation-based algorithm for training deep convolutional networks for thermodynamic inference on Ising machine hardware. Our image classification models achieve accuracies of 94.9% on CIFAR-10 and 76.0% on CIFAR-100 under binary Gibbs sampling. We then develop and experimentally validate a mathematical theory relating inference cost to accuracy and controlling autocorrelation times. Subsequently, we calculate asymptotic results showing that inference cost is bounded by a well-controlled tradeoff with performance and exhibit algorithms for computing optimal inference schedules. Finally, we discuss implications for hardware development and the future of high-temperature thermodynamic AI models.

</details>


### [73] [TallyTrain: Communication-Efficient Federated Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00173)
*Radhakrishna Achanta,Will Reed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TallyTrain协议，通过仅传输各节点的argmax类别索引（将类别数压缩至⌈log₂C⌉比特），解决联邦学习中模型大小与类别数两个正交维度的带宽瓶颈，在非IID数据下优于软标签蒸馏，通信量降低达三个数量级，并结合稀疏参数合并进一步优化模型大小维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临两大带宽瓶颈：模型大小限制参数平均合并频率，类别数导致软标签蒸馏在大词表下代价过高。随着系统规模扩大，这两个上限愈发紧缩，亟需通信高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出TallyTrain协议：各节点仅传输argmax硬标签索引，将类别维度压缩至⌈log₂C⌉比特；利用多数投票过滤欠训练节点的确信性错误；进一步结合稀疏参数合并，构建带宽桥接变体，同时优化模型大小维度。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，TallyTrain性能媲美或超越软标签蒸馏，通信量减少达三个数量级；带宽桥接变体在所有测试工作点上帕累托优于FedAvg、FedProx和FedDF基线。

Conclusion: 硬标签共识不仅极大压缩通信，在非IID设置下通过多数投票机制还能抑制软标签平均放大的噪声，结合稀疏参数合并可同时突破模型大小与类别数两大带宽瓶颈，为大规模联邦学习提供高效实用范式。

Abstract: Federated learning is bandwidth-bound on two orthogonal axes: model size, which limits how often parameter-averaging methods can afford to merge, and class count, which makes per-probe soft-label distillation prohibitive at large vocabularies. Both ceilings tighten as modern systems scale. We collapse the class-count axis to $\lceil \log_2 C \rceil$ bits per probe by transmitting only each peer's $\arg\max$ class index, where $C$ is the number of output classes. The resulting protocol, TallyTrain, is not merely compressed: under non-IID training it can be preferable to soft-label distillation, because under-trained peers are confidently wrong and majority voting filters this noise where soft-label averaging amplifies it. Across standard benchmarks, TallyTrain matches or beats soft-label distillation at up to three orders of magnitude less communication. We also relax the model-size axis: we compose the cheap hard-label consensus with sparse parameter merges to obtain a bandwidth-bridge variant, which Pareto-dominates every tested operating point of the standard FedAvg, FedProx and FedDF baselines.

</details>


### [74] [Play Like Champions: Counterfactual Feedback Generation in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00190)
*Andrzej Białecki,Adam Mastalerz,Han Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“Latent Maps of Performance”框架，利用引导变分自编码器在职业选手回放数据上学习潜在表示空间，并设计四种反事实路径生成策略（线性插值、迭代最优传输、密度正则化梯度上升、神经流匹配），将业余玩家的游戏表现轨迹映射向专家获胜配置，从而提供多粒度的可执行反馈，旨在填补即时战略游戏中AI辅助人类提升的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习研究多聚焦于击败人类，而非提供反馈以助人类进步。国际象棋和围棋已将AI融入训练体系，但即时战略（RTS）游戏缺乏将专家知识转化为可操作反馈的原则性框架。受运动科学冠军模型启发，作者希望构建反事实路径生成机制，将玩家表现视为学习表示空间中的算法补救，从而实现从失败到获胜的轨迹导航。

Method: 1. 在23,305场职业比赛回放上训练引导变分自编码器（Guided VAE），学习玩家表现的潜在表示空间。2. 针对业余回放构建的分布外（OOD）数据，设计并验证四种反事实遍历策略：线性插值、迭代最优传输、密度正则化梯度上升、神经流匹配。3. 这些策略生成多步改进轨迹，保持轨迹落在观测到的专家行为分布内，同时将玩家画像推向获胜配置。4. 从轨迹中提取多粒度反馈，以适应不同阶段玩家的改进需求。

Result: 四种遍历策略均能在OOD业余数据上生成合理的反事实改进路径，但各存在权衡：线性插值简单但可能偏离专家分布；迭代最优传输能更好地保持分布约束但计算开销大；密度正则化梯度上升平衡了路径质量与计算效率；神经流匹配提供了更平滑的轨迹但训练复杂度高。实验表明框架能提取可操作的多粒度反馈，验证了将玩家改进建模为潜在空间算法补救的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究确认了不同路径寻找方法之间存在权衡，并指出未来研究应致力于开发更完善的面向人类提升的模型解决方案，进一步缩小AI专家知识与人类可执行反馈之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Recent advances in reinforcement learning have produced superhuman agents across a wide range of competitive games. As a byproduct, researchers have begun studying how these agents play, extracting behavioral representations, analyzing decision structure, and modeling the latent geometry of expert performance. However, this growing body of work has overwhelmingly focused on defeating human players rather than providing feedback, leaving a critical gap in creating model solutions to improve human players. Unlike chess and Go, where AI has become integral to player training, real-time strategy (RTS) games lack principled frameworks for translating expert knowledge into actionable feedback. We introduce Latent Maps of Performance, a framework for counterfactual path generation. We focus on StarCraft~II data to model player improvement as an algorithmic recourse within a learned representation space. As inspiration for our work, we have looked at the championship model used in sports science. We trained a Guided Variational Autoencoder model on 23,305 professional tournament replays, enabling counterfactual traversal between losing and winning gameplay profiles. To fulfill our goal, we have devised and verified four traversal strategies on out-of-distribution (OOD) data randomly sampled from a dataset of amateur replays, namely linear interpolation, iterative optimal transport, density-regularized gradient ascent, and neural flow matching, each designed to generate multi-step improvement trajectories that remain grounded in observed expert behavior while moving a player's profile toward winning configurations. Feedback is extracted at multiple granularities to support players at different stages of improvement. Finally, we conclude that there is a trade-off between the path-finding methods we employ and hope that future research will focus on developing model solutions for human improvement.

</details>


### [75] [TRIE: An Evaluation Framework for Stochastic PDE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00196)
*Bharat Srikishan,Javier E. Santos,Nikhil Muralidhar,Charles D. Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出TRIE评估框架，系统评估随机PDE代理模型，发现生成模型在捕捉不变测度统计和预测不确定性方面表现最佳，潜在生成模型可将推理时间减少约12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学系统存在随机强迫、未解析自由度或不完美观测带来的不确定性，使得可靠的代理预测本质上是分布式的而非点式的。确定性神经代理无法捕捉统计量度和预测不确定性，因此需要一个评估框架来检验模型是否能再现不变测度、提供可信的预测不确定性并实现高效概率生成。

Method: 提出TRIE评估框架，在两个平稳混沌空间扩展SPDEs（随机Kuramoto-Sivashinsky和随机Kolmogorov流）上，跨11个参数值评估标准点式训练神经代理、近似不确定性方法（蒙特卡洛Dropout、异方差高斯似然）及生成模型的表现，指标包括不变测度统计、校准度、CRPS等。

Result: 标准点式训练神经代理能产生合理的短期滚动预测，但无法匹配长时统计结构；近似不确定性方法虽产生随机预测，但在时空不确定性诊断下常表现为校准不良和过度自信；生成模型在所有概率设置中最一致地捕捉不变测度统计并达到最低CRPS；带自动维度发现的潜在生成模型保留了大部分统计保真度，同时将Kolmogorov推理时间减少约12倍。

Conclusion: 生成模型是随机PDE代理建模的最佳选择，能同时实现准确的统计保真度和可信的不确定性量化；潜在生成模型提供了高效的推理加速，TRIE框架为随机PDE预测模型的可复现评估提供了基准。

Abstract: Many scientific systems exhibit uncertainty from stochastic forcing, unresolved degrees of freedom, or imperfect observations, making reliable surrogate forecasting fundamentally distributional rather than pointwise. For such systems, deterministic neural surrogates fail to capture statistical measures and forecast uncertainty. We introduce TRIE, an evaluation framework for stochastic PDE surrogates that asks whether models reproduce invariant measures, provide trustworthy predictive uncertainty, and scale to efficient probabilistic generation. We demonstrate TRIE on two stationary chaotic spatially extended SPDEs, stochastic Kuramoto--Sivashinsky and stochastic Kolmogorov flow, across 11 parameter values. Our evaluation shows that standard pointwise-trained neural surrogates can produce plausible short rollouts while failing to match long-time statistical structure. Approximate uncertainty methods such as Monte Carlo dropout and heteroscedastic Gaussian likelihoods produce stochastic forecasts, but are often miscalibrated and overconfident under temporal and spatial uncertainty diagnostics. Across these criteria, generative models provide the most consistent performance, accurately capturing invariant measure statistics and achieving the lowest CRPS in all reported probabilistic settings. Finally, we show that latent generative models with automatic dimension discovery retain much of this statistical fidelity while reducing Kolmogorov inference time by roughly $12\times$. We release our code and data at https://github.com/scailab/TRIE-SPDE-Bench to support reproducible evaluation of stochastic PDE forecasting models.

</details>


### [76] [StateFlow: Dual-State Recurrent Modeling for Long-Horizon Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00197)
*Haroon Gharwi,Yue Dai,Kai Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出StateFlow框架，扩展VARNN用于长序列预测，采用双状态循环骨干网络捕捉隐状态轨迹和残差记忆轨迹，分块解码器实现直接多步预测，两阶段优化策略，在基准测试中表现优异且模型紧凑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列多变量时间序列预测面临非平稳性、机制转移、误差累积等挑战；现有VARNN仅限单步序列回归，不直接支持多步预测，需扩展至长时域预测。

Method: StateFlow框架，以VARNN为双状态循环骨干网络，捕捉隐状态轨迹（主要时序动态：趋势、季节性、水平变化、周期模式）和残差记忆轨迹（由单步基础预测与观测值误差的非线性循环变换驱动的结构化局部预测偏差）；分块解码器分别汇总两轨迹并映射至未来时域实现直接多步预测；两阶段优化：先用单步基础预测目标训练VARNN编码器优化回溯序列内部表示，再训练特定时域的解码器进行直接多步预测。

Result: 在标准LTSF基准上，StateFlow对比强线性、循环、卷积、Transformer基线取得有竞争力的性能，同时保持线性循环编码和紧凑模型设计。

Conclusion: StateFlow通过双状态循环骨干网络和分块解码器有效解决了长时域预测中的非平稳性和误差累积问题，两阶段优化策略提升了表示学习和预测精度，实现了高性能且模型轻量化。

Abstract: Long-horizon multivariate time series forecasting (LTSF) remains challenging due to non-stationarity, regime shifts, and error accumulation. The Variability-Aware Recursive Neural Network (VARNN) is designed to track such variability by maintaining a residual-memory state driven by one-step prediction errors. However, its original formulation is limited to one-step sequence regression and does not directly support multi-step forecasting. In this work, we extend VARNN to long-horizon forecasting and introduce StateFlow, a recurrent forecasting framework that uses VARNN as a dual-state recurrent backbone to capture two complementary signals from the lookback sequence: a hidden-state trajectory representing primary temporal dynamics, including trend, seasonality, level changes, and recurring patterns, and a residual-memory trajectory representing structured local prediction deviations, driven from a nonlinear recurrent transformation of errors between one-step base predictions and observed values. A chunk-based decoder separately summarizes these trajectories and maps them to the future horizon for direct multi-step forecasting. We further employ a two-stage optimization strategy that first trains the VARNN encoder through a one-step base prediction objective to optimize the internal representations over the lookback sequence, and then trains a horizon-specific decoder for direct multi-step forecasting. Experiments on standard LTSF benchmarks show that StateFlow achieves competitive performance against strong linear, recurrent, convolutional, and Transformer-based baselines while preserving linear recurrent encoding and a compact model design.

</details>


### [77] [Device Passport: Enabling Spatio-Temporal Pretrained Models to Generalize Across Input Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00249)
*Geeling Chau,Ran Liu,Juri Minxha,Wenhui Cui,Erdrin Azemi,Ellen L. Zippi,Behrooz Mahasseni,Christopher M. Sandino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Device Passport 的新通道嵌入技术，用于改善生物信号基础模型在不同设备布局间的迁移性能。通过结合通道的功能活动和元数据，Device Passport 在受控子集迁移实验和真实耳部 EEG 迁移任务中均表现优异，优于现有最强基线，表明通道嵌入设计是大规模预训练生物信号模型复用到新设备时的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新设备布局缺乏大规模数据集，导致建模困难。生物信号基础模型若能有效泛化到新布局，可提供解决方案。现有通道嵌入方法通常仅使用功能信息或元数据，难以应对预训练布局与下游解码布局差异大的情况，因此需要研究更有效的跨布局迁移方法。

Method: 提出 Device Passport，一种新的通道嵌入技术，学习专家模型和混合模型，同时接收每个通道的功能活动和元数据作为输入。这与先前仅使用功能信息或元数据查找学习到的或固定位置嵌入的方法形成对比。

Result: 在受控子集迁移实验和真实耳部 EEG 迁移任务中，Device Passport 总体表现具有竞争力，且在激发本工作的布局迁移场景下优于最强学习基线。

Conclusion: 通道嵌入设计是将大规模预训练生物信号模型复用到新设备时的关键考虑因素。Device Passport 通过融合功能活动和元数据，有效提升了跨布局迁移性能。

Abstract: New device layouts pose a challenging modeling problem due to the lack of large datasets for each specific layout. Biosignal foundation models offer a plausible solution if they are able to generalize to new layouts effectively. To improve cross-layout transfer, we study how different channel embedding techniques behave when pretraining layouts differ substantially from the downstream decoding layout. We propose Device Passport, a new channel embedding technique that learns experts and mixture models that take each channel's functional activity and metadata as input. This contrasts with prior embedding methods, which typically use only functional information or only metadata to look up learned or fixed positional embeddings. Across controlled subset-transfer experiments and realistic transfer to ear-EEG, Device Passport is competitive overall and improves over the strongest learned baseline in the layout-transfer regimes that motivate this work. These results suggest that channel embedding design is a key consideration when reusing large-scale pretrained biosignal models on new devices.

</details>


### [78] [Distributionally Robust Linear Regression With Block Lewis Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00252)
*Naren Sarayu Manoj,Kumar Kshitij Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对群体分布鲁棒（GDR）最小二乘问题的算法，利用分块刘易斯权重和加速近端方法，在中等精度下优于内点法，且在ℓ_∞回归特例下达到最优保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决群体分布鲁棒最小二乘问题，即在给定多个群体的设计矩阵和响应向量时，寻找对最坏群体分布具有鲁棒性的参数向量。现有内点法在中等精度下效率不高，需要更快的算法。

Method: 基于几何构造——分块刘易斯权重，将经验GDR问题转化为精心选择的最小二乘问题，并应用加速近端方法求解。核心是利用分块对角矩阵B的线性系统求解A^⊤ B A。

Result: 算法以Õ(min{rank(A),m}^{1/3} ε^{-2/3})次线性系统求解获得(1+ε)倍最优解，改进了内点法在中等精度下的性能，并在ℓ_∞回归特例下匹配最先进保证。还提供了在平均最小二乘损失和分布鲁棒损失之间平滑插值的算法。

Conclusion: 该工作通过分块刘易斯权重和加速近端方法，为GDR最小二乘问题提供了高效算法，填补了理论空白，且在实际中等精度场景下优于传统内点法，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for the group distributionally robust (GDR) least squares problem. Given $m$ groups, a parameter vector in $\mathbb{R}^d$, and stacked design matrices and responses $\mathbf{A}$ and $\mathbf{b}$, our algorithm obtains a $(1+\varepsilon)$-multiplicative optimal solution using $\widetilde{O}(\min\{\mathsf{rank}(\mathbf{A}),m\}^{1/3}\varepsilon^{-2/3})$ linear-system-solves of matrices of the form $\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{A}$ for block-diagonal $\mathbf{B}$. Our technical methods follow from a recent geometric construction, block Lewis weights, that relates the empirical GDR problem to a carefully chosen least squares problem and an application of accelerated proximal methods. Our algorithm improves over known interior point methods for moderate accuracy regimes and matches the state-of-the-art guarantees for the special case of $\ell_{\infty}$ regression. We also give algorithms that smoothly interpolate between minimizing the average least squares loss and the distributionally robust loss.

</details>


### [79] [Learning dynamical systems from noisy data with Weak-form Kernel Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00257)
*Max Kreider,John Harlim,Daning Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出弱形式核岭回归（WKRR），结合弱形式的噪声滤波机制与核学习，有效预测复杂动力系统，在混沌基准系统（高达64维）和真实流体数据（15000维）上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核岭回归在干净数据上表现良好，但对噪声数据鲁棒性不足；弱形式被观察到能滤波噪声，但缺乏系统性的误差分析与统一框架。

Method: 分析弱形式的滤波机制，进行偏差-方差误差分解，据此将弱形式与核岭回归结合，提出弱形式核岭回归（WKRR）框架。

Result: WKRR实现简单，对干净和噪声数据均有效，在高达64维的混沌基准系统和15000维真实流体数据上优于多种基线方法。

Conclusion: 弱形式核岭回归通过弱形式的内在噪声滤波特性，显著提升了核学习在噪声动力系统预测中的鲁棒性和精度，为高维复杂系统建模提供了通用有效工具。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of complex dynamical systems from noisy measurements remains a significant challenge in scientific computing. Kernel ridge regression learning strategies are often effective when applied to clean data, but have limited success with noisy data. Recent work has observed that a weak formulation can act to filter noisy data, and different learning strategies have achieved increased noise robustness with a weak-form framework. In this manuscript, we give an overview of the filtering mechanism behind the weak formulation and provide a bias-variance error decomposition. Using these insights, we combine a weak formulation with a kernel learning strategy to propose Weak-form Kernel Ridge Regression (WKRR) for learning dynamical systems. The proposed framework is simple to implement, effective for both clean and noisy data, and outperforms several baseline methods. We demonstrate the performance of WKRR on chaotic benchmark systems in up to 64 dimensions, as well as 15,000-dimensional real-world fluid data.

</details>


### [80] [Validating Causal Abstraction Metrics on Simulated Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00267)
*Maxime Méloux,Tiago Pimentel,François Portet,Maxime Peyrard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含十个复杂系统的基准测试，系统评估了30多个候选指标来衡量高层因果解释的有效性，发现只有因果指标结合未映射变量的忠实性测试才能可靠区分有效与无效抽象，并基于此提出了因果抽象误差(CAE)作为通用有效性度量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学的核心目标是产生对复杂系统的有效解释，即高层因果解释能忠实反映低层机制行为，但目前缺乏共识度量标准来衡量高层解释是否真正有效。

Method: 构建涵盖离散/连续状态空间、静态/动态机制的十个复杂系统基准，每个系统配备共识真值因果解释和无效对比条件；在统一因果抽象框架下，系统评估来自观测、函数、信息论、因果四大家族的30多个候选指标。

Result: 只有因果指标结合未映射变量的忠实性测试能可靠区分有效/无效抽象；基于此提出的因果抽象误差(CAE)是一个连续有效性指标，包含显式忠实性测试，通过所有系统的所有区分测试，仅需30次采样干预即可收敛。

Conclusion: CAE可作为发现和验证高层因果解释的通用度量指标，解决了长期以来缺乏共识有效性度量标准的问题。

Abstract: A central goal of science is to produce valid explanations of complex systems: high-level causal accounts that faithfully reflect the behavior of lower-level mechanisms. Yet no consensus exists on how to measure whether a proposed high-level explanation is actually valid. We introduce a benchmark of ten complex systems spanning both discrete and continuous state spaces, as well as static and dynamical regimes, each equipped with consensual ground-truth causal explanations and invalid contrastive conditions. Within a unified causal abstraction framework, we systematically evaluate over thirty candidate metrics drawn from observational, functional, information-theoretic, and causal families. Our results show that only the latter reliably discriminates valid from invalid abstractions, and only when incorporating faithfulness testing over unmapped variables. Building on these findings, we introduce the Causal Abstraction Error (CAE), a continuous validity metric with an explicit faithfulness test, which passes all discrimination tests across every system and can converge with as few as 30 sampled interventions. We offer it as a general-purpose metric for the discovery and validation of high-level explanations.

</details>


### [81] [Entropy-Regularized Probabilistic Gates for Sparse Model Discovery in Scarce-Data Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00275)
*Krishna Harsha Kovelakuntla Huthasana,Alireza Olama,Andreas Lundell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于熵正则化的概率门控机制，用于联邦学习中的稀疏模型训练，通过维持参数空间的不确定性来避免过早收敛到稀疏支持集，在异构数据和部分客户端参与的条件下，优于现有的联邦迭代硬阈值和剪枝后的FedAvg方法，在测试性能和稀疏恢复准确性上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中稀疏模型训练面临数据异质性、客户端参与度不均以及高维小样本（d >> N）导致的泛化困难；现有基于幅度的剪枝无法在参数空间中探索不确定性，容易过早锁定稀疏支持集，导致泛化性能下降。

Method: 引入概率门控分布并施加L0约束，通过熵正则化保持门控分布的不确定性，防止训练早期过度承诺特定稀疏配置；在联邦优化框架下结合数据异质性、客户端参与异质性和稀疏性约束进行实验验证。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准测试中，该方法在统计测试性能和稀疏支持恢复准确性上均持续优于联邦迭代硬阈值（Fed-IHT）和稠密联邦平均训练后剪枝（FedAvg+剪枝）。

Conclusion: 熵正则化的概率门控机制能有效维持稀疏联邦优化中的不确定性，缓解异构环境下的过早收敛问题，为高维小样本联邦学习中的稀疏模型训练提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is a distributed machine learning (ML) paradigm with collaboration among multiple clients without sharing data. FL is challenging under data heterogeneity and partial client participation. Learning sparse models is useful for communication and computational efficiency in FL, but it is especially difficult in the small-sample high-dimensional regime (d >> N) where optimization can yield parameter configurations that fail to generalize to unseen test data. While magnitude-based pruning doesn't account for uncertainty exploration in the parameter space, a formulation with probabilistic gates and an L0 constraint allows sampling from competing sparse configurations during training. In this work, we study entropy regularization of gate distributions as a mechanism to maintain uncertainty in sparse federated optimization by preventing early commitment to sparse support. We examine its impact under data heterogeneity, client participation heterogeneity, and sparsity. Experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show consistent improvements over federated iterative hard thresholding (Fed-IHT) and pruning after dense federated averaging (FedAvg) training, both in statistical performance on test data and in sparsity recovery accuracy.

</details>


### [82] [Testing Frontier Large Language Models' Physics Literacy in Parallel Physical Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00276)
*Dong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可审计的四阶段诊断框架，用于评估大语言模型在陌生物理框架中的推理能力，而非单纯依赖答案准确率。实验在三个平行物理世界（反事实单方程世界 F=mv、历史框架亚里士多德力学、四域反事实衰变世界）上测试 Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro，发现模型整体通过率极低（6/15、6/15、0/15），并揭示了定性与定量推理的不对称性：模型几乎从不预测错误的变化方向，但常因退回标准物理关系而计算错误比率。此外，LLM 评判员的可靠性不跨框架迁移，第四阶段自我复盘极弱，模型在至少三分之二的含错试验中错误报告无早期错误。论文发布了完整提示、响应、裁决和审计记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 物理基准主要以答案准确率评分，无法区分真实推理与熟悉问题模式的记忆，也难以定位模型推理断裂点。需要一种可审计的诊断协议，在陌生物理框架中系统评估模型的归纳、建模、预测和复盘能力。

Method: 设计四阶段诊断：归纳、建模、预测、复盘。采用锁定预注册、阶段间全新会话、双 LLM 裁判、人工审计通道。在三个平行物理世界（F=mv、亚里士多德力学、衰变世界）上对三款前沿模型进行平行测试，计算内容与结构双轴通过率（衰变世界仅内容轴）。

Result: 三模型在三世界的综合通过率分别为 6/15、6/15、0/15。关键模式：衰变世界中模型几乎从不预测错误变化方向（定性正确），但频繁计算错误比率（定量错误，退回标准物理）。方法论发现：LLM 裁判可靠性不跨框架迁移；第四阶段自我复盘在所有框架均弱，模型在 ≥2/3 含错试验中错误报告无早期错误。

Conclusion: 答案准确率不足以衡量 LLM 物理推理能力。所提四阶段诊断揭示了模型在陌生框架中的系统性缺陷：定性/定量不对称、自我监控失效、评判员可靠性受框架限制。该协议及发布的完整审计记录为未来推理基准提供了可复现、可审计的范式。

Abstract: Current large-language-model (LLM) physics benchmarks are usually scored by answer accuracy, which cannot distinguish genuine reasoning from recall of familiar problem patterns and reveals little about where a model's reasoning breaks down. We introduce an auditable four-stage diagnostic that evaluates whether an LLM can reason inside an unfamiliar physics framework through induction, formulation, prediction, and review. The diagnostic combines locked pre-registrations, fresh sessions between stages, dual-LLM judging, and a human-audit pathway, and we apply it to three parallel physics worlds: a single-equation counterfactual world ($F=mv$), a historical framework (Aristotelian mechanics), and a four-domain counterfactual world (Decay World). Across Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5.5, and Gemini 3.1 Pro, the three worlds yield composite PASS rates are 6/15, 6/15, and 0/15 respectively (content $\land$ structural for $F=mv$ and Aristotelian, content axis only for Decay World where the structural axis is out of scope). The most pointed empirical pattern is a qualitative-versus-quantitative asymmetry: in Decay World, models almost never predict the wrong direction of change, but frequently compute the wrong ratio by slipping back to standard-physics relations. The protocol also surfaces two methodology findings: LLM-judge reliability does not transfer across frameworks, and Stage 4 self-review is weak in every framework, with the model's own review wrongly reporting no earlier error in at least two-thirds of the trials that actually contained one. We release the full prompts, responses, verdicts, and audit records.

</details>


### [83] [Understanding Guest Preferences and Optimizing Two-sided Marketplaces: Airbnb as an Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00280)
*Yufei Wu,Daniel Schmierer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Airbnb结合经济建模与因果推断技术，分析客人基于价格等因素的预订行为及偏好异质性，以优化定价工具和个性化推荐，提升市场供需匹配效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Airbnb致力于连接房客与房东，需深入理解客人对价格等因素的敏感度差异，以便为房东提供更优的定价工具，同时实现个性化推荐，更好地匹配供需双方需求。

Method: 采用经济建模与因果推断相结合的方法，量化分析客人预订决策中价格弹性的影响，并识别不同客人群体和房源类型下的偏好异质性。

Result: 研究为Airbnb提供了优化房东定价建议工具、提升客人个性化推荐算法、以及改善市场供需平衡机制的数据支持与理论依据。

Conclusion: 通过严谨的经济计量与因果分析，Airbnb能够更精准地理解市场微观机制，从而设计更有效的市场干预工具，实现房东收益最大化与客人体验优化的双赢。

Abstract: Airbnb is a community based on connection and belonging -- many hosts on Airbnb are everyday people who share their worlds to provide guests with the feeling of connection and being at home; Airbnb strives to connect people and places. Among our efforts to connect guests and hosts, we provide tools to enable hosts to set competitive prices, which helps improve affordability for guests while helping hosts get more bookings. We also personalize the guest experience to show them the listings that match their needs.
  To help inform these efforts, we combine economic modeling and causal inference techniques to understand how guests book stays based on the prices hosts set, among other factors, and how that preference varies across different guests and listings. Such understanding helps us identify opportunities for Airbnb to support the marketplace and better connect guests and hosts. For example, understanding how much guests respond to different prices helps optimize the tools that we provide to hosts, in order to enable hosts to choose and set competitive prices that further balance demand and supply. As another example, understanding heterogeneity in guest preferences helps us personalize the guest experience and better match them with the listings that meet their needs, based on how much they respond to different prices and other factors.

</details>
