Abstract: Mixed-precision quantization (MPQ) has become a key technique for deploying large language models under stringent memory and compute constraints. We first identify a phenomenon that we term the Perplexity Illusion: layers ranked as important by perplexity-based sensitivity show little rank correlation with those that are most influential for complex reasoning performance, with Kendall $τ\approx 0$ in our analysis. We further reveal an Alignment-Diversity Tradeoff: using only target-task calibration data can degrade post-quantization performance, whereas incorporating general-domain data stabilizes sensitivity estimation and improves robustness across tasks. Based on these observations, we propose TASA (Task-Aware Sensitivity Analysis), a two-level framework that jointly optimizes calibration-data composition and mixed-precision bit allocation. Specifically, TASA searches for a calibration-data mixture using a training-free gradient-trace alignment criterion, and then aggregates perplexity and reasoning-oriented sensitivity signals to guide both inter-layer and intra-layer bit allocation. Experiments on LLaMA-3-8B and Qwen2.5-7B reveal a precision inversion: appropriately allocated 3.5-bit models can match or surpass less task-aware 4-bit baselines. At an average precision of 3.5 bits, TASA matches or outperforms several competitive 4-bit uniform baselines in aggregate accuracy, and improves over the strongest W3 baseline on GSM8K by more than 20 absolute points on LLaMA-3-8B. These results show that calibration-data composition substantially affects task-sensitive quantization, a factor underexplored in prior work.

</details>


### [122] [Valdi: Value Diffusion World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00917)
*Christopher Lindenberg,Kashyap Chitta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Value Diffusion World Models (Valdi)，结合端到端在线训练与潜在扩散动力学模型，用单步扩散实现低延迟 MPC，在 CarRacing 上与确定性 MLP 基线持平，揭示多模态预测与控制性能的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型可用于模型预测控制 (MPC)，但需要既快速又能表示不确定未来的动力学预测。扩散模型虽能建模不确定动力学，但其迭代推理导致高延迟，难以用于低延迟潜在规划。

Method: 提出 Value Diffusion World Models (Valdi)，结合端到端在线训练用于 MPC 与潜在扩散动力学模型，在训练和推理均使用单步扩散以实现低延迟。

Result: 在 CarRacing 环境的初步实验中，Valdi 使用单步扩散在训练和推理时均能匹配确定性 MLP 基线的性能。实验揭示了在该设置下预测多模态性与控制性能之间的权衡。

Conclusion: Valdi 通过单步扩散桥接了扩散模型的多模态建模能力与低延迟 MPC 规划的需求，但在当前设置中多模态预测与控制性能存在权衡，未来工作可探索如何更好地利用多模态性提升控制。

Abstract: World models can enable Model Predictive Control (MPC), but this requires dynamics prediction that is both fast enough for online use and expressive enough to represent uncertain futures. Diffusion models offer a natural mechanism for modeling uncertain dynamics, yet their iterative inference procedure makes them difficult to use for low-latency latent planning. We bridge this gap with Value Diffusion World Models (Valdi), combining end-to-end online training for MPC with a latent diffusion dynamics model. In preliminary experiments on the CarRacing environment, we show that Valdi, using a single diffusion step at both training and inference, matches a deterministic MLP baseline. Our experiments expose a trade-off between predictive multimodality and control performance in this setup. Code is available at https://github.com/Kit115/ValueDiffusionWorldModels.

</details>


### [123] [Human-Machine Collaboration on Generative Meta-Learning: Model and Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00926)
*Midhun Parakkal Unni,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式元学习与人类反馈（GMHF）框架，利用专家直觉引导数据合成，解决目标域数据不可用时的分布外泛化问题。理论推导泛化误差界，证明将生成数据分布与人类对目标物理的认知对齐可降低风险。实验在Duffing振子与概率模型上验证：专家可靠性越高，部署损失越低，生成分布与目标分布散度收敛，确认了理论预测的散度最小化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在训练分布之外的环境中泛化困难，尤其当目标域数据完全或部分不可用时。现有方法难以在无目标数据情况下有效缩小域差距，需要利用人类专家的先验知识指导数据生成。

Method: 提出GMHF框架：基于泛化误差理论推导风险界；采用条件神经常微分方程（cNODE）作为生成式数字孪生，配合强化学习（RL）智能体；RL智能体根据专家反馈迭代优化生成轨迹的潜在物理参数，引导元学习器逼近未观测的目标分布。

Result: 在非线性Duffing振子上实验表明：GMHF随专家可靠性提升显著降低部署损失，生成数据与目标数据的散度在可靠反馈下收敛，验证了理论预测的散度最小化机制；在非动力学概率模型上进一步确认框架可推广至非ODE系统。

Conclusion: GMHF通过人机协作将专家直觉形式化为生成过程的优化目标，实现了无目标数据条件下的鲁棒分布外泛化，为分布偏移下的可靠泛化提供了理论与算法双重保障。

Abstract: Generalizing machine learning models to environments that differ from their training distribution remains a critical hurdle, particularly when data from the target domain is entirely or partially unavailable. We propose Generative Meta-Learning with Human Feedback (GMHF), a novel framework that bridges this domain gap by leveraging expert intuition to guide data synthesis. Grounded in a theoretical analysis of generalization error, we derive bounds demonstrating that aligning the distribution of generated data with human beliefs regarding the target physics significantly mitigates risk. GMHF operationalizes this insight by employing a Conditional Neural ODE (cNODE) as a generative digital twin, coupled with a Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent. The agent iteratively refines the latent physical parameters of the generated trajectories based on feedback, effectively steering the meta-learner toward the unobserved target distribution. Empirical validation on a nonlinear Duffing oscillator shows that GMHF substantially reduces deployment loss as expert reliability increases, and that the divergence between generated and target data falls under reliable feedback, directly corroborating the divergence-minimisation mechanism predicted by our theory. Further experiments on a non-dynamical probabilistic model confirm that the framework extends beyond ODE-governed systems, establishing human-AI collaboration as a rigorous catalyst for robust generalisation under distribution shift.

</details>


### [124] [Explainable AI for Cancer Drug Response Prediction: Beyond Univariate Feature Attributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00931)
*Martino Ciaperoni,Margherita Lalli,Simone Piaggesi,Martina Varisco,Francesco Carli,Riccardo Guidotti,Dino Pedreschi,Francesco Raimondi,Fosca Giannotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了ILLUME+，一个可扩展的后验可解释性框架，用于从转录组谱预测癌症药物响应。该方法超越了单基因评估，捕捉多种互补的解释形式，产生比现有基线更稳定的基因重要性评分，恢复已知的药物-基因关联和作用机制，并支持AI辅助假设生成以发现新的相互作用驱动的分子信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在癌症药物响应预测中的可解释性方法存在计算成本高、鲁棒性差、且将复杂的药物响应简化为单变量基因重要性评分的问题，忽略了驱动敏感性和耐药性的协调基因活动。

Method: 提出了ILLUME+，一个可扩展的后验可解释性框架，集成到端到端流程中，超越单基因评估，捕捉多种互补形式的解释。

Result: ILLUME+产生比现有基线更稳定的基因重要性评分，成功恢复已建立的药物-基因关联和作用机制，并能够实现AI辅助的假设生成以发现新的相互作用驱动的分子信号。

Conclusion: ILLUME+解决了现有可解释性方法的局限性，为精准肿瘤学提供了可靠的生物学洞察，兼顾了预测准确性和生物学解释的可靠性。

Abstract: Predicting cancer drug response from transcriptomic profiles is a cornerstone of precision oncology, yet the scientific value of machine learning models hinges not solely on predictive accuracy, but also on their capacity to generate reliable biological insights. Current explainability approaches in this setting are computationally costly, lack robustness, and reduce complex drug response to univariate gene importance scores, overlooking the coordinated gene activity that drives sensitivity and resistance. In this work, we present ILLUME+, a scalable post-hoc explainability framework that moves beyond single-gene assessments to capture multiple, complementary forms of explanation. Integrated into our end-to-end pipeline, ILLUME+ produces more stable gene importance scores than existing baselines, recovers established drug-gene associations and mechanisms of action, and enables AI-assisted hypothesis generation to uncover novel interaction-driven molecular signals in cancer biology.

</details>


### [125] [Diffeomorphic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00947)
*Ludwig Winkler,Andrew Leaver-Fay,Joseph Kleinhenz,Pan Kessel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于微分同胚的优化方法，将生成模型中的数据流形映射到简单的基础空间中进行梯度下降，从而在保持流形几何结构的同时实现更平滑的优化，并将其扩展到矩阵李群SO(3)和SE(3)上，应用于蛋白质设计任务，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型学习的数据分布位于高维环境空间中的低维流形上，直接在环境空间进行梯度下降会因忽略流形几何而迅速偏离流形，且优化目标高维、崎岖且非凸。需要一种能够利用流形几何结构、保持轨迹在流形上的优化方法。

Method: 利用扩散模型和流模型提供从数据流形到简单基础空间的映射，在基础空间执行梯度下降，证明这等价于数据流形上的黎曼梯度下降（含O(λ²)修正项）。将方法推广到矩阵李群SO(3)和SE(3)，推导出与自动微分兼容的SO(3)梯度，并设计广义伴随状态方法用于通过李群ODE求解器的反向传播。

Result: 在蛋白质设计任务中：二级结构靶向任务上，FrameFlow配合微分同胚优化达到91.3%的Ramachandran靶向残基率，远超调优引导的63.3%；肽段结合亲和力任务上，优于OC-Flow且速度提升2倍；在PDB测试集上，数百残基结构的Rosetta能量降低数千单位。

Conclusion: 微分同胚优化通过利用生成模型隐含的流形几何，提供了一种原则性的、保流形的优化框架，且在蛋白质设计等实际任务中展现出优越的性能和效率，为几何深度学习在科学计算中的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generative models learn data distributions that reside on a low-dimensional manifold within a higher-dimensional ambient space. Optimizing differentiable objectives on this manifold is challenging: the ambient loss landscape is high-dimensional, rugged, and non-convex. Direct gradient descent, blind to the manifold's geometry, quickly drifts off it. Diffeomorphic optimization starts from the observation that diffusion and flow models provide a map from the data manifold to a much simpler base space in which we perform gradient descent. Using differential geometry, we show this is equivalent to Riemannian gradient descent on the data manifold up to $\mathcal{O}(λ^2)$ corrections, keeping trajectories on-manifold by construction and yielding a smoother optimization surface. For protein design, we extend diffeomorphic optimization to the matrix Lie groups $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ and $\mathrm{SE}(3)$, deriving an autograd-compatible $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ gradient and a generalized adjoint-state method for backpropagation through Lie-group ODE solvers. Diffeomorphic optimization improves over tuned guidance on secondary-structure targeting with FrameFlow ($91.3\%$ vs. $63.3\%$ of residues in the Ramachandran target), outperforms OC-Flow on peptide binding affinity at $2\times$ the speed, and reduces Rosetta energies by thousands of units across the PDB test set for structures with hundreds of residues.

</details>


### [126] [Aionoscope: Debugging Latent-State Accessibility in Time-Series Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00956)
*Alexander Chemeris,Ming Jin,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Aionoscope 是一种基于生成器的诊断工具，用于评估时间序列表征中潜在状态的可访问性。实验表明，大多数模型在粗粒度组件识别上表现良好，但在细粒度过程状态（如时序、相位、幅度、频率、机制变量）的恢复上存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列模型评估主要关注预测或分类分数，无法揭示表征是否保留了用户关心的过程状态（事件时序、相位、幅度、频率、机制变量）。需要一种工具来诊断冻结表征中潜在状态的可访问性。

Method: 提出 Aionoscope，将过程生成与观测渲染分离，生成带有精确分类和密集标签的合成数据流。将其实例化为原始过程混合，并使用通用池化线性探针协议评估 37 个模型加适配器系统。

Result: 发现粗粒度与细粒度可访问性之间存在不匹配：大多数系统易于恢复组件存在性，但密集过程状态恢复不可靠。最高密集探针行平均掩码 R^2 为 0.689，而密集特征 Oracle 达到 0.999。

Conclusion: Aionoscope 成功揭示了表征在粗粒度看似信息丰富，实则隐藏了调试所需的精细过程状态变量的失败模式，为时间序列表征的诊断提供了新工具。

Abstract: Time-series models are often evaluated by what they can forecast or classify, but those scores do not show whether their representations preserve the process state a user may want to inspect: event timing, phase, amplitude, frequency, or regime variables. We introduce Aionoscope, a generator-based diagnostic tool for debugging latent-state accessibility in frozen time-series representations. Aionoscope separates process generation from observation rendering, producing seeded synthetic streams with exact categorical and dense labels across mixture complexity and nuisance variation.
  We instantiate Aionoscope as Primitive Process Mixtures and evaluate 37 model-plus-adapter systems with a common pooled linear-probe protocol. The main result is a mismatch between coarse and fine-grained accessibility. Most systems make component presence easy to recover, but expose dense process state much less reliably: the highest observed dense-probe row reaches 0.689 mean masked $R^2$, while a dense-feature oracle reaches 0.999. This is the failure mode Aionoscope is designed to surface: a representation can look informative at the level of "what kind of signal is present" while hiding the timing, phase, amplitude, frequency, or regime variables needed for debugging.

</details>


### [127] [LeNEPA: No-Augmentation Next-Latent Prediction for Time-Series Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00958)
*Alexander Chemeris,Ming Jin,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeNEPA是一种无需数据增强的时间序列自监督学习方法，采用因果骨干网络的下一个潜在token预测目标，结合SIGReg各向同性正则化。实验表明，在固定配方压力测试中，LeNEPA在PTB-XL和合成Diag数据集上均能保持冻结探针性能增益，且早期表示学习更快；外部验证显示其在UCR-128上达到77.65%随机森林准确率，接近最先进方法。整体支持无增强潜在预测作为低调优时间序列SSL的候选配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列自监督学习通常依赖领域特定的视图和增强选择，编码领域特定不变性。本文研究当预训练信号家族改变时，保持方法特定配置不变重用SSL配方的表现，将其构建为固定配方压力测试，而非与最优调优方法对比。

Method: 提出Latent Euclidean Next-Embedding Prediction Architecture (LeNEPA)，一种无增强的下一个潜在token预测目标，采用因果骨干网络。LeNEPA用基于SIGReg的各向同性正则化替代原版NEPA的stop-gradient/EMA稳定化，并在轻量级投影空间计算预测损失（评估时丢弃）。

Result: 在固定视野冻结探针协议下，ECG调优的JEPA配方在PTB-XL上域内表现强但未改动重用于Diag时较弱；LeNEPA在两数据集上均保持有用的冻结探针增益。学习曲线显示LeNEPA更快获得早期表示：达到最终AUROC/AUPRC增益80%仅需2-5k更新，而JEPA需5-10k。外部冻结编码器检查中，CauKer预训练的LeNEPA变体在UCR-128随机森林准确率达77.65%，距Mantis 1.16分，距MOMENT 0.24分。

Conclusion: 结果支持无增强潜在预测作为低调优时间序列自监督学习的有用候选配方。

Abstract: Time series are central to modern data mining applications, from industrial telemetry and server metrics to finance and physiology, yet time-series self-supervised learning often depends on view and augmentation choices that encode domain-specific invariances. We study how an SSL recipe behaves when its method-specific configuration is reused unchanged after the pretraining signal family changes, framing this as a fixed-recipe stress test rather than a comparison against optimally tuned methods. We introduce Latent Euclidean Next-Embedding Prediction Architecture (LeNEPA), a no-augmentation next-latent-token objective with a causal backbone. LeNEPA replaces the stop-gradient/EMA stabilization used by vanilla NEPA with SIGReg-based isotropy regularization and computes the predictive loss in a lightweight projected space that is discarded for evaluation. We compare LeNEPA with an ECG-tuned JEPA recipe under a fixed-horizon frozen-probe protocol on PTB-XL and Diag, a synthetic diagnostic corpus generated with Aionoscope. Both methods are retrained independently on each dataset while keeping their method-specific recipes unchanged. In this protocol, the ECG-tuned JEPA recipe is strong in-domain on PTB-XL but weaker when reused unchanged on Diag, whereas LeNEPA preserves useful frozen-probe gains on both datasets. Learning curves suggest faster early representation acquisition: LeNEPA reaches 80% of its final AUROC/AUPRC gain after 2--5k updates, compared with 5--10k updates for the faster JEPA readout. As a separate external frozen-encoder check, a CauKer-pretrained LeNEPA variant reaches 77.65% mean UCR-128 Random-Forest accuracy in a single-seed, best-checkpoint run, within 1.16 points of Mantis and within 0.24 points of MOMENT (77.89%). Overall, the results support no-augmentation latent prediction as a useful candidate recipe for low-retuning time-series SSL.

</details>


### [128] [Automatic Detection of Stress from Speech in the Trier Social Stress Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00986)
*Hanna Drimalla,Wieland R. Cremer,Christine Kraus,Oliver T. Wolf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 语音可作为无创指标，自动检测压力情境并预测生理/情感应激反应的多个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动检测语音中的压力可为行为研究或临床评估提供无创洞察。

Method: 从50名参与者收集语音数据（TSST压力组 vs 非压力对照组），采用包含说话人分离和机器学习模型的处理流水线进行分析。

Result: 压力检测性能显著高于均值基线；相关生理和情感应激反应可从声学韵律特征中部分预测；特征重要性分析识别出对模型性能贡献最大的预测因子。

Conclusion: 研究发现语音可作为人类应激反应多维度的有意义且无创的指标。

Abstract: Automatically detecting stress in speech provides an unobtrusive way to gain insights relevant to behavioral research or clinical assessment. This study investigates the automatic differentiation between a stressful and non-stressful situation, and the prediction of physiological and affective stress responses. Speech data was collected from 50 participants who either completed the Trier Social Stress Test (TSST) or a non-stressful control condition. With a processing pipeline that included speaker diarization and machine learning models, we achieved stress detection performance significantly above a mean baseline. Moreover, relevant physiological and affective stress responses were partially predictable from acoustic-prosodic features. Feature-importance analyses identified the most informative predictors contributing to model performance. The findings demonstrate that speech can serve as a meaningful and unobtrusive indicator of multiple dimensions of the human stress response.

</details>


### [129] [Generative Model Proposal based Particle Filtering for Data Assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01012)
*Chandni Nagda,Mayank Shrivastavam Gudrun Thorkelsdottir,Gan Zhang,Morteza Mardani,Arindam Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Proposal Particle Filters (FPPF)，通过学习基于条件生成模型的最优建议分布来近似粒子传播的方差最小化建议，结合观测引导粒子向高似然区域移动，降低权重方差并延缓退化，同时保留可计算的似然评估和贝叶斯更新步骤，并通过局部化策略扩展到高维问题，在非线性、非高斯和高维动力系统中优于统计基线和生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典数据同化方法常依赖线性高斯等简化假设，导致在复杂场景下不准确；粒子滤波虽移除这些假设，但在高维空间易崩溃；近期生成式方法虽学习条件状态转移，但缺乏 principled Bayesian updates，无法恢复正确的滤波后验且长时程易累积误差。

Method: FPPF 学习一个条件生成模型作为建议分布，近似方差最小化的最优建议，利用观测将粒子引向高似然区域再加权，降低权重方差并延缓退化；该建议分布允许解析似然评估，从而计算准确的重要性权重并保留贝叶斯更新步骤；针对高维问题进一步引入局部化策略解决标准粒子滤波的另一失效模式。

Result: 在多种动力系统上的大量实验表明，FPPF 在非线性、非高斯和高维设置下均优于统计基线方法和其他生成式方法。

Conclusion: FPPF 将生成式建模与粒子滤波原理结合，提供了一种既保留贝叶斯更新严谨性又能应对高维、非线性非高斯系统的滤波框架，有效缓解了粒子退化和误差累积问题。

Abstract: Data assimilation models state dynamics conditioned on sequential observations, and has wide-ranging scientific applications. In the filtering setting, the goal is to model the posterior over the current state given all observations so far. Classical solutions typically make simplifying distributional or functional assumptions, e.g., linear-Gaussian systems, which can be inaccurate in many scenarios. In principle, particle filters (PFs) remove these assumptions, yet often collapse in high dimensions. Recent generative approaches learn conditional state transitions, but without principled Bayesian updates they do not recover the correct filtering posterior and can accumulate error over long horizons. In this work, we introduce Flow Proposal Particle Filters (FPPF), which learn a conditional generative model based proposal approximating the variance-minimizing optimal proposal for particle propagation. Conditioning on observations steers particles toward high-likelihood regions before weighting, reducing weight variance and delaying degeneracy. Since our proposal admits tractable likelihood evaluation, FPPF computes accurate importance weights and retains a Bayesian update step. We further extend FPPF to high-dimensional problems through localization strategies, adressing another standard PF failure mode. Extensive experiments on a variety of dynamical systems show that FPPF outperforms statistical baselines and other generative methods in non-linear, non-Gaussian, and high-dimensional regimes.

</details>


### [130] [Seahorse: A Unified Benchmarking Framework for Spatiotemporal Event Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01022)
*Yahya Aalaila,Gerrit Großmann,Sebastian Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEAHORSE是一个统一的时空点过程（STPP）实验框架，解决了神经STPP模型间对比不可复现的问题，并配合合成压力测试套件HawkesNest，揭示了不同模型家族在事件模式复杂度增加时的归纳偏差差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经STPP模型（强度模型、条件密度模型、连续时间潜在动力学、归一化流空间解码器、基于分数的生成机制）实现差异大，涉及预处理、坐标归一化、数据划分、似然约定和评估协议等，导致模型间公平对比极其困难。

Method: 提出SEAHORSE统一框架，将神经STPP形式化为通用的encode-evolve-decode接口，在单一可执行基准协议下训练、调优和评估所有模型家族，支持原始坐标似然报告。配合HawkesNest合成压力测试套件，通过控制事件模式复杂度进行诊断性研究。

Result: 实验表明，随着事件模式复杂度增加，不同模型家族的归纳偏差显现，部分模型性能急剧下降而其他模型保持稳定，验证了SEAHORSE进行受控诊断研究的能力。

Conclusion: SEAHORSE为STPP研究提供了可复现的统一基准，不仅实现了公平模型对比，更支持深入的归纳偏差分析，为时空点过程建模的理论理解和实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal point processes (STPPs) model event data in continuous time and space, with applications in mobility, epidemiology, and public safety. Recent neural STPPs span expressive intensity models, conditional density models, continuous-time latent dynamics, normalizing-flow spatial decoders, and score-based generative mechanisms. Yet comparison remains fragile because implementations differ in preprocessing, coordinate normalization, splits, likelihood conventions, and evaluation protocols. We present SEAHORSE, a unified framework for reproducible STPP experimentation. SEAHORSE formalizes neural STPPs through a common encode-evolve-decode interface and trains, tunes, and evaluates every model family under a single executable benchmark protocol with raw-coordinate likelihood reporting. This enables fair comparisons but, more importantly, controlled diagnostic studies. We pair SEAHORSE with HawkesNest, a synthetic stress-test suite, and show that increasing event-pattern complexity exposes each family's inductive bias, degrading some models sharply and leaving others stable. Code: https://github.com/YahyaAalaila/seahorse.

</details>


### [131] [The Model Organism Lottery: Model Organism Interpretability Strongly Depends on Training Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01033)
*Andrzej Szablewski,Gabriel Konar-Steenberg,Raffaello Fornasiere,Nikita Menon,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了模型有机体（MOs）作为白盒可解释性技术测试平台的有效性，发现当前基于事后微调构建的MOs可能使可解释性任务变得过于简单，而更现实的集成训练方法往往产生更难解释的MOs，对现有MOs作为可解释性代理的有效性提出质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MOs主要通过事后监督微调（SFT）或合成文档构建，但先前研究表明这些方法可能使可解释性方法过于容易地识别隐藏行为，缺乏现实性。需要系统评估不同训练目标、目标行为、模型架构和数据生成管道对MOs可解释性的影响。

Method: 基于OLMo2-1B和gemma-3-1b-it构建了54个MOs，采用七种不同训练技术（包括标准事后SFT、事后DPO、以及将MO数据集成到OLMo后训练DPO阶段的更现实集成训练）。使用激活预言机、激活转向、logit lens和稀疏自编码器四种可解释性方法进行基准测试。

Result: （1）MOs的可解释性强烈依赖于训练目标、目标行为、模型架构和训练数据生成管道；（2）即使控制目标行为表达强度的差异，仍存在显著方差；（3）更现实的集成训练往往产生比标准事后方法更难解释的MOs。

Conclusion: 实验结果对当前MOs作为可解释性代理的有效性提出了实质性质疑，表明需要更现实的MOs构建方法来评估可解释性技术。

Abstract: Model organisms (MOs) - language models trained to exhibit undesired or unnatural behaviours - are frequently used as testbeds for evaluating white-box interpretability techniques. Current MOs are typically constructed via post-hoc supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on behavioural transcripts or synthetic documents. Prior research has shown that interpretability methods can easily identify hidden behaviours in these MOs. However, recent work suggests that such post-hoc training methods may make interpretability unrealistically easy. We investigate this claim by constructing a suite of 54 $\verb|OLMo2-1B|$- and $\verb|gemma-3-1b-it|$-based MOs trained with seven different techniques, including standard post-hoc SFT, post-hoc DPO, and more realistic integration of MO data into the OLMo post-training DPO phase. We use these MO variants to benchmark activation oracles, activation steering, logit lens, and sparse autoencoders. Our findings show that (i) MO interpretability depends strongly on training objective, target behaviour, model architecture, and training data generation pipeline; (ii) substantial variance remains even after controlling for differences in the strength of target behaviour expression; and (iii) our more realistic $\textit{integrated training}$ often yields less interpretable MOs than standard post-hoc methods. Our results cast substantial doubt on the validity of current MOs as interpretability proxies.

</details>


### [132] [GSRQ: Gain-Shape Residual Quantization for Sub-1-bit KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01065)
*Soosung Kim,Minjae Park,Eui-Young Chung,Jaeyong Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了增益-形状残差量化（GSRQ）用于大语言模型KV缓存压缩，通过改进K-means算法解决高维空间下的质心收缩问题，在1-bit量化下将LLaMA-3-8B在LongBench上的平均准确率从11.34提升至33.54，超越VQLLM 22.20个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署受限于KV缓存内存随上下文长度线性增长。向量量化（VQ）特别是残差量化（RQ）虽能将存储推向sub-1-bit，但现有方法多依赖标准ℓ2 K-means学习码本。作者发现高维下欧氏质心平均会导致质心收缩，削弱ℓ2失真的角度对齐项，使方向保持更困难。

Method: 提出增益-形状K-means（GSKM）作为K-means的直接替代，提升方向保真度同时匹配或改善ℓ2失真。在此基础上构建增益-形状残差量化（GSRQ），将加权扩展的GSKM集成到RQ流水线中。

Result: 在LLaMA-3-8B上，GSRQ在各比特率下显著优于强基线。1-bit时，LongBench任务平均准确率从11.34提升至33.54，比VQLLM提高22.20个百分点。

Conclusion: GSKMS解决了高维K-means的质心收缩问题，在保持或改善ℓ2失真的同时提升方向保真度，使sub-1-bit KV缓存量化成为可能。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs) with extended context windows is increasingly constrained by the linear growth of Key-Value (KV) cache memory. Vector Quantization (VQ), particularly Residual Quantization (RQ), is a promising approach for pushing KV cache storage toward the sub-1-bit regime by progressively encoding residuals with small codebooks. However, most VQ methods still rely on standard $\ell_2$ $K$-means as the core codebook-learning primitive. We identify a subtle high-dimensional issue of this primitive: Euclidean centroid averaging can induce centroid shrinkage, which weakens the angular alignment term in the $\ell_2$ distortion and makes directional preservation harder. To address this issue, we propose Gain-Shape $K$-means (GSKM), a drop-in replacement for $K$-means that improves directional fidelity while matching, and in some regimes improving, $\ell_2$ distortion. We then build Gain-Shape Residual Quantization (GSRQ) by incorporating a weighted extension of GSKM into an RQ pipeline. On LLaMA-3-8B, GSRQ substantially improves over strong KV cache quantization baselines across bit rates. At 1-bit, it improves the average accuracy across LongBench tasks from 11.34 to 33.54, a gain of 22.20 percentage points over VQLLM.

</details>


### [133] [Balancing Expressivity and Learnability in Quantum Kernel Bandit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01080)
*Yuqi Huang,Vincent Y. F. Tan,Sharu Theresa Jose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出投影量子核和经典核近似技术，降低量子核特征维度并保留关键量子性质，开发误指定高斯过程老虎机算法，推导遗憾界权衡近似误差与信息增益，指导最优模型复杂度选择，实验显示样本效率优于全量子核且大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NISQ时代任务（量子控制、态制备、变分量子算法）中，量子核可提供领域特定归纳偏置带来的量子优势，但直接使用高维全量子核会增加模型复杂度和信息增益，导致累积遗憾升高、可学习性变差。

Method: 提出投影量子核和经典核近似技术，降低特征维度同时保留关键量子性质；基于这些近似核开发误指定高斯过程老虎机算法，并推导遗憾界以表征近似误差与信息增益的权衡。

Result: 遗憾界为选择最优模型复杂度提供原则性指导；实验表明，该方法在样本效率上优于全量子核，并大幅降低计算开销，实现了可扩展的量子原生应用高斯过程优化。

Conclusion: 通过投影量子核和经典近似技术降低量子核维度，结合误指定GP老虎机算法，可有效平衡近似误差与信息增益，显著提升样本效率和计算可扩展性，为NISQ时代量子原生优化任务提供实用框架。

Abstract: We investigate Gaussian process (GP) bandit optimization with quantum kernels, assuming the mean reward function lies in the reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) induced by the quantum kernel. This setting is motivated by NISQ-era tasks such as quantum control, state preparation and variational quantum algorithms. While quantum kernels can offer a `quantum advantage' via domain-specific inductive biases, naïvely using full, high-dimensional kernels increases model complexity and information gain, leading to higher cumulative regret and poor learnability. To address this, we propose projected quantum kernels and classical kernel approximation techniques that reduce feature dimensionality while preserving key quantum properties. Using these approximate kernels, we develop misspecified GP bandit algorithms and derive regret bounds that characterize the trade-off between approximation error and information gain. The regret bounds provide principled guidance for selecting the optimal model complexity. Empirically, our methods outperform full quantum kernels in sample efficiency, while substantially reducing computational overhead, enabling scalable GP optimization for quantum-native applications.

</details>


### [134] [When Context Compensates for Sparse Event History: AlphaEarth for Spatio-Temporal Point-Process Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01082)
*Yahya Aalaila,Mouad Elhamdi,Gerrit Großmann,Daniel Jenson,Elizaveta Semenova,Sebastian Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用 AlphaEarth 嵌入作为空间上下文，在事件历史稀疏时显著提升跨区域点过程预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在局部事件历史稀疏时，外生空间上下文是否能补偿时空点过程模型跨空间泛化的不足。

Method: 基于固定的对数高斯考克斯过程骨干网络，对比仅使用事件的模型与加入 AlphaEarth 嵌入作为线性空间上下文的模型，在八个保留区域上进行急救医疗服务（EMS）预测的空间迁移评估，固定预测锚点，扫描历史长度 w，仅使用锚点前可用的 AlphaEarth 嵌入。

Result: AlphaEarth 嵌入在所有历史长度下均提升域外预测性能，历史稀缺时增益最大：1-2 周历史长度下获得约 2-6 倍乘法改进，随历史增加逐渐减弱至 w=20-104 周时约 10-20% 的提升。

Conclusion: 当事件历史有限时，上下文信息能显著稳定跨空间迁移的点过程预测。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal point-process models must often generalise across space when local event histories are sparse. We study whether exogenous spatial context can compensate in such regimes. Using a fixed log-Gaussian Cox process backbone, we compare an event-only model with the same model augmented by AlphaEarth embeddings as linear spatial context. We evaluate spatial transfer on emergency medical services (EMS) forecasting across eight held-out regions, fixed forecast anchors, and a sweep over history length $w$, using only AlphaEarth (AE) embeddings available strictly before each anchor. AE improves out-of-region predictive performance across all history regimes, with the largest gains under scarce histories: approximately $2$--$6\times$ multiplicative improvements at $1-2$ weeks, tapering to roughly $10$--$20\%$ at $w=20$--$104$ weeks. These results show that contextual information can substantially stabilise spatially transferred point-process forecasts when event history is limited.

</details>


### [135] [Staleness-Learning Rate Scaling Laws for Asynchronous RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01083)
*Jingwei Song,Haofeng Xu,Jie Xiao,Chengke Bao,Jingwei Shi,Pengbin Feng,Weixun Wang,Yuhang Han,Chuan Wu,Linfeng Zhang,Bill Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究异步GRPO中陈旧rollout的影响，显式建模行为策略，推导出代理梯度偏差为O(S*η)，并给出两约束稳定性条件η << min{R_batch/(S*G_upd), R_crit/(T*G_upd)}，解释了最大稳定学习率在有限视界下对陈旧性依赖较弱的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高吞吐RLHF系统将rollout生成与策略优化解耦，导致学习器更新时使用陈旧rollout。本文旨在研究这种陈旧性在异步GRPO中的影响。

Method: 在GRPO代理目标中显式引入行为策略，区分学习器使用的代理梯度映射与分布相关总体目标的真实全导数。在局部有界性、分布平滑性和行为策略平滑性假设下进行理论分析。

Result: 证明陈旧rollout引入的每步代理梯度偏差为O(S*η)（S为最大rollout滞后，η为学习率）。推导条件性崩溃时间缩放定律：当周期内漂移低于批次级裁剪半径时，崩溃主要由累积学习器漂移T*η决定；当陈旧rollout约束激活时，稳定性显式依赖于S*η。得到双约束稳定性条件η << min{R_batch/(S*G_upd), R_crit/(T*G_upd)}。

Conclusion: 该稳定性条件解释了为何在视界受限 regime 下，最大稳定学习率似乎对陈旧性依赖较弱。

Abstract: High-throughput RLHF systems often decouple rollout generation from policy optimization, leading to the use of stale rollouts during learner updates. In this work, we study the effect of such staleness in asynchronous GRPO. We make the behavior policy explicit in the GRPO surrogate objective and distinguish between the surrogate-gradient mapping used by the learner and the true total derivative of a distribution-dependent population objective. Under assumptions of local boundedness, distributional smoothness, and behavior-policy smoothness, we show that stale rollouts introduce a per-step surrogate-gradient bias of order O(S * eta), where S denotes the maximum rollout lag and eta denotes the learning rate. We further derive a conditional collapse-time scaling law: when within-cycle drift remains below a batch-level clipping radius, collapse is governed primarily by cumulative learner drift T * eta; when the stale-rollout constraint is active, stability instead depends explicitly on S * eta. This yields a two-constraint stability condition eta << min{R_batch / (S * G_upd), R_crit / (T * G_upd)}, explaining why the maximum stable learning rate may appear weakly dependent on staleness in the horizon-limited regime.

</details>


### [136] [CausalMix: Data Mixture as Causal Inference for Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01104)
*Zinan Tang,Yukun Zhang,Shaomian Zheng,Zhuoshi Pan,Qizhi Pei,Dingnan Jin,Jun Zhou,Yujun Wang,Biqing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In Large Language Model (LLM) training, data mixing plays a pivotal role in determining model performance. Recent methods optimize mixture weights via proxy models, but they rely on the assumption of static data distributions. As a result, when the underlying data pool shifts, these methods require costly retraining from scratch. This limitation restricts their ability to scale seamlessly from small settings to larger data pools and model sizes. In this paper, we propose CausalMix to address this limitation by casting data mixture optimization as a causal inference problem. We formulate the statistical features of the data pool as covariates and the domain mixture as the treatment. After fitting a causal model on 512 runs of Qwen2.5-0.5B to estimate the Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE), we extrapolate the optimal mixture for an 800K data pool and apply it to train a 7B model. Furthermore, we successfully generalize the framework to long chain-of-thought data on Qwen3-4B-Base. By leveraging causal modeling to isolate confounding biases, CausalMix dynamically infers state-dependent optimal data mixtures. Extensive experiments show that the mixture guided by CausalMix consistently improves performance across multiple downstream tasks, outperforming RegMix and other baselines. In addition, we use the CATE Interpreter to provide visual analysis of the learned mixing strategy. Overall, CausalMix offers a causal and interpretable framework for optimizing LLM data mixtures.

</details>


### [137] [SynLaD: Latent Diffusion for Generating Synthesizable Molecules Conditioned on 3D Pharmacophore Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01105)
*Miruna Cretu,John Bradshaw,Patricia Suriana,Saeed Saremi,Omar Mahmood,Kirill Shmilovich,Kangway Chuang,Vishnu Sresht,Colin Grambow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present SynLaD, a latent diffusion framework for small-molecule generation that unifies ligand-based drug design objectives (what to make) with synthetic accessibility (how to make it). Current models typically optimize one objective at the expense of the other, creating a bottleneck for discovering high-scoring and synthesizable molecules. SynLaD combines reaction-constrained generation with pharmacophore-conditioned 3D design by learning a latent space that decodes to both 3D structures and synthesis pathways. An encoder maps molecules to a latent representation used by two decoder heads: (i) a geometric head that reconstructs atom types and coordinates and (ii) an autoregressive synthesis head that outputs synthetic routes in a serialized, reaction-based notation. A diffusion transformer generates novel latents in the learned space, conditioned on pharmacophore profiles. Across analogue generation tasks for bioactive ligands, SynLaD outperforms existing baselines in synthesizable and diverse hit generation, demonstrating that a single model can produce shape-aligned molecules with feasible synthesis plans.

</details>


### [138] [Muon as a Residual Connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01124)
*Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Muon优化器可理解为训练过程中的隐式残差连接，通过正交化更新牺牲即时梯度保真度以保留下游层表征，在线性优化设置中验证了这一权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon作为大规模神经网络训练的高效优化器，其经验成功缺乏统一的机制性解释，现有解释视角分散。

Method: 提出机制性解释，将Muon视为隐式残差连接；分析正交化更新在即时梯度保真度与下游层表征保留之间的权衡；在受控线性优化设置中研究该权衡。

Result: 实验表明Muon学习的表征虽较慢拟合局部目标，但更易被下游层利用；为优化器设计提供平衡局部下降与下游可用性的视角。

Conclusion: Muon的成功归因于其隐式残差连接特性，该工作为优化器设计提供了新的理论视角，强调局部优化与下游表征保留的平衡。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as one of the most effective optimizers for training large neural networks, yet its empirical success has been explained from several different perspectives. In this paper, we propose a simple mechanistic interpretation: Muon can be understood as an implicit residual connection during training. Specifically, orthogonalizing the update can sacrifice some immediate gradient fidelity while improving representation preservation for downstream layers. We study this trade-off in controlled linear optimization settings, where Muon can learn representations that are slower to fit a local target but easier for downstream layers to exploit. Our results suggest a conceptual explanation for Muon and a design perspective for optimizers that balance local descent with downstream usability.

</details>


### [139] [ZO-Act: Efficient Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning via One-Shot Activation-Informed Low-Rank Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01125)
*Xun Dong,Yibo Xu,Naigang Wang,Xin Li,Penghang Yin,Zi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZO-Act是一种基于激活信息的零阶优化微调方法，通过将扰动限制在由输入激活导出的固定低秩子空间中，仅优化轻量级系数矩阵，显著降低了扰动维度和方差，兼容Adam等动量优化器，并天然支持量化LLM微调。实验在Llama-3-8B、OPT-13B和INT4量化模型上均优于现有ZO微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶(ZO)优化方法在无法使用反向传播或显存受限时微调大语言模型，但通常对全量模型权重或随机构造的低维子空间进行扰动，导致梯度估计方差大、性能受限。需要一种能降低扰动维度、减少方差、兼容主流优化器并支持量化模型的ZO微调方法。

Method: ZO-Act在初始化时为每个线性层计算一次小规模激活基，将扰动限制在该固定低秩子空间内，仅优化轻量级系数矩阵。通过前向传播损失评估进行零阶优化，显式暴露可训练变量，兼容Adam等动量优化器，且保持低比特权重冻结，天然支持量化LLM微调。理论分析表明该方法降低了方差相关收敛项和有限差分误差，代价是可控的子空间近似偏差，但可由LLM激活和梯度的低秩结构缓解。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B、OPT-13B和INT4量化的Llama-3-8B上进行语言理解、问答和常识推理任务实验，ZO-Act在多个强ZO微调基线上取得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: ZO-Act通过激活引导的低秩子空间扰动，有效解决了零阶优化中扰动维度高、方差大、不兼容动量优化器及量化模型微调困难的问题，为大模型零阶微调提供了实用且理论有保证的新范式。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables fine-tuning large language models when backpropagation is unavailable or memory-prohibitive, but existing methods often perturb full model weights or randomly constructed low-dimensional subspaces, yielding high-variance estimates and limited performance. We propose ZO-Act, an activation-informed ZO fine-tuning method that restricts perturbations to a fixed low-rank subspace derived from input activations. For each linear layer, ZO-Act computes a small activation basis once at initialization and optimizes only lightweight coefficient matrices using forward-only loss evaluations. This reduces the effective perturbation dimension, exposes explicit trainable variables compatible with momentum-based optimizers such as Adam, and naturally supports quantized LLM fine-tuning by keeping low-bit weights frozen. We analyze ZO-Act as zeroth-order optimization over a restricted coefficient space and show that perturbing the low-dimensional coefficients reduces both the variance-dependent convergence term and the finite-difference error of the ZO estimator, at the cost of a controlled subspace approximation bias that is mitigated by the low-rank structure of LLM activations and gradients. Experiments on Llama-3-8B, OPT-13B, and INT4 Llama-3-8B show consistent gains over strong ZO fine-tuning baselines across language understanding, question answering, and commonsense reasoning.

</details>


### [140] [GAIA: Geometry-Adaptive Operator Learning for Forward and Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01128)
*Meenakshi Krishnan,Pranav Pulijala,Ke Chen,Haizhao Yang,Ramani Duraiswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAIA 是一种几何自适应积分自编码器，通过几何标记和交叉注意力实现任意几何下的前向、边界值和逆问题的统一算子学习，无需重新训练或迭代优化，在多个基准测试中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何自适应神经算子主要针对输入输出共享同一空间域的前向问题，难以处理输入输出位于不同域的边界值问题（BVP）和逆问题。

Method: 提出几何自适应积分自编码器（GAIA），将域边界和内部分布编码为几何标记，通过交叉注意力条件化积分变换层，使核函数局部自适应几何特征，实现前向（含BVP）和逆问题的单一架构一遍求解。

Result: 在七个2D/3D基准（含四个新/扩展的逆问题和BVP基准）上评估，GAIA在所有逆问题和BVP任务上建立新SOTA：气动外形流场重构中位相对L2误差降低64%，EIT降低27%，在MCB所有形状类别上超越基线；前向问题上与专用求解器竞争，且在Transformer基线退化的点分辨率下保持稳定精度。

Conclusion: GAIA 为任意几何下的 PDE 算子学习提供了统一、高效且精度稳健的框架，显著推进了边界值问题和逆问题的摊销求解能力。

Abstract: Operator learning for partial differential equations (PDEs) on arbitrary geometries builds fast neural surrogates for large-scale simulation. Although recent geometry-adaptive neural operators have made substantial progress, they are mainly designed for forward problems in which inputs and outputs share the same spatial domain. This limits their applicability for boundary value problems (BVPs) and inverse problems, where inputs and outputs may live on different domains. We introduce the Geometry-Adaptive Integral Autoencoder (GAIA), an operator learning model that encodes the domain boundary and the interior field distribution into geometry tokens, and conditions integral transform layers on these tokens via cross-attention, allowing the kernel to adapt locally to geometric features. This yields a single architecture for forward (including BVPs) and inverse problems on arbitrary domains in one pass, without retraining, iterative optimization, or graph construction. We evaluate GAIA on seven 2D and 3D benchmarks, four of which are new or substantially extended benchmarks for inverse problems and BVP: electrical impedance tomography, optical tomography, 3D Darcy flow on varying geometries, and a modified setting of Poisson BVP on mechanical components benchmark (MCB). GAIA sets new state-of-the-art results on every inverse and BVP task, reducing median relative $L^2$ error by 64% on airfoil flow reconstruction and 27% on EIT relative to the next best amortized method, and outperforming all baselines on every shape category of MCB. On other forward problems, GAIA is competitive with specialized solvers while maintaining stable accuracy across point resolutions on which transformer-based baselines degrade.

</details>


### [141] [Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps for Online Feedback-Driven Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01144)
*Binglin Ji,Anindya Sarkar,Hengchang Lu,Jens Sjölund,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IMPFM框架，通过交互式多粒子流映射实现样本高效的在线反馈驱动搜索，解决偏好未知且需通过序列反馈揭示时的广域探索问题，理论上证明其能收敛到KL倾斜目标分布，防止模式崩塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在奖励对齐中主要擅长窄分布区域的局部探索，但当偏好先验未知、仅通过序列反馈揭示时，需要广域探索以发现高效用区域，现有方法难以应对。

Method: 提出Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps (IMPFM)框架：利用流映射实现粒子间后验样本共享，在每次重采样步骤用集合整体后验样本校正单粒子漂移；引入基于多粒子交互的探索-利用重加权机制，显式保持结构多样性并克服标准SMC采样器的权重退化；理论证明该框架产生多粒子交互感知的Feynman-Kac校正器，逐步引导系统向KL倾斜目标分布收敛。

Result: 在多样搜索与对齐任务上的大量实验与消融研究证实，IMPFM优于现有基线，实现了全局探索并有效缓解奖励过度优化和模式崩塌。

Conclusion: IMPFM通过原理化的多粒子交互动力学与流映射机制，在偏好未知的序列反馈设定下实现了样本高效的广域探索，为在线奖励对齐提供了理论保证与实证有效的新范式。

Abstract: While generative models have enabled training-free reward alignment, current methods typically excel in local exploration within narrow regions of the underlying distribution. These approaches struggle when preferences are unknown a priori and only revealed through sequential feedback-a scenario demanding broad exploration to uncover high-utility regions. To address this, we propose Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps (IMPFM), a framework for sample-efficient online feedback-driven search. IMPFM progressively transports a group of interactive particles toward the target distribution, maintaining the broad coverage essential for heterogeneous preference alignment. IMPFM introduces a principled and efficient posterior sample sharing mechanism across particles powered by flow maps. By correcting individual particle drift with the collective posterior samples of the entire ensemble at each resampling step, the framework maximizes sample utility to enable global exploration while actively mitigating reward over-optimization, typical of standard control frameworks. Paired with a principled exploration-exploitation reweighting mechanism involving multi-particle interaction, this sequentially corrected multi-particle dynamics explicitly preserves structural diversity and overcomes the weight degeneracy inherent to standard SMC samplers. Crucially, we prove that the resulting sampling framework yields a multi-particle interaction-aware Feynman-Kac corrector that progressively steers the multi-particle system toward a KL-tilted target distribution, facilitating global exploration and preventing mode collapse. Extensive empirical evaluations and rigorous ablations across diverse search and alignment tasks confirm the efficacy of IMPFM over existing baselines.

</details>


### [142] [A Lightweight Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Multivariate Time Series using Hierarchical-JEPA on ECG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01145)
*Siwon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ER-JEPA，一种用于多变量时间序列（12导联心电图）的轻量级自监督学习框架，采用两阶层 JEPA 结构与 ViT 主干，在约 18 万条 10 秒记录上预训练，在 ST-MEM 基准上达到最优下游性能，且计算快、资源占用少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学数据分析常面临目标数据集有限、但存在大量未标注的通用分布数据的场景，自监督学习（SSL）能有效利用大规模无标签数据，成为心电图（ECG）分析的热门选择。

Method: 提出 Event Reconstruction Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (ER-JEPA)，包含：(1) 两阶段结构，先为每个时间区间构建表示，再将这些表示作为单变量时间序列处理；(2) 两个 Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) 的层级化集成，形成 Hierarchical JEPA (H-JEPA)；(3) 采用 Vision Transformer (ViT) 作为主干网络。

Result: 在约 18 万条 10 秒 ECG 记录上预训练后，模型在 ST-MEM 基准上取得最先进的下游任务性能，同时实现快速计算和极低资源消耗。

Conclusion: 成功将 H-JEPA 应用于 12 导联 ECG 多变量时间序列，并对预训练阶段层级表示的敏感性进行了分析，验证了该框架的有效性与高效性。

Abstract: Data analysis in the medical domain often encounters scenarios involving a limited target dataset and a large, unannotated dataset with a general distribution. Under such circumstances, self-supervised learning (SSL) methods are highly effective for utilizing large datasets, making them a popular choice for electrocardiogram (ECG) analysis. This work presents the Event Reconstruction Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (ER-JEPA), a lightweight SSL framework for multivariate time series, whose name and two-fold hierarchical structure are inspired by the diagnostic approach of cardiologists. At its core, ER-JEPA features: (1) a two-stage structure that constructs representations for each time interval and subsequently processes these representations as a univariate time series, (2) the hierarchical integration of two Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), and (3) a Vision Transformer (ViT) backbone. The structural concatenation of two JEPAs categorizes the model as a Hierarchical JEPA (H-JEPA), designed to encode multiple levels of abstract representations for enhanced prediction on complex tasks. This study reports a successful application of H-JEPA to 12-lead ECG data as a multivariate time series alongside an analysis of the sensitivity of hierarchical representation during the pretraining stage. Pretrained on approximately 180,000 10-second recordings, the model achieves state-of-the-art downstream performance on the ST-MEM benchmark, with rapid computation and minimal resource usage.

</details>


### [143] [QuasiMoTTo: Quasi-Monte Carlo Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01179)
*Michael Y. Li,Anthony Zhan,Kanishk Gandhi,Noah D. Goodman,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 QuasiMoTTo，利用拟蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法生成相关但边缘分布保持不变的采样，替代传统的独立同分布（i.i.d.）采样，显著提升推理计算与强化学习的样本效率。在四个推理基准上，QuasiMoTTo 仅需 25%-47% 的样本即可达到 i.i.d. 采样的 pass@k 性能；在 GRPO 策略梯度训练中，仅需 50% 的训练步数即可达到同等效果。核心优势在于更高的输出空间覆盖率，带来更强的学习信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模并行采样是提升语言模型能力的有效手段，但默认的独立采样会产生大量冗余解，浪费推理算力。虽然独立性使并行采样易于扩展，但这种权衡并非根本限制：存在丰富的相关采样设计空间，可在完全并行的前提下生成精确的相关样本。本文旨在探索该设计空间，以提升推理计算扩展和强化学习的样本效率。

Method: 提出 QuasiMoTTo：将自回归采样重参数化为逆累积分布函数（inverse-CDF）采样，并使用拟蒙特卡洛（QMC）序列绘制底层均匀变量。QMC 比 i.i.d. 均匀变量更均匀地覆盖单位超立方体，从而使生成的样本在输出空间覆盖更广、冗余更低。尽管批次内样本相关，但每个样本的边缘分布仍严格服从语言模型分布，因此可直接用于策略梯度训练（如 GRPO）。为评估相关采样器，开发了无偏 bootstrap 估计器以替代标准 pass@k 估计器。

Result: 在四个推理基准上，QuasiMoTTo 仅需 i.i.d. 采样 25%-47% 的样本数即可达到相同的 pass@k 准确率，且经常达到任意保边缘分布采样器的 pass@k 理论上界。在 GRPO 策略梯度强化学习中，QuasiMoTTo 仅需 50% 的训练步数即可匹配 i.i.d. 基线性能。增益来源于更高的输出空间覆盖率，使每个批次提供更强的学习信号。

Conclusion: QuasiMoTTo 证明了通过精心设计的相关采样（基于 QMC 的逆 CDF 重参数化），可以在不改变边缘分布的前提下，大幅降低推理计算和强化学习训练所需的样本量。该方法作为即插即用的替代方案，打破了“独立采样易于扩展但冗余高”的固有认知，为大规模语言模型推理和训练提供了更高效的采样范式。

Abstract: Scaling inference compute, by generating many parallel attempts per problem, is a costly but reliable lever for improving language model capabilities. By default these attempts are generated independently, wasting inference compute on redundant solutions. This waste seems unavoidable. After all, independence is what makes parallel sampling trivial to scale. However, this tradeoff is not fundamental: there is a rich design space of samplers that generate correlated but exact samples entirely in parallel. We explore this design space as an avenue for improving sample efficiency in scaling inference compute and reinforcement learning (RL). Concretely, we introduce QuasiMoTTo, which uses correlated samples as a drop-in replacement for i.i.d. samples. To generate these samples, QuasiMoTTo uses a reparameterization of autoregressive sampling as inverse-CDF sampling and draws the underlying uniforms with quasi-Monte Carlo (QMC); because QMC spreads the uniforms out more evenly than i.i.d., the resulting samples cover the output space with far less redundancy. Even though the batch is correlated, each sample is marginally distributed according to the language model, so we can use the batch for policy-gradient training. Our empirical analysis focuses on understanding how efficiently QuasiMoTTo can turn compute into performance. To evaluate correlated samplers, whose dependence breaks standard pass@k estimators, we first develop an unbiased bootstrap estimator. Across four reasoning benchmarks, QuasiMoTTo matches i.i.d. pass@k accuracy with 25-47% fewer samples. Strikingly, QuasiMoTTo often saturates an upper bound on pass@k that holds for any marginal-preserving sampler. We also apply QuasiMoTTo to policy-gradient RL (GRPO) where it matches i.i.d. performance with 50% fewer training steps. These gains come from higher coverage, which yields a stronger learning signal per batch.

</details>


### [144] [Efficient Compression of Structured and Unstructured Volumes via Learned 3D Gaussian Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01164)
*Landon Dyken,Sharmistha Chakrabarti,Nathan Debardeleben,Steve Petruzza,Qi Wu,Will Usher,Sidharth Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Recent work has shown that implicit neural representations (INRs) can be trained to effectively compress structured and unstructured volume data, allowing for direct data querying with a reduced memory footprint. However, as existing INRs for unstructured volumes do not encode geometry, they require partial mesh storage for later sampling, limiting achievable compression. At the same time, novel view synthesis methods have shown that explicit collections of 3D Gaussians can be used to accurately visualize volume data. In this work, we introduce an explicit model for volume data compression based on 3D Gaussian primitives. We reinterpret collections of 3D Gaussians as an explicit representation of a scalar field and use a sampling strategy that reconstructs scalar values at spatial locations through weighted aggregation of intersecting Gaussians. We develop optimized CUDA-accelerated pipelines for structured and unstructured model sampling, loss functions that encourage accurate domain encoding by our models, and a novel sampling-error based densification strategy. Our explicit formulation naturally encodes domain geometry, eliminating the need for mesh storage in unstructured volumes and introducing significantly higher compression opportunities. Compared to existing INRs, we demonstrate that our explicit model achieves competitive reconstruction quality with significant training speedups on structured volumes, while markedly outperforming in all metrics on unstructured volumes.

</details>


### [145] [Right in the Right Way: LM Training with Verifiable Rewards and Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01181)
*Mehul Damani,Isha Puri,Idan Shenfeld,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种对抗性生成器-判别器框架，将可验证奖励与从人类演示中学习的信号结合，同时优化任务准确性和类人输出特性，解决RLVR中多样性崩塌、不自然输出和奖励欺骗等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅优化可客观评分的指标，忽视主观、非可验证的人类输出特性（如风格、结构），导致多样性崩塌、响应不自然、奖励欺骗等失败模式。

Method: 采用对抗性生成器-判别器框架：生成器通过强化学习最大化任务准确性和来自判别器的对抗奖励；判别器与生成器策略联合训练，学习区分人类编写输出与模型生成输出，作为人类输出分布的学习代理，为难以形式化为标量奖励的生成方面提供反馈。

Result: 在多个领域（Bug修复、开放式生成）实验表明，该方法持续改善非可验证属性同时保持RLVR的准确性增益。Bug修复中显著降低编辑距离且性能相当；故事生成中显著提高胜率并产生更多样、更人类化的故事；奖励欺骗基准中几乎消除模型不当行为同时保持高分。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了强化学习与监督微调的差距，提供了一条可扩展的路径，联合优化任务的可验证和不可验证属性。

Abstract: RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for training LMs on tasks with well-defined success metrics, such as code generation and mathematical reasoning. However, current RLVR methods optimize only what can be objectively scored, often neglecting subjective, non-verifiable aspects of human-like outputs, such as style and structure. This limitation leads to well-documented failure modes such as diversity collapse, unnatural-sounding responses, and reward hacking. We propose an adversarial generator-discriminator framework that augments verifiable rewards with a learned signal from human demonstrations. A generator model is trained using RL to maximize both task accuracy and an adversarial reward derived from a discriminator. The discriminator, trained alongside the generator policy, learns to distinguish human-written outputs from model-generated ones. The discriminator serves as a learned proxy for the human output distribution, providing feedback on aspects of generation that are difficult to formalize as scalar rewards. Across diverse domains, including bug fixing and open-ended generation, our approach consistently improves non-verifiable properties while preserving the accuracy gains of RLVR. In bug fixing, our method produces solutions with significantly lower edit distance compared to RLVR baselines while matching end performance. In story generation, our method significantly improves win rate while producing stories that are diverse and more human-like. And in a simple reward hacking benchmark, our method nearly eliminates model misbehavior while maintaining high benchmark scores. Together, these results show that our approach bridges RL and SFT, offering a scalable path toward jointly optimizing the verifiable and non-verifiable properties of a task.

</details>


### [146] [Decision-Aware Training for Sample-Based Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01171)
*Kornelius Raeth,Nicole Ludwig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出决策感知训练框架，将能量分数与可微决策损失结合，使生成模型在高风险决策场景下针对成本敏感区域实现针对性改进，同时保留完整概率预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于样本的生成模型常用严格合适评分规则（如能量分数）训练，其训练信号按数据密度分配，忽略决策者的成本结构，导致预测错误在下游决策中代价高昂的区域未得到足够关注。

Method: 在能量分数目标中引入可微决策损失，直接惩罚基于模型预测采取行动所产生的决策成本；该组合损失在理论上成立，因为决策损失本身也是合适评分规则。

Result: 在一个合成任务和两个真实任务上验证：方法在成本敏感区域实现针对性性能提升，同时保持完整的概率预测质量。

Conclusion: 决策感知训练有效将决策成本纳入生成模型训练目标，显著提升高风险决策场景下的预测效用，且不损害概率预测的完整性。

Abstract: Sample-based generative models are increasingly used for probabilistic forecasting in high-stakes decision settings, yet their training objectives are blind to the decision maker's cost structure. These models are commonly trained with strictly proper scoring rules, such as the energy score, which allocate their training signal in proportion to data density, with no awareness of where forecast errors are most costly for downstream decisions. We therefore propose decision-aware training for sample-based generative models, augmenting the energy score objective with a differentiable decision loss that directly penalises the cost incurred by acting on the model's forecast. This combined loss is theoretically grounded, as the decision loss is itself a proper scoring rule. We validate our method on one synthetic and two real-world tasks, showing targeted improvements in cost-sensitive regions while retaining full probabilistic forecasts.

</details>


### [147] [Is One Layer Enough? Training A Single Transformer Layer Can Match Full-Parameter RL Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01232)
*Zijian Zhang,Rizhen Hu,Athanasios Glentis,Dawei Li,Chung-Yiu Yau,Hongzhou Lin,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过系统性的逐层研究发现，大语言模型强化学习后训练的收益高度集中在Transformer中间的少数层（甚至单层），训练单层即可恢复大部分甚至超越全参数RL的增益，且这一层级贡献模式在不同模型、算法、任务间高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练方法通常均匀更新所有参数，隐含假设每层贡献相似。论文旨在挑战这一假设，量化各Transformer层对RL收益的实际贡献，揭示层级分布规律。

Method: 引入“层贡献”指标，度量单独训练某层能恢复全参数RL改进的比例。在Qwen3、Qwen2.5两个模型家族共七个模型上，使用GRPO、GiGPO、Dr. GRPO三种RL算法，跨数学推理、代码生成、智能体决策等任务进行系统实验。

Result: 单层训练即可恢复大部分甚至超过全参数RL的增益；RL收益高度集中在Transformer中间层，输入/输出端层贡献极低；层级贡献排名在不同数据集、任务、模型家族和RL算法间强相关。

Conclusion: RL适应性在Transformer层间分布极不均匀，中间层为关键。这为参数高效的RL后训练提供了理论依据，未来可仅针对高贡献层进行训练，大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central component of post-training large language models (LLMs), yet little is understood about how RL adaptation is distributed across transformer layers. Existing approaches typically update all model parameters uniformly, implicitly assuming that every layer contributes similarly to the gains obtained during RL post-training. In this work, we challenge this assumption through a systematic layer-wise study of RL training. Surprisingly, we find that training a single transformer layer can recover most of the gains achieved by full-parameter RL training, and in some cases even surpass it. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the quantity layer contribution, which measures the fraction of full RL improvement recovered by training a layer in isolation. Across seven models spanning two model families (Qwen3, Qwen2.5), three RL algorithms (GRPO, GiGPO, Dr. GRPO), and multiple task domains including mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic decision-making, we observe a remarkably stable pattern: RL gains are highly concentrated in a small subset of, and in many cases even a single, transformer layers. More strikingly, the same structural pattern consistently emerges: high-contribution layers concentrate in the middle of the transformer stack, while layers near the input and output ends contribute substantially less. The resulting layer rankings remain strongly correlated across datasets, tasks, model families, and RL algorithms.

</details>


### [148] [Neural Certificate Pricing for Combinatorial Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01185)
*Jingyi Chen,Xinyuan Zhang,Xinwu Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 神经证书定价(NCP)利用组合优化中验证与搜索的不对称性，通过无监督学习预测对偶价格并结构化恢复原始边际，在多类CO问题上超越或匹配SOTA基线且计算更高效，展现更强的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化(CO)问题因离散结构的指数级搜索空间而困难，验证最优性需枚举指数级候选，但给定解后其结构可行性可在多项式时间内验证。这种验证与搜索的不对称性为设计高效学习方法提供了切入点。

Method: 提出神经证书定价(NCP)框架：神经网络预测证书层面的对偶价格，结构化恢复层构造诱导的原始边际。可视为分摊分离——不枚举违反不等式，而是学习进入恢复的残差价格聚合效应。当证书一致性条件成立时，恢复的边际全局可行；局部理论表明预测价格的一阶误差仅导致目标值的二阶损失。

Result: 在三类CO问题上，NCP要么大幅超越最先进神经基线，要么以极小计算时间匹配其性能，并展现更强的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: NCP通过无监督学习利用CO问题的验证-搜索不对称性，实现了高效、有效且泛化能力强的组合优化求解新范式。

Abstract: Combinatorial optimization (CO) problems are difficult because certifiable discrete structure induces exponential search. One needs to search over the set exponentially many candidates to certify optimality, however, the structural feasibility of a path, packing, or cover can be verified in polynomial time once supplied. In this study, we introduce Neural Certificate Pricing (NCP) that exploits this asymmetry under an unsupervised learning framework. A neural network is trained to predict certificate-level dual prices, while a structured recovery layer constructs the induced primal marginal. NCP can be viewed as amortized separation: instead of enumerating violated inequalities, it learns the residual prices through which their aggregate effect enters recovery. When the certificate-consistency condition holds, the recovered marginal is globally feasible, and a local theory shows that first-order errors in the predicted price induce only second-order loss in objective value. Across three classes of CO problems, NCP either outperforms state-of-the-art neural baselines by large margins or matches them at a fraction of the computation time, and shows stronger out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [149] [Quantum vs. Classical Machine Learning: A Unified Empirical Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01197)
*Chuanming Yu,Jiaming Liu,Zihao Ge,Xiongfei Wu,Lulu Zhu,Pengzhan Zhao,Jianjun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究对比了七组量子机器学习（QML）模型与经典基线模型在监督学习和强化学习任务上的表现，结果显示当前QML模型在预测性能、策略稳定性和训练时间上尚未超越经典模型，但在噪声过滤和假阳性控制方面展现潜力，并总结了硬件环境、训练效率和收敛稳定性等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算被视为机器学习的有前景计算范式，可能提供计算优势，但目前支持QML模型相对于经典模型的性能和优势的证据不足，亟需系统性的实证评估来填补这一空白。

Method: 开展实证研究，选取七对模型配对，涵盖监督学习和强化学习两大任务类别，系统对比QML模型与其经典对应模型在整体预测性能、策略稳定性和训练时间等维度的表现。

Result: 评估的QML模型在整体预测性能、策略稳定性和训练时间上均未超越经典基线；但QML在噪声过滤和控制假阳性方面显示出潜在优势；研究总结了QML在硬件环境适应性、训练效率和收敛稳定性方面面临的挑战。

Conclusion: 当前QML模型尚未在核心性能指标上超越经典模型，但其在特定鲁棒性任务（如噪声过滤、假阳性控制）上展现价值。研究为QML的鲁棒性改进和参数优化提供了实证基础，代码已开源。

Abstract: Quantum computing has emerged as a promising computational paradigm for machine learning (ML), with the potential to offer computational advantages over classical approaches. At this stage, the evidence supporting the performance and advantages of quantum machine learning (QML) models relative to classical models is insufficient.To address this gap, this paper presents an empirical study on the performance of QML models and their classical counterparts. We compare seven model pairs spanning supervised learning and reinforcement learning. Our results indicate that the evaluated quantum machine learning models do not yet surpass the classical baselines in overall prediction performance, policy stability, or training time. Nevertheless, QML remains a promising approach for filtering noise and controlling false positives. Our research findings summarize the challenges facing quantum machine learning across hardware environments, training efficiency, and convergence stability, providing a foundation for research into the robustness and parameter optimization of QML. This work is publicly available at https://github.com/Z-537-437/QML.

</details>


### [150] [TiRex-2: Generalizing TiRex to Multivariate Data and Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01204)
*Patrick Podest,Marco Pichler,Elias Bürger,Levente Zólyomi,Bernhard Voggenberger,Wilhelm Berghammer,Daniel Klotz,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TiRex-2是一个基于xLSTM的循环时间序列基础模型，将单变量TiRex扩展至支持过去和未来协变量的多变量预测。采用内存中心的循环设计实现常数时间复杂度，结合双向时间混合器和非对称分组注意力变量混合器，首次在保持目标变量严格因果性的同时整合未来已知协变量。提出合成耦合管道从单变量语料库生成多变量训练样本，实现零样本SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列预测具有连续流式到达、多变量联合演化、部分协变量预知等特点。现有基于Transformer的基础模型虽能捕捉跨变量依赖，但存在二次方复杂度且需全历史重计算，难以满足流式预测的实时性和可扩展性需求。

Method: 提出TiRex-2循环架构：1) 双向时间混合器捕捉时间依赖；2) 非对称分组注意力变量混合器建模跨变量交互，同时保持目标变量严格因果性并整合未来已知协变量；3) 合成耦合管道从大规模单变量语料库即时组合生成多样化多变量样本，支持可扩展多变量预训练。

Result: 在GIFT-Eval和fev-bench基准上实现零样本SOTA性能；流式推理时性能稳定，支持任意上下文长度，单patch推理成本恒定；单变量模式38.4M激活参数，多变量模式额外激活44.1M参数。

Conclusion: TiRex-2是首个兼具流式常数复杂度、多变量建模、未来协变量整合与严格因果性的时间序列基础模型。通过合成数据管道解决多变量预训练数据稀缺问题，为实际部署提供了高效可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce TiRex-2, a recurrent xLSTM-based time series foundation model that generalizes the univariate TiRex to multivariate forecasting with both past and future covariates. Real-world forecasting is inherently sequential: observations arrive continuously, variables evolve jointly, and a subset of covariates is known ahead of time. Existing Transformer-based time series foundation models capture cross-variate dependencies but incur quadratic complexity in context length and require full-history recomputation as new observations arrive. TiRex-2 addresses these limitations through a memory-centric recurrent design that operates at constant per-patch cost under streaming. The model combines a bidirectional time mixer with an asymmetric grouped-attention variate mixer, enabling the integration of future-known covariates while preserving strict causality over target variables. To our knowledge, this is the first time series foundation model that achieves this combination of properties. To support scalable multivariate pretraining, we propose a synthetic coupling pipeline that composes diverse multivariate samples on the fly from large univariate corpora. Empirically, TiRex-2 achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench, remains stable when streamed to arbitrary context lengths, and maintains constant inference cost per patch. The model uses 38.4M active parameters in univariate mode, with an additional 44.1M parameters activated for multivariate forecasting.

</details>


### [151] [Language-Critique Imitation Learning from Suboptimal Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01225)
*Chih-Han Yang,Dai-Jie Wu,Yun-Ping Huang,Ping-Chun Hsieh,Kenneth Marino,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语言评论的模仿学习框架，利用自然语言作为结构化监督信号，从次优演示中学习鲁棒策略，避免了标量信号的表达局限。该方法构建语言标签描述任务进度、识别次优行为并给出细粒度纠正指导，设计语言评论损失直接训练策略，并实例化为 LC-BC 和 LC-DP。理论上证明该目标上界专家性能差距，实验在导航、操作和游戏等连续控制任务上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从次优演示学习的方法依赖置信度估计、判别器分数或重要性权重等压缩的标量监督信号，这些信号无法显式表达任务进度、失败模式或纠正动作等中间推理，限制了反馈的表达能力。

Method: 1. 从演示构建语言标签，显式描述当前进度、识别次优行为、提供细粒度纠正指导。2. 引入语言评论损失，直接用结构化语言信号训练策略，不将其降维为标量。3. 分别在行为克隆和扩散策略上实例化，得到 LC-BC 和 LC-DP。4. 理论分析证明该目标在标准假设下上界专家性能差距。

Result: 在涵盖导航、操作、游戏的多样化连续控制任务上，LC-BC 和 LC-DP 持续优于强模仿学习和离线强化学习基线。理论结果表明所提目标上界专家性能差距。

Conclusion: 自然语言可作为一种强大且结构化的监督形式，从次优数据中学习鲁棒策略，语言评论框架有效克服了标量监督信号的表达局限。

Abstract: Prior work on imitation learning from suboptimal demonstrations typically relies on compressed supervision signals such as confidence estimates, discriminator scores, or importance weights. These scalar signals are inherently limited, as they cannot explicitly express intermediate reasoning about task progress, failure modes, or corrective actions. We propose a language-critique framework for imitation learning from suboptimal demonstrations that instead leverages natural language as a structured supervision signal, avoiding the collapse of expressive feedback into scalars. Our method first constructs language labels from demonstrations that explicitly describe current progress, identify suboptimal behaviors, and provide fine-grained corrective guidance. We then introduce a language-critique loss that directly trains policies using these structured signals without reducing them to scalars, and instantiate it for both behavior cloning and diffusion policies, yielding LC-BC and LC-DP. We further provide a theoretical result showing that the proposed objective upper-bounds the expert performance gap under standard assumptions. Empirically, we evaluate on diverse continuous control tasks spanning navigation, manipulation, and gameplay, where our methods consistently outperform strong imitation learning and offline reinforcement learning baselines. These results demonstrate that language can serve as a powerful and structured form of supervision for learning robust policies from suboptimal data.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [From "Strings" to "Things" for Personal Knowledge Graphs: Evaluating LLM Triple Extraction for Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00003)
*Abhirup Dasgupta,Fernando Spadea,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复现的管道，利用轻量级大语言模型（Qwen和Gemma）从对话数据中提取符合RDF标准且链接到Wikidata标识符的用户偏好三元组，用于构建个人知识图谱（PKG），并评估了三元组抽取保真度及下游推荐任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人知识图谱（PKG）为用户偏好建模提供了隐私保护框架，但从非结构化、去中心化的对话数据构建PKG仍具挑战。需要弥合对话“字符串”与语义“实体”之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用轻量级大语言模型（Qwen和Gemma系列）从对话数据中抽取RDF合规的三元组并链接至Wikidata标识符，构建PKG。评估维度包括语义抽取保真度及生成图谱在下游推荐任务中的效用。

Result: 部分模型在三元组抽取任务上表现优异，且其下游推荐性能与抽取性能呈正比关系，即抽取质量越高，推荐效果越好。

Conclusion: 基于轻量级LLM的可复现管道能有效从对话数据构建PKG，实现隐私保护下的用户偏好建模，且模型抽取性能直接影响下游推荐效果，验证了该方法的实用性。

Abstract: Personal Knowledge Graphs (PKGs) offer a privacy-preserving framework for modeling user preferences, yet constructing them from unstructured, decentralized conversational data remains a challenge. This paper bridges the gap between conversational "strings" and semantic "things" by presenting a reproducible pipeline for extracting structured user-preference triples using lightweight Large Language Models. We evaluate Qwen- and Gemma-based models on their ability to extract RDF-compliant triples linked to Wikidata identifiers from conversational data for PKG construction. Our evaluation assesses both the semantic extraction fidelity and the utility of the resulting graphs in a downstream recommendation task. We found that certain models performed well and had proportionally high downstream performance relative to their triple extraction performance.

</details>


### [153] [Why Advanced Encoders Lag on Sparse Retrieval? The Answer and an Approach to Bridging Vocabulary Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00004)
*Zhichao Geng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文揭示了现代基础模型（如ModernBERT）在稀疏检索中落后于BERT-base的根本原因是“词汇鸿沟”：现代分词器使用区分大小写的原始词表导致语义单元映射为冗余表面形式，浪费模型容量。提出词汇迁移（VT）框架，通过语义初始化和激活势校准（APC）将先进编码器迁移到稀疏友好的归一化词表，使ModernBERT在BEIR基准上达到SOTA（52.4 nDCG，+4.7），并挽救了RoBERTa-large等失效模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基础模型在密集检索中表现优异，但在学习稀疏检索（LSR）中反而不如老旧的BERT-base。作者识别出根本原因是词汇鸿沟：现代分词器采用面向无损重建的区分大小写原始词表，将单一语义单元映射为冗余的表面形式，引入形态噪声，阻碍词汇匹配。理论分析表明，适当的词汇粗粒度化可在保持语义完整性的前提下收紧泛化界。

Method: 提出模型无关的词汇迁移（VT）框架，以最小计算成本将先进编码器迁移到稀疏友好的归一化词表。核心组件包括：1) 基于空间拓扑的语义初始化，保持几何结构；2) 激活势校准（APC）机制，将预训练流形与稀疏性约束对齐，防止标准微调中的死神经元和密集坍缩问题。

Result: VT普遍有效：ModernBERT在BEIR基准达到52.4 nDCG（+4.7提升），实现SOTA；挽救了RoBERTa-large等失效模型；无缝泛化到无推理架构和专业领域。实验确认性能滞后源于词汇不匹配而非架构缺陷。

Conclusion: 先进模型在稀疏检索中的劣势并非架构缺陷，而是可解决的词汇不匹配问题。通过词汇迁移框架，可将现代编码器适配至稀疏检索友好的词表，显著提升性能并保持模型通用性。

Abstract: While advanced foundation models like ModernBERT significantly outperform older architectures in dense retrieval, they surprisingly lag behind the aging BERT-base baseline in learned sparse retrieval (LSR). We identify the root cause as the \textit{Vocabulary Gap}: modern tokenizers utilize raw, case-sensitive vocabularies designed for lossless reconstruction, which map single semantic units to redundant surface forms, wasting model capacity on morphological noise and hindering lexical matching. We formalize this intuition through a theoretical framework, demonstrating that appropriate vocabulary coarse-graining can tighten the generalization bounds by reducing complexity of the hypothesis class, provided that semantic integrity is preserved. To resolve this, we propose \textbf{Vocabulary Transfer (VT)}, a model-agnostic framework that migrates advanced encoders to sparse-friendly, normalized vocabularies with minimal computational cost.
  VT utilizes a novel \textbf{Semantic Initialization} via spatial topology to preserve geometric structure and an \textbf{Activation Potential Calibration (APC)} mechanism to align pre-trained manifolds with sparsity constraints, preventing the dead neuron and dense collapse observed in standard fine-tuning. Empirically, VT is universally effective: it enables ModernBERT to achieve state-of-the-art performance on the BEIR benchmark (\textbf{52.4} nDCG, a \textbf{+4.7} improvement), resuscitates failing models like RoBERTa-large, and generalizes seamlessly to inference-free architectures and specialized domains. These results confirm that the performance lag is not an architectural deficiency but a solvable vocabulary mismatch. We've released our code and models.\footnote{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/vocab-transfer/. All details included.}

</details>


### [154] [Topological Void Analysis A Mathematical Framework for Systematic Technical Innovation Discovery in Knowledge Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00005)
*Kris Pan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TVA是一个数学框架，通过在稠密-稀疏混合嵌入空间中定义拓扑空洞（三元组A、B、C），自动发现高维知识空间中未被探索但与目标相关的创新机会，在约14万文档上验证生成2128个候选，经专家对抗评审获得191个REVISE和1个APPROVE判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在操作系统、软硬件协同设计等稠密技术领域中识别创新切点，本质上是高维知识空间中的搜索问题。现有方法（关键词搜索、引文邻近性、人工直觉）均未形式化「同时与目标相关且不在现有技术中」的未探索区域。

Method: 提出拓扑空洞分析（TVA），在稠密-稀疏混合嵌入空间中将拓扑空洞定义为三元组(A, B, C)，需满足三条件：(i) A、B均与领域锚点C语义内聚；(ii) A、B两两相似度落在校准的边际带内——避开显而易见的组合与无关噪声；(iii) 共享稀疏词法桥接，且嵌入超球面上的测地线中点无占用。

Result: 在约14万篇索引文档上应用，TVA跨96个目标生成2128个发明候选；90%通过自动质量过滤，经四专家对抗评审得到191个REVISE和1个APPROVE判定（端到端0.05%）。两个案例研究证实框架能挖掘非显而易见的连接组织，而非仅给出显而易见的相关对。

Conclusion: TVA通过在混合嵌入空间中形式化拓扑空洞，有效识别稠密技术领域中的非显而易见创新机会，自动化候选生成与专家评审结果表明其具备实用潜力。

Abstract: Identifying where to innovate in a dense technical domain - such as operating systems or hardware/software co-design - is fundamentally a search problem in a high-dimensional knowledge space. Existing approaches rely on keyword search, citation proximity, or human intuition, none of which formalise the notion of an unexplored region that is simultaneously relevant to a target goal and absent from prior art.
  We present Topological Void Analysis (TVA), a mathematical framework that defines topological voids as triads (A, B, C) in a dense-sparse hybrid embedding space. A void requires three conditions: (i) both concepts A and B are semantically cohesive with domain anchor C; (ii) their pairwise similarity falls within a calibrated marginality band - avoiding both obvious combinations and unrelated noise; and (iii) they share a sparse lexical bridge while the geodesic midpoint on the embedding hypersphere is unoccupied.
  Applied to ~140k indexed documents, TVA generates 2,128 invention candidates across 96 targets; 90% survive automated quality filtering, yielding 191 REVISE and 1 APPROVE verdict from four-specialist adversarial review (0.05% end-to-end). Two case studies demonstrate the framework surfaces non-obvious connective tissue rather than merely obvious related pairs.

</details>


### [155] [BaRA: BFS-and-Reflection Web Data Collection Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00007)
*Soojeong Lee,Joseph Lee,Yongseong Cho,Sunjae Kim,Youngwoo Moon,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出BFS-and-Reflection Agent (BaRA)框架，结合有界广度优先搜索（BFS）遍历与基于历史的自我反思，在固定交互预算下实现站点级数据收集。实验表明BaRA在链接发现和可下载多模态提取上优于Pure LLM、SeeAct-Vision和Browser-use，尤其在有效图像和视频恢复方面提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的网页智能体虽减少了手动脚本编写，但在真实网站上常遗漏相关页面、返回不完整的多模态输出，或给出不可直接下载的媒体URL。因此需要一种在固定交互预算下能更全面、准确地进行站点级数据收集的方法。

Method: 提出BaRA框架，核心组件为有界广度优先搜索（BFS）遍历与基于历史的自我反思机制。BFS用于系统性探索网站链接结构，自我反思则利用交互历史改进决策，二者结合实现高效的站点级多模态数据收集。

Result: 在50个合成网站（含真值参考集）及3个布局复杂或动态的公共网站上评测。BaRA在链接发现和可下载多模态提取指标上均超越Pure LLM、SeeAct-Vision和Browser-use，尤其在可下载图像和视频的恢复率上增益最显著。

Conclusion: BaRA通过有界BFS遍历与历史自我反思的结合，有效解决了LLM网页智能体在真实环境下的遗漏页面、输出不完整及媒体不可下载等问题，显著提升了站点级数据收集的完整性和可用性，为自动化网页数据采集提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based web agents reduce manual scripting for web data collection, yet on live websites, they often miss relevant pages, return incomplete multimodal outputs, or return media URLs that are not directly downloadable. We present BFS-and-Reflection Agent (BaRA), a framework for site-level collection under a fixed interaction budget. The framework combines bounded breadth-first search (BFS) traversal with history-based self-reflection. We evaluate BaRA on 50 synthetic websites with ground-truth reference sets. We additionally test on three public websites with cluttered or dynamic layouts. BaRA outperforms Pure LLM, SeeAct-Vision, and Browser-use on link discovery and downloadable multimodal extraction, with the largest gains in download-valid image and video recovery. Our code is available at https://github.com/MLAI-Yonsei/BaRA-Agent.

</details>


### [156] [SchemaRAG: Dynamic Large Schema Reduction for LLM-driven Structured Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00008)
*Sin Yu Bonnie Ho,Arlie Coles,Erik Larsson,Eric Marshall,Nathan Bodenstab,Paul Vozila*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SchemaRAG是一种检索增强生成框架，用于大规模复杂schema下的结构化信息抽取，通过动态剪枝schema空间，显著提升性能并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当目标schema大且复杂时，将完整schema放入prompt会增加成本和延迟，导致lost-in-the-middle性能下降，甚至超出上下文长度限制。

Method: 提出SchemaRAG，一种RAG框架，利用schema元数据和few-shot示例动态剪枝输出schema空间，用于schema-conditioned信息抽取任务。

Result: 在医疗和电商真实数据集上评估，微F1提升高达8.8%，延迟降低47%，token成本降低48%。

Conclusion: SchemaRAG证明了在大规模schema抽取任务中的实用性。

Abstract: Extracting structured data from unstructured text using large language models (LLMs) becomes challenging when target schemas are large and complex. In such cases, including the full schema in the prompt increases cost and latency, risks lost-in-the-middle performance degradation, and can exceed context length limits. We propose SchemaRAG, a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework that dynamically prunes the output schema space for schema-conditioned information extraction tasks by leveraging schema metadata and few-shot examples when available. We evaluate SchemaRAG on real-world healthcare and e-commerce datasets. Our results show that SchemaRAG can achieve up to an 8.8% increase in micro-F1, a 47% reduction in latency, and a 48% reduction in token costs, demonstrating its practicality for large-schema extraction.

</details>


### [157] [Prompt Optimization for User Simulation in Conversational Recommender Systems: A Multi-Objective Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00010)
*Nipun B Nair,Tongtong Wu,Weiqing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动优化提示词的框架，用于对话推荐系统(CRS)中的大语言模型(LLM)用户模拟器，解决了现有方法存在的系统性正向偏差、数据泄露、行为多样性有限以及依赖脆弱的人工提示工程等问题，实验证明该框架在多种提示设置下均能实现与人类交互模式更好的行为对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话推荐系统(CRS)作为下一代智能推荐系统的核心组件，允许用户主动引出偏好、澄清意图并实时调整推荐。然而，CRS领域面临两大关键障碍：评估和训练数据获取。通过真人研究评估CRS比传统推荐系统更关键，但成本高昂且耗时；同时由于隐私担忧，CRS交互数据难以获取用于模型训练。基于LLM的用户模拟器通过生成合成用户交互在解决这两个挑战方面显示出前景，但现有方法存在系统性正向偏差、数据泄露、行为多样性有限等问题，且依赖需要大量领域专业知识的脆弱人工提示工程。

Method: 提出一个自动优化提示词的框架，用于CRS中的LLM基础用户模拟器，同时缓解系统性正向偏差、数据泄露、行为多样性有限以及脆弱的人工提示工程依赖等问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，与基线方法相比，所提出的框架在多样化的提示设置下实现了与人类交互模式更好的行为对齐。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自动提示优化框架有效解决了CRS评估和训练中的关键挑战，通过自动优化LLM用户模拟器的提示词，显著改善了与人类交互行为的对齐程度，为CRS的评估和训练数据生成提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems (CRSs) are a core component of next-generation intelligent recommender systems because they enable users to actively elicit preferences, clarify intentions, and adapt recommendations in real time. However, there are two key obstacles in the CRS domain: evaluation and access to training data. Evaluating CRSs through real human studies is more critical than for traditional recommender systems, yet such studies are both costly and time-consuming. Moreover, CRS interaction data are often difficult to obtain for model training due to privacy concerns. Large language model (LLM)-based user simulators have shown promise in addressing both challenges by generating synthetic user interactions for evaluation and training. However, existing approaches suffer from systematic positive bias, data leakage, and limited behavioral diversity, and they rely on brittle manual prompt engineering that requires extensive domain expertise. In this paper, we propose a framework to automatically optimize prompts for LLM-based user simulators in CRSs, simultaneously mitigating these issues. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves improved behavioral alignment with human interaction patterns compared to baseline methods across diverse prompt settings.

</details>


### [158] [SkillSelect-Serve: Budget-Controllable and QoS-Aware Skill Service Recommendation and Composition for Small LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00011)
*Jingyuan Zheng,Dongjing Wang,Xin Zhang,Butian Huang,Haiping Zhang,Dongjin Yu,Shuguang Deng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSelect-Serve框架，将智能体技能选择建模为技能服务推荐与组合问题，通过结构化技能服务表示、微代理需求规划器、双粒度效用建模，在预算可控和QoS感知下提升技能检索与组合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体技能库复用方法将技能视为可检索文档，仅返回固定top-k列表，缺乏预算控制、QoS感知及技能间依赖与组合优化，无法满足复杂任务对技能组合的覆盖、冗余、成本、风险权衡需求。

Method: 1) 将原始技能结构化为包含功能描述、依赖、上下文成本、风险、QoS属性的技能服务；2) 本地微代理需求规划器将自然语言任务转为结构化服务需求；3) 共享发现主干从大型注册表检索候选服务；4) 双粒度效用建模：技能级边际适配度估计与包级校准，兼顾覆盖、冗余、成本、风险权衡。

Result: 在35,353个技能和586个任务查询上实验，SkillSelect-Serve在相同预算下持续提升包级召回率和平均效用，优于固定top-k检索基线。

Conclusion: SkillSelect-Serve通过将技能选择重构为服务推荐与组合问题，引入结构化表示、需求规划与双粒度效用建模，有效解决了预算约束下的技能组合优化，为LLM智能体技能库基础设施提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Reusable skill libraries are becoming important infrastructure for large language model (LLM) agents, yet existing selection methods often treat skills as retrievable documents and return fixed top-k lists. This paper presents SkillSelect-Serve, a budget-controllable and QoS-aware framework that formulates agent skill selection as Skill Service Recommendation and Composition. SkillSelect-Serve represents raw skills as structured Skill Services with functional descriptions, dependencies, context cost, risk, and QoS-related attributes.
