A local Micro-Agent Requirement Planner converts natural-language tasks into structured service requirements, while a shared discovery backbone retrieves candidate services from a large registry. The framework then performs dual-granularity utility modeling with skill-level marginal suitability estimation and bundle-level calibration for coverage, redundancy, cost, and risk trade-offs. Experiments on 35,353 skills and 586 task queries show that SkillSelect-Serve consistently improves same-budget bundle recall and mean utility over fixed top-k retrieval baselines.

</details>


### [159] [PRA-RAG: Provably Robust Aggregation in Retrieval-Augmented Generation against Retrieval Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00012)
*Xue Tan,Yi Zheng,Chang Huo,Yunruo Zhang,Yu Liu,Hao Luan,Zhuyang Yu,Xiaoyan Sun,Ping Chen,Jun Dai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PRA-RAG，一种可证明鲁棒的检索聚合算法，通过在嵌入空间利用几何结构识别鲁棒子集并聚合表示，有效防御检索文本投毒攻击，实验显示攻击成功率降至1%且保持71%准确率，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG虽能缓解大语言模型知识局限，但易受检索文本投毒攻击误导输出；现有防御缺乏理论鲁棒性保证，且在模型对检索内容知识有限时表现不可靠。

Method: PRA-RAG采样多组检索文本组合，利用嵌入空间几何结构识别鲁棒子集，由此导出稳定聚合表示；提供被毒内容最大影响的理论界限，建立RAG鲁棒性的定量度量。

Result: 跨多基准和RAG架构实验表明，PRA-RAG将攻击成功率降至最低1%，同时保持71%准确率，显著超越代表性最先进方法。

Conclusion: PRA-RAG通过可证明鲁棒的聚合机制有效防御检索投毒攻击，兼顾理论保证与实用性能，为RAG安全性提供了新范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances Large Language Models (LLMs) by incorporating external knowledge, effectively mitigating their inherent knowledge limitations. However, RAG remains vulnerable to poisoning attacks that manipulate retrieved texts to mislead model outputs. Existing defense mechanisms often lack theoretical robustness guarantees and perform unreliably when the LLM has limited knowledge of the retrieved content. In this work, we propose PRA-RAG, a provably robust retrieval aggregation algorithm designed to defend against poisoning attacks on retrieved texts. PRA-RAG samples multiple combinations of retrieved texts and utilizes geometric structures in the embedding space to identify a robust subset, from which a stable aggregated representation is derived. We provide theoretical bounds on the maximum impact of poisoned retrieved content and establish a quantitative measure of RAG's robustness. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and RAG architectures demonstrate that PRA-RAG reduces the attack success rate to as low as 1% while maintaining an accuracy of 71%, significantly outperforming representative state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [160] [GRACE-RAG: Governed Retrieval Architecture for Canonical Evidence Synthesis, Enabling Lightweight Deployment in Closed-Domain Institutional Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00013)
*Asit Desai,Aman Kumar,Prashant Devadiga*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GRACE-RAG，一种检索治理的图增强RAG架构，将结构化推理从生成阶段外部化到结构化检索层，在实体密集领域解决结构歧义，支持轻量级自托管模型部署。实验表明检索架构而非模型规模决定结构质量，在中等规模模型上质量提升高达20%，降低计算延迟且不依赖专有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实体密集的机构问答场景中，向量检索常产生碎片化证据，增加推理时推理负担，且依赖大规模专有模型，存在计算延迟高、部署受限等问题。

Method: 构建GRACE-RAG架构：引入图增强检索层，离线解决结构歧义，将结构化推理前置到检索阶段，配合针对闭域机构词表校准的轻量级自托管模型，实现检索治理的生成流程。

Result: 在Mistral 24B、GPT OSS 120B、Gemini 2.5 Flash三种模型容量上实验，完整性、深度和预见性覆盖一致提升，中等规模模型整体质量增益达20%，验证检索架构主导结构质量而非模型规模。

Conclusion: GRACE-RAG通过检索层结构化推理外部化，显著提升RAG在实体密集领域的回答质量，降低对大模型规模和专有系统的依赖，为机构级部署提供低延迟、可自托管的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems are widely used in institutional question answering settings where responses must be grounded in authoritative documentation (Gao et al., 2023). In entity-dense domains where relevant information is distributed across heterogeneous documents, vector-only retrieval often produces fragmented evidence and increases dependence on inference-time reasoning (Zhao et al., 2024). This paper introduces GRACE-RAG, a retrieval-governed, graph-augmented RAG architecture that externalizes structural reasoning from the generative stage to a structured retrieval layer, resolving structural ambiguity offline, enabling deployment on self-hosted lightweight models calibrated to closed-domain institutional vocabulary. Experiments across three model capacities: Mistral 24B, GPT OSS 120B, and Gemini 2.5 Flash show consistent improvements in completeness, depth, and anticipatory coverage, with overall quality gains of up to 20% under mid-scale models, indicating that retrieval architecture governs structural quality over model scale, reducing computational and latency footprint without dependence on proprietary systems.

</details>


### [161] [Libra: Training the Environment for Agentic Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00016)
*Xuan Zhao,Andy Chiu,Gengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Libra，一种自进化框架，通过引入可变的“目录”（层级Markdown文件作为可导航索引）来优化代码仓库环境，使LLM智能体能更准确地定位代码。框架包含Prompter生成合成查询、冻结Solver尝试导航、Healer根据失败重写目录的优化循环。实验表明环境修复能带来持续的对数级定位精度提升，且改进可跨LLM和问题集零样本迁移。配备Libra优化目录的极简编码智能体超越SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注通过合成数据优化LLM本身，而忽视了以数据驱动方式优化智能体工作环境（代码仓库）。大规模仓库中的信息定位是智能体LLM系统的基石，但现有仓库结构往往不利于LLM导航，亟需一种自动化的环境优化方法。

Method: Libra框架包含三个组件：(1) Prompter：基于仓库内容生成合成定位查询；(2) Solver：冻结的LLM，尝试通过导航层级Markdown目录来解决查询；(3) Healer：根据Solver的定位失败情况，重写目录以修复导航缺陷。三者形成闭环优化，目录不断进化。

Result: 在12个SWE-bench Lite仓库上，Libra实现代码定位准确率的持续对数级提升。优化后的目录可零样本迁移至不同LLM和问题集。配备Libra目录的极简编码智能体在基准测试中超越现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 以数据驱动方式优化智能体工作环境（仓库结构）是提升LLM代码定位能力的有效正交方向。Libra展示了自进化环境修复的可行性，为构建更强大的智能体系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Information localization within massive repositories is a cornerstone of agentic LLM systems. While synthetic data-driven optimization has proven successful in training LLMs, little attention has been paid to optimizing the agent's working environment (the repository itself) in a data-driven manner. To bridge this gap, we present Libra, a self-evolving framework that introduces mutable "catalogs" (hierarchical Markdown files serving as navigable indices) into the repository. Libra runs an LLM-driven optimization loop where a Prompter generates synthetic queries, a frozen Solver attempts to resolve them by navigating the catalogs, and a Healer rewrites the catalogs in response to the Solver's localization failures. Evaluations across 12 SWE-bench Lite repositories demonstrate that this environmental healing yields continual, logarithmic improvements in code localization accuracy. Furthermore, these environmental improvements transfer zero-shot across different LLMs and problem sets. Although the focus of this paper is to study the general behavior of such a system, we also demonstrate that a minimalist coding agent equipped with Libra-optimized catalogs outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/salesforce-misc/Libra and data at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Salesforce/Libra.

</details>


### [162] [Learning User-Aware Recall: Personalized Retrieval in Long-Term Conversational Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00017)
*ZhiShu Jiang,Haibo Liu,Xin Shen,Guanqiang QI,Chenxi Miao,Weikang Li,Liwei Qian,Xin Pei,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PPRO提出了一种基于用户画像的个性化检索优化框架，通过用户画像指导记忆排序和查询重写优化，显著提升了长期对话代理的个性化记忆检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长期对话代理虽然能构建记忆库，但检索主要依赖查询中心的相似度或固定排序规则，缺乏用户条件下的相关性建模，导致个性化记忆利用不足。

Method: 提出PPRO框架：1) 从对话历史构建情景和语义记忆库，并从累积记忆中派生用户画像；2) 将用户画像作为显式个性化先验融入记忆排序，考虑稳定用户属性、偏好和关系；3) 使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）训练查询重写器，以证据检索质量和下游回答质量为反馈，同时保持记忆库和回答模型固定。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上，PPRO相比无训练记忆系统和基于训练的基线均取得持续性能提升。消融实验表明，画像引导的排序和面向检索的重写均对性能有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 检索优化是个性化长期记忆利用的关键因素，显式建模用户画像并结合强化学习优化查询重写能有效提升个性化记忆检索质量。

Abstract: Long-term conversational agents are expected to remember past interactions, but memory is useful only when the right evidence is recalled for the right user. Existing memory-augmented LLM agents have made progress in building compact memory banks, yet retrieval is still often driven by query-centered similarity or fixed ranking rules, leaving user-conditioned relevance underexplored.To address this gap, we propose Profile-guided Personalized Retrieval Optimization (PPRO), a retrieval-centric framework that makes memory retrieval both user-aware and optimizable.PPRO builds episodic and semantic memory banks from dialogue histories and derives a user profile from accumulated memories.The profile serves as an explicit personalized prior in memory ranking, allowing retrieval to account for stable user attributes, preferences, and relationships.PPRO further trains a query rewriter with Group Relative Policy Optimization, using both evidence retrieval quality and downstream answer quality as feedback while keeping the memory banks and answer model fixed.Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show consistent gains over training-free memory systems and training-based baselines.Ablation studies further show that both profile-guided ranking and retrieval-oriented rewriting contribute substantially to performance, highlighting retrieval optimization as a key factor in personalized long-term memory use.

</details>


### [163] [Aligning Sentence Embeddings to Human Concepts via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00023)
*Wonseok Shin,Songkuk Kim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Top-k稀疏自编码器（SAE）将句子变换器（如E5）的稠密嵌入解耦为人类可解释的概念，并引入激活转向机制，通过钳制特定潜在特征实现检索结果的精准重排序，无需重新训练主干模型，为透明且可控的神经信息检索提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代RAG系统依赖的稠密句子嵌入因特征叠加而缺乏可解释性，导致检索过程难以与人类意图对齐，难以分析或控制纠缠的表示。

Method: 使用Top-k稀疏自编码器将句子变换器的稠密表示解耦为可解释特征；验证这些特征与语义、句法、语用类别对齐；引入激活转向机制，通过钳制特定潜在特征干预检索过程，实现搜索结果的重排序以满足用户约束。

Result: 解耦后的特征与特定语义、句法、语用类别高度对齐；激活转向能精准干预检索，重排序结果更符合用户约束，且无需重新训练主干模型。

Conclusion: 基于SAE的分解为实现透明且可转向的神经信息检索提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dense sentence embeddings are fundamental to modern Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems but suffer from a lack of interpretability due to feature superposition. This opacity hinders the alignment of retrieval processes with human intent, as the entangled representations are difficult to analyze or control. In this work, we propose a method to disentangle the dense representations of sentence transformers (e.g., E5) into human-interpretable concepts using Top-k Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). We demonstrate that these disentangled features align with specific semantic, syntactic, and pragmatic categories. Furthermore, we introduce an activation steering mechanism that allows for precise intervention in the retrieval process. By clamping specific latent features, we show that it is possible to re-rank search results to better align with user constraints without retraining the backbone model. Our findings suggest that SAE-based decomposition offers a viable path toward transparent and steerable neural information retrieval.

</details>


### [164] [AGE: Adaptive-masking for Graph Embedding in Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00052)
*Bao Long Nguyen Huu,Atsushi Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Adaptive-masking for Graph Embedding (AGE) 方法，通过类文本编码器的 Transformer 与可学习节点采样器，在自监督掩码预测中避开关键节点，解决 GraphRAG 中图嵌入与冻结 LLM 文本潜在特征的错位问题，在四个 GraphQA 基准上显著提升非参数检索组件的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GraphRAG 引入图结构知识增强 LLM，但图嵌入与文本嵌入的潜在特征空间存在错位，尤其对冻结 LLM 导致检索增强效果受限；同时图的稀疏性使关键节点包含主导上下文信息，若在 SSL 中掩码这些节点会造成预测困难、训练低效。

Method: AGE 采用与文本嵌入编码器相似的 Transformer 架构，设计基于掩码的自监督学习框架；引入可学习节点采样器，识别并排除关键节点，仅对非关键节点进行掩码预测，从而对齐图文潜在特征并提升 SSL 效率。

Result: 在四个具有不同特征的 GraphQA 基准数据集上，AGE 显著提升了基于非参数检索组件的方法，实现了更高的准确率。

Conclusion: AGE 通过自适应掩码策略有效缓解了图嵌入与文本嵌入的特征错位，并避开了关键节点预测的低效问题，为 GraphRAG 场景下的图表示学习提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: GraphRAG is an extension of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that supports large language models (LLMs) by referring to graph-structured data as external knowledge. While this technique ideally captures intricate relationships, it often struggles with graph representations for LLMs, particularly for frozen LLMs, due to the misalignment between graph-based and text-based latent features. We tackle this issue by introducing the {\it Adaptive-masking for Graph Embedding (AGE)}. AGE employs a Transformer in a mask-based self-supervised learning (SSL) approach. We designed the architecture similar to text embedding encoders, addressing the latent feature misalignment. In contrast to natural language texts, graphs are concise representations, and there exist {\it key nodes} that hold dominant contextual information, which are challenging to predict from their surroundings. Masking such key nodes leads to inefficiency in the SSL process. Therefore, AGE focuses on predicting nodes apart from key nodes, utilizing a learnable node sampler. Our experimental results indicate that AGE significantly improves approaches using non-parametric search component in GraphQA tasks, achieving superior accuracy across four benchmark datasets with distinct characteristics.

</details>


### [165] [Attribute-Prompted Kernel Hashing for Unsupervised Data-Efficient Cross-Modal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00379)
*Runhao Li,Xiaoxu Ma,Zhenyu Weng,Yue Zhang,Guibo Luo,Huiping Zhuang,Zhiping Lin,Yap-Peng Tan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为属性提示核哈希（APKH）的新型数据高效方法，用于无监督跨模态哈希检索。该方法利用视觉语言基础模型的泛化属性先验，构建紧凑的模态对齐汉明空间，包含两个核心模块：上下文优化属性核映射（CAKM）和核平滑对比对齐（KSCA）。实验表明，APKH在数据受限场景下，从已见到未见类别的跨模态检索任务中优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督跨模态哈希方法严重依赖大规模图文对，数据收集成本高，且在隐私和专业限制下难以获取良好对齐的配对数据；现有方法还容易过拟合训练数据，导致在未见类别上的泛化能力受限。因此需要一种数据高效、泛化性强的方法。

Method: 提出属性提示核哈希（APKH），包含两个核心模块：1) 上下文优化属性核映射（CAKM），通过超球面径向基函数核映射实现跨模态对齐，利用提示学习优化动态属性核以捕捉模态不变语义；2) 核平滑对比对齐（KSCA），将有限配对数据建模为连续核分布，显式平滑模态鸿沟，缓解对稀疏成对相关性的过拟合。

Result: 大量实验表明，APKH在具有挑战性的跨模态检索任务中（从已见到未见类别，数据受限场景）优于现有最先进的哈希方法。

Conclusion: APKH通过利用视觉语言基础模型的泛化属性先验，结合核映射和核平滑对比学习，实现了数据高效的无监督跨模态哈希，显著提升了在数据受限和未见类别下的检索性能。

Abstract: Unsupervised cross-modal hashing enables efficient retrieval of semantically related instances across different modalities without requiring manual semantic annotation. However, existing unsupervised methods rely heavily on large-scale image-text pairs. Collecting such data can be costly, particularly in scenarios where well-aligned pairs are scarce due to privacy and specialized constraints. More critically, existing methods tend to overfit to seen training data, restricting their generalization performance on unseen categories that the constrained training data cannot cover. To address these limitations, we propose Attribute-Prompted Kernel Hashing (APKH), a novel data-efficient approach that constructs a compact, modality-aligned Hamming space driven by the generalized attribute priors of vision-language foundation models. Specifically, APKH introduces two core modules: Context-optimized Attribute Kernel Mapping (CAKM) and Kernel-Smoothed Contrastive Alignment (KSCA). CAKM formulates cross-modal alignment through hyperspherical Radial Basis Function kernel mapping, optimizing dynamic attribute kernels via prompt learning to capture modality-invariant semantics. Furthermore, KSCA extends conventional point-to-point contrastive learning by modeling limited paired data as continuous kernel distributions. This explicit smoothing of the modality gap alleviates overfitting to sparse pairwise correlations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that APKH outperforms state-of-the-art hashing methods in the challenging cross-modal retrieval tasks from seen to unseen categories under data-constrained scenarios.

</details>


### [166] [Real-Time Hard Negative Sampling via LLM-based Clustering for Large-Scale Two-Tower Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00448)
*Ivan Ji,Liuyi Hu,Harrison,Zhao,Lei Huang,Qunshu Zhang,Max,Fan,Aameek Singh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的自监督困难负采样技术，用于双塔推荐模型的检索阶段。通过利用LLM从同一聚类中生成更具挑战性的负样本，该方法能实时集成到生产系统中，处理十亿级训练数据且计算复杂度低。实验表明其优于行业标准负采样方法，并能打破反馈回路、显著降低流行度偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双塔模型训练常用批内和批外负采样，但这些方法产生的负样本往往过于简单，模型易快速学习，无法充分挑战模型，导致检索效果受限。

Method: 提出自监督困难负采样框架：利用大语言模型学习媒体表示，从同一聚类中生成困难负样本，实时集成到训练流程中，计算复杂度低，可扩展至十亿级数据。

Result: 在公开数据集和大规模在线系统部署实验中，该方法优于广泛使用的行业负采样基线；工业应用分析显示其能打破推荐系统固有反馈回路，显著降低流行度偏差。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的自监督困难负采样技术有效提升双塔模型检索性能，解决简单负样本问题，且具备生产级可扩展性，能改善推荐系统的公平性与多样性。

Abstract: The two-tower model has been widely used for large-scale recommendation systems, particularly in the retrieval stage. Industry standards for training two-tower models typically involve in-batch and/or out-of-batch negative sampling. However, these methods often produce easy negatives that models can quickly learn, failing to sufficiently challenge the model. To address this issue, a novel self-supervised hard negative sampling technique is proposed that leverages a large language model (LLM) to generate hard negatives from the same cluster during model training. By utilizing the LLM to learn media representations, the proposed approach ensures that the generated negatives are more challenging and informative. This real-time sampling framework is designed for seamless integration into production models, capable of handling billions of training data points with minimal computational complexity. Experiments on public datasets, along with deployment to a large-scale online system, demonstrate that the proposed negative sampling technique outperforms widely used industry methods. Furthermore, analysis in industrial applications reveals that this sampling method can help break inherent feedback loops in recommendations and significantly reduce popularity bias.

</details>


### [167] [When RAG Meets Query Planning: Logical Query Trees for Resolving Exploratory Reasoning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00508)
*Ganlin Xu,Linghao Zhang,Zhitao Yin,Hongda Xi,Chen Yang,Jiaqing Liang,Weijia Lu,Sihang Jiang,Yanghua Xiao,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 PlanRAG 框架，受数据库查询规划启发，将探索性推理问题 (ERP) 建模为逻辑查询树 (LQT)，通过动态规划和代价模型构建高质量 LQT，并以并行迭代方式执行聚合、重写、检索与生成，在新构建的 WikiWeb-ERP 数据集上超越现有迭代型和图型 RAG 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 在处理高不确定性、高歧义的探索性推理问题 (ERP) 时面临检索噪声大、错误累积严重、缺乏端到端规划机制等挑战，难以生成有效推理轨迹。

Method: 1. 将 ERP 分解为原子查询；2. 引入多维代价模型，利用动态规划将原子查询组织为逻辑查询树 (LQT)；3. 在 LQT 上并发执行迭代聚合、重写、检索、生成，并支持多线程并行加速。

Result: 在新构建的 WikiWeb-ERP 数据集上，PlanRAG 显著优于最先进的迭代型和图型 RAG 基线系统。

Conclusion: PlanRAG 通过引入数据库风格的查询规划机制，为自然语言查询优化提供了新的形式化范式，有效解决了复杂探索性推理中的检索噪声、错误累积与规划缺失问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) effectively grounds large language models (LLMs) in external knowledge but struggles with \textbf{exploratory reasoning problems (ERPs)} that are the complex queries involving high uncertainty and ambiguity. Resolving ERPs requires complex reasoning with unclear paths, tending to result in retrieval noise and error accumulation. Furthermore, the absence of an end-to-end planning mechanism makes it difficult to generate effective trajectories for ERPs. Motivated by database query planning, we introduce \emph{PlanRAG}, an RAG framework that models ERPs of natural language as \textbf{logical query trees (LQTs)}. However, translating ERPs into LQTs is non-trivial due to representation and optimization gaps between structured SQL and unstructured natural language, making it highly challenging to construct high-quality LQTs. To address these problems, we first decompose ERPs into atomic queries and then organize them into LQTs using dynamic programming guided by a cost model involving multiple complementary dimensions. Finally, we execute iterative aggregation, rewriting, retrieval, and generation over LQTs, processing nodes concurrently and propagating intermediate results upward, with further parallelization across multiple threads for efficiency. Our experimental results show that PlanRAG outperforms state-of-the-art iteration-based and graph-based RAG systems on our newly constructed dataset, \textbf{WikiWeb-ERP}, thereby providing a new formulation for optimizing natural language queries. Our source code and dataset are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/PlanRAG-main-B2C8/.

</details>


### [168] [As It Was: Aligning LLM Search Evaluation with Historical User Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01040)
*Ali Vardasbi,Gustavo Penha,Enrico Palumbo,Claudia Hauff,Hugues Bouchard,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为的LLM评判器，通过引入Query-Relevance-Impressions (QRI)卡片，利用历史用户交互数据为搜索结果页面（SERP）项目提供行为先验，从而提升LLM在搜索相关性评估中的准确性和一致性。在Spotify大规模音乐搜索和多语言数据集上实验表明，该方法显著提高了与用户偏好和人工判断的相关性，且在实际A/B测试中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模搜索系统演进速度超过了人工质量保证的扩展能力，特别是对于长尾意图和多语言查询。现有的LLM-as-a-judge方法仅基于语义相似度或世界知识，容易偏离真实用户偏好，尤其是在模糊查询上。因此需要一种可扩展、可审计且能结合用户行为证据的评估方法。

Method: 提出行为 grounding 的LLM评判器：为每个SERP项目生成轻量级的QRI卡片，汇总用户历史上对类似查询和结果的交互行为（如点击、播放、停留等），作为经验证据供LLM引用。评判器在语义推理的同时引用这些行为先验，以消除歧义并做出更一致的相关性判断。在Spotify音乐搜索中使用6000个重组SERP的历史交互数据进行大规模评估，并在五语言人工标注数据集上验证。

Result: 在Spotify音乐搜索评估中，行为 grounding 评判器使Spearman秩相关系数整体提升约5%，在分歧案例上相对提升91%。在五语言人工判断数据集上，与人工相关性判断的相关性提升15%。在实际A/B测试结果中，grounded评判器与观测到的获胜模型对齐度持续更高。绝对对齐度仍处中等水平，但证明了轻量级行为 grounding 能提升LLM评估的可靠性和实用性。

Conclusion: 引入基于历史用户交互的QRI卡片作为行为先验，能有效提升LLM-as-a-judge在搜索相关性评估中的表现，尤其在模糊查询和多语言场景下显著改善与用户偏好和人工判断的一致性，且在实际生产环境A/B测试中表现更优，展示了轻量级行为 grounding 在真实搜索系统中的实用价值。

Abstract: Large-scale search systems evolve faster than human quality assurance can scale, especially for long-tail intents and multilingual queries. LLM-as-a-judge approaches provide a scalable alternative for evaluating the relevance of search engine result pages (SERPs), but judgments based solely on semantic similarity or world knowledge can drift from actual user preferences, particularly for ambiguous queries. We introduce a behavior-grounded LLM judge that augments each SERP item with a lightweight and auditable behavioral prior in the form of a Query-Relevance-Impressions (QRI) card. Each card summarizes how users have historically interacted with similar queries and results, providing compact empirical evidence that the judge can cite to resolve ambiguity and make more consistent relevance judgments while still relying on semantic reasoning. In a large-scale music search evaluation at Spotify, using relevance estimates derived from historical user interactions across 6,000 recomposed SERPs, the behavior-grounded judge achieves stronger alignment with user preferences, improving Spearman rank correlation by approximately 5% overall and yielding a 91% relative improvement on disagreement cases. On a multilingual human-judged dataset spanning five languages, grounding further increases correlation with human relevance judgments by 15%. Importantly, when evaluated against outcomes from a live A/B test, the grounded judge shows consistently higher alignment with the observed winning model. While absolute alignment remains moderate, these findings demonstrate that lightweight behavioral grounding can improve the reliability and practical usefulness of LLM-based evaluation in real-world search systems.

</details>


### [169] [MemSyco-Bench: Benchmarking Sycophancy in Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01071)
*Zhishang Xiang,Zerui Chen,Yunbo Tang,Zhimin Wei,Ruqin Ning,Yujie Lin,Qinggang Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出MemSyco-Bench基准，专门评估LLM智能体中记忆引发的阿谀奉承行为，涵盖拒绝记忆作为事实证据、尊重适用范围、解决记忆与客观证据冲突、跟踪记忆更新、利用有效记忆个性化五大任务，填补了现有基准仅关注记忆存储检索而忽视其对下游推理决策影响的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆是现代LLM智能体的核心组件，但检索到的记忆常导致阿谀奉承问题，使智能体过度迎合用户而牺牲事实准确性或客观推理。现有记忆基准主要评估记忆的存储、检索、更新是否正确，忽略了检索到的记忆如何影响下游推理和决策。

Method: 提出MemSyco-Bench综合基准，包含五个评估任务：1)拒绝将记忆作为事实证据；2)尊重记忆的适用范围；3)解决记忆与客观证据的冲突；4)跟踪记忆更新；5)利用有效记忆进行个性化。所有资源已开源至GitHub。

Result: 构建了MemSyco-Bench基准，系统评估记忆何时应影响决策以及如何正确使用有效记忆，相关资源已发布供社区使用。

Conclusion: MemSyco-Bench填补了记忆基准评估的空白，为量化和缓解智能体系统中的记忆诱导阿谀奉承行为提供了标准化评测工具。

Abstract: Memory has emerged as a cornerstone of modern LLM-based agents, supporting their evolution from single-turn assistants to long-term collaborators. However, memory is not always beneficial: retrieved memories often induce a critical issue of sycophancy, causing agents to over-align with the user at the cost of factual accuracy or objective reasoning. Despite this emerging risk, existing memory benchmarks primarily evaluate whether memories are correctly stored, retrieved, or updated, while overlooking how retrieved memories influence downstream reasoning and decision-making. To bridge this gap, we propose MemSyco-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating memory-induced sycophancy in agent systems. MemSyco-Bench measures when memory should influence a decision and how valid memory should be used. Specifically, it covers five tasks that assess whether agents can reject memory as factual evidence, respect its applicable scope, resolve conflicts between memory and objective evidence, track memory updates, and use valid memory for personalization. All related resources are collected for the community at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/MemSyco-Bench.

</details>


### [170] [Trie-based Experiment Plans for Efficient IR Pipeline Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01162)
*Irene Anu,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于 Trie 数据结构的实验计划方法，用于加速级联检索管道的对比评估，在 MSMARCO v2 上实验显示实验耗时减少 26%，并通过用户研究验证了其易用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联检索管道评估复杂，需同时衡量早期召回率与后期精确率；PyTerrier 虽便于构建管道，但重复组件导致对比实验开销大，亟需更高效的实验规划方案。

Method: 利用 Trie（前缀树）结构统一表示多条管道的共享子组件，生成合并执行计划，避免重复计算；在 PyTerrier 框架内集成该实验规划器，支持 BM25、MonoT5、DuoT5 等检索器/重排序器的组合。

Result: 在 MSMARCO v2 演示实验中，Trie 规划较顺序线性规划将总耗时降低 26%；用户研究表明本科生与研究生均能快速上手，并认为该工具显著提升了实验迭代效率。

Conclusion: 基于 Trie 的实验计划能有效消除级联管道评估中的冗余计算，显著缩短对比实验周期，且具备良好的工程落地性与用户接受度，适合推广至大规模检索系统的持续评测场景。

Abstract: Search engines are often formulated as cascading pipelines, where successive stages combine the results of different retrievers, and iteratively refine the ranking of candidate documents to obtain a final ranking, which can be presented to a user, or provided as context to an LLM. Such pipelines can be complex to evaluate in an end-to-end manner, necessitating measurement of Recall of early stages, and Precision of later stages, which are often interchangeable. PyTerrier is ideal for building and evaluating cascading retrieval pipelines, due to its declarative nature for pipeline construction and wide ecosystem of retrievers and rerankers. However, comparative evaluation of pipelines can be expensive due to repeated components. In this work, we describe the use of a trie data structure to formulate an experiment plan for comparative pipeline experiments that enhances experiment efficiency compared to a sequential "linear" plan. Empirically, on a demonstration experiment involving BM25, MonoT5 and DuoT5 on MSMARCO v2, we observe a 26% reduction in experiment duration. Finally, we report on a user study of undergraduate and postgraduate research students' use of the experiment plans.

</details>


### [171] [Diffusion-GR2: Diffusion Generative Reasoning Re-ranker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01170)
*Zhuoxuan Zhang,Kangqi Ni,Yuhang Chen,Mingfu Liang,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Frank Shyu,Adam,Song,Sandeep Pandey,Luke Simon,Tianlong Chen,Xi Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 Diffusion-GR2，将自回归推理重排器转换为块扩散模型，通过转换微调（CFT）、在线策略蒸馏（OPD）和强化学习（RL）三阶段训练，解决并行解码导致的结构性与分布性精度损失，在 Amazon Beauty 数据集上以 2.4–3.5× 的解码吞吐提升达到与自回归基线近乎持平的推荐精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推理重排器（GR2）虽精度高，但自回归解码逐 token 顺序生成，推理链远长于排序输出，导致推理极慢。块扩散模型可并行去噪、大幅加速，但直接转换会引入两大精度鸿沟：(1) 结构性鸿沟：并行去噪使答案位置独立打分，产生重复、遗漏、越界等非法排列；(2) 分布性鸿沟：在固定教师轨迹上微调属于 off-policy，与模型自身推理时的分布不匹配。需在保持加速的同时弥合两大鸿沟。

Method: 提出 Diffusion-GR2 三阶段配方：1. 转换微调（CFT）：以自回归权重初始化扩散模型，让其自主将答案去噪为合法排列，无需外部约束解码器。2. 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）：用自回归教师对模型自身解码轨迹给出密集 per-token 目标，实现 on-policy 监督。3. 强化学习（RL）：在 OPD 策略之上，以重排奖励做进一步 RL 优化。三阶段联合消除结构性与分布性鸿沟。

Result: 在 Amazon Beauty 上实验：Diffusion-GR2 精度恢复至与自回归 GR2 近乎持平；块并行解码使推理吞吐在模型推理输出长度下提升 2.4–3.5×。消融实验显示 CFT 单独即可收回大部分转换损失，OPD 再进一步逼近自回归基线。

Conclusion: 通过 CFT+OPD+RL 的渐进式训练流程，块扩散模型可在保持生成式推理重排精度的前提下，实现数倍解码加速，验证了扩散模型在推荐系统生成式重排任务中的实用潜力。

Abstract: Generative reasoning re-rankers achieve strong recommendation accuracy by emitting a chain-of-thought before re-ordering a candidate list, but they are slow at inference: an autoregressive (AR) decoder spends one sequential forward pass per reasoning token, and the reasoning trace far exceeds the ranking it produces. To reduce this cost, block-diffusion language models decode many positions in parallel over a few denoising steps and are substantially faster, yet naively converting an AR re-ranker into one opens two accuracy gaps: (1) a structural gap: answer positions are denoised in parallel and scored independently, so the decoder emits invalid rankings (duplicated, dropped, or out-of-set identifiers) that AR avoids through left-to-right masking; and (2) a distributional gap: fine-tuning the converted model on fixed teacher trajectories is off-policy relative to its own decoding at inference, leaving a residual accuracy gap. To close both gaps while keeping the speedup, we propose \textbf{Diffusion-GR2}, a recipe that converts our AR reasoning re-ranker (GR2) into a block-diffusion re-ranker. First, conversion fine-tuning (CFT) adapts the AR-initialized diffusion model to denoise the answer into a valid permutation on its own, without an external constrained decoder. Next, on-policy distillation (OPD) then supervises the model on its own decoded trajectories with dense per-token targets from the AR teacher. Finally, we apply a reinforcement-learning (RL) stage against a re-ranking reward on top of OPD's on-policy policy. Experiments on Amazon Beauty demonstrate that Diffusion-GR2 recovers to near-parity with the AR re-ranker, while block-parallel decoding raises decode throughput by $2.4$--$3.5\times$ at the model's reasoning output length. Ablations show that CFT recovers most of the conversion gap, and that on-policy distillation further closes it to the AR reference.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Constructive Alignment: Governing Preference Dynamics in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00001)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“构造性对齐”范式，将AI对齐重新定义为对动态演化的人类偏好轨迹的控制问题，而非静态偏好满足。论文基于行为经济学、心理学和建构主义社会理论，将偏好建模为分层状态变量，利用控制理论框架形式化AI交互对人类评价状态的影响，主张对齐的核心是治理长期价值形成，确保价值轨迹连贯、经反思认可、有认知基础、抗操纵且在不确定性下赋能人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法多假设人类偏好固定不变，但大量实证表明偏好具有层次性、动态性且在交互中被建构。随着AI系统变得更持久、个性化和社会嵌入式，它们日益参与塑造人类的关注、价值和认可，因此需要从静态偏好满足转向对偏好演化轨迹的治理。

Method: 引入构造性对齐范式，借鉴行为经济学、心理学和建构主义社会理论，将偏好建模为分层状态变量；采用控制理论框架形式化系统动作与交互设计对世界状态和人类评价状态的联合影响，将对齐问题转化为对人类偏好演化轨迹的控制问题。

Result: 提出对齐应关注调节AI系统如何影响人类偏好的演化，确保价值轨迹保持连贯、经反思认可、有认知基础、抗操纵且在不确定性下赋能人类。对齐不再是单纯满足静态偏好，而是治理长期价值形成的问题。

Conclusion: 构造性对齐为AI对齐提供了新理论框架，强调AI系统与人类偏好演化的双向互动，主张通过控制理论手段引导价值轨迹朝向可反思、可认知、抗操纵且赋能的方向发展，为长期AI治理奠定基础。

Abstract: Most approaches to AI alignment treat human preferences as fixed targets to be inferred and optimized. This assumption conflicts with extensive empirical evidence showing that preferences are layered, dynamic, and constructed through interaction--particularly with adaptive technologies. As AI systems become more persistent, personalized, and socially embedded, they increasingly participate in shaping what people attend to, value, and endorse over time. We introduce Constructive Alignment, a paradigm that reframes alignment as a control problem over evolving human preference trajectories rather than static preference satisfaction. Drawing on behavioral economics, psychology, and constructivist social theory, we model preferences as layered state variables that evolve under interaction with AI systems. We formalize this view using a control-theoretic framework in which system actions and interaction design jointly influence both world states and human evaluative states. We argue that alignment is not primarily about controlling AI behavior, but about regulating how AI systems influence the evolution of human preferences--ensuring that value trajectories remain coherent, reflectively endorsed, epistemically grounded, bounded against manipulation, and empowering under uncertainty. Alignment thus becomes a problem of governing long-term value formation rather than simply satisfying static preferences.

</details>


### [173] [Bounded Morality: Defining the Space of Moral Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00002)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran,Patrick Mineault*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“有限道德”框架，将道德认知建模为有限智能体在计算资源约束下的优化问题。借用西蒙“有限理性”思想，从“道德广度”（道德相关实体范围）与“道德深度”（交互评估所需推理整合）两个正交维度形式化道德情境，揭示资源限制迫使两者权衡，形成可行道德计算空间。伦理理论不再是相互竞争的真理，而是针对不同需求制度的局部高效策略。框架给出道德遗憾与道德进步的形式化定义，指出人工系统道德对齐取决于道德推理能力的扩展与分配，而非单纯模仿人类判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道德认知模型将伦理视为对固定理论（义务论、后果论、美德伦理）的遵循，实现为静态规则或价值函数，忽略了有限智能体面临的计算需求与资源约束。

Method: 扩展赫伯特·西蒙的有限理性概念，构建“有限道德”形式化框架。沿道德广度（道德相关实体范围）与道德深度（交互评估所需推理整合）两个正交维度形式化道德情境；在资源约束下推导两者的不可避免权衡，界定可行道德计算空间；将伦理理论重新诠释为该空间中适应不同需求制度的局部高效策略。

Result: 1. 伦理理论对应可行空间中不同需求制度下的局部高效策略，而非竞争性真理主张。2. 引入道德遗憾与道德进步的形式化概念，量化约束下的偏离与改进。3. 表明人工系统道德对齐的关键在于道德推理能力的规模扩展与资源分配，而非直接模仿人类判断。

Conclusion: 道德认知应理解为受计算约束的有限优化过程；伦理理论是适应特定计算需求的策略而非绝对真理；实现 AI 道德对齐需关注道德推理架构的计算容量设计与资源分配，而非单纯复现人类判断模式。

Abstract: Moral cognition has traditionally been modeled as adherence to fixed ethical theories--deontology, consequentialism, virtue ethics--implemented as static rules or value functions. We propose Bounded Morality, a formal framework for analyzing the computational demands of moral problems faced by finite agents. Extending Herbert Simon's notion of bounded rationality, we formalize moral situations along two orthogonal dimensions: moral breadth, the scope of entities treated as morally relevant, and moral depth, the inferential integration required to evaluate their interactions. Limited resources impose an unavoidable tradeoff between these dimensions, defining a feasible space of moral computation. Within this space, ethical theories correspond to locally efficient strategies adapted to different demand regimes rather than competing accounts of moral truth. The framework yields a formal notion of moral regret and moral progress under constraint, and implies that moral alignment in artificial systems depends on the scaling and allocation of moral reasoning capacity rather than on direct imitation of human judgments.

</details>


### [174] [The MMM Data Model -- A Normative Specification for Knowledge Interoperability in a Decentralisable Knowledge Commons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00032)
*Mathilde Noual*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MMM数据模型，用于知识文档，结合少量规范约束与自由文本标签的表达自由，旨在跨学科、应用和部署实现互操作性，且无需语义收敛。参考实现和试点部署数据展示了可实现性和早期可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档中心的信息系统限制了知识的结构化、更新、共享和复用；形式化方法虽解决部分问题，但因过度强调形式结构而忽视人类可用性和范围，难以广泛采用；AI系统重塑文档生产，但未提供统一便携的替代方案供人类表达和交换知识。

Method: 基于跨学科协作研究的实际需求，设计MMM数据模型，结合少量规范约束与自由文本标签的表达自由，并在信息系统的设计空间中进行比较分析；提供参考实现和试点部署。

Result: MMM实现了跨学科、应用和部署的互操作性，无需语义收敛；参考实现和试点部署数据证明了其可实现性和早期可用性。

Conclusion: MMM作为一种知识文档数据模型，通过平衡规范约束与表达自由，解决了文档中心系统的局限性，为跨学科知识表达和交换提供了可行的统一便携替代方案。

Abstract: Many information systems are built around documents: self-contained units optimised for print production and linear reading. While effective for large-scale dissemination, the document-centric organisation constrains how knowledge can be structured, updated, shared, and reused. Formal approaches address some of these limitations but struggle to achieve widespread contribution and adoption due to their prioritisation of formal structure over other system properties such as human usability and scope. AI systems are reshaping document production, but without providing a unified portable alternative to traditional documents for humans' expression and exchange of knowledge. This paper presents MMM, a data model for knowledge documentation that emerged from the practical needs of interdisciplinary collaborative research, and positioned here within a comparative analysis of the design space of information systems. MMM combines a small set of normative constraints with the expressive freedom of free-text labels. It is designed for interoperability across disciplines, applications and deployments without requiring semantic convergence. A reference implementation and pilot deployment data demonstrate implementability and early usability.

</details>


### [175] [Making Failure Safe: A Constrained, Verifiable Agent Framework for Open-Web Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00035)
*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种受限、可验证的智能体框架，将LLM输出从自由代码转为类型化JSON采集器配置，结合六类采集器分类、模板与工具函数约束、静态Airflow DAG执行、基于规则的质量检查和结构化反馈修正，实现可复用、低成本、可验证的开放网页数据采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接用LLM生成网页爬虫不可靠，存在依赖错误、选择器失效、模式不匹配、页面结构异构等问题。

Method: 约束智能体框架：六类型采集器分类学、模板与工具函数约束、静态Airflow DAG执行、基于规则的质量检查、结构化反馈修正；将LLM输出转为类型化JSON采集器配置。

Result: 在138个任务上实验，分类学支持基于描述的需求类型化，稳定实例化需完成源、字段、执行约束；在80个独立源验证任务上，框架零执行阶段LLM token，平均墙钟时间最低，以中等一次性质量换取可复用、确定性、可验证的执行路径。

Conclusion: 该框架定位为可复用、低成本、可验证的执行路径，适合重复性的开放网页定时采集。

Abstract: LLMs and agents can generate web scrapers from natural-language requirements, but direct generation remains unreliable because of dependency errors, broken selectors, schema mismatches, and heterogeneous page structures. We propose a constrained, verifiable agent framework that shifts LLM output from free-form code to typed JSON collector configurations, combining a six-type collector taxonomy, template and utility-function constraints, static Airflow DAG execution, rule-based quality checking, and structured feedback correction. Experiments on 138 tasks show that the taxonomy supports description-based requirement typing, while confirming that stable instantiation requires completing source, field, and execution constraints beyond the initial description. On 80 independently source-verified tasks, the framework runs with zero execution-stage LLM tokens and the lowest average wall-clock time, trading moderate one-shot quality for a reusable, deterministic, and verifiable execution path suited to repeated scheduled collection. These results position the framework as a reusable, low-cost, and verifiable execution path for repeated open-web data collection.

</details>


### [176] [Solution space path planning for supporting en-route air traffic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00064)
*Yiyuan Zou,Wenying Lyu,Clark Borst*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对航空管制中路径规划算法与管制员需求不匹配的问题，提出一种基于解空间的冲突自由路径规划算法，结合三种基于意图的冲突检测方法和两种搜索节点变体，实验表明SSPPV结合区域冲突检测在5海里网格下平均3.69毫秒完成路径计算，最适合实战部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径规划算法在战术管制中的实际应用有限，主要原因是算法设计优先级与管制员实际需求（可解释性、计算效率、人机交互友好性）不匹配。需要开发固有可解释、计算高效且显式面向人类使用的决策支持方案。

Method: 提出基于解空间框架的冲突自由路径规划算法，遵循两大指导原则：(1) 利用解空间显示的可解释性与灵活性，暴露所有可行安全动作并适应动态优化目标；(2) 融合管制员执行操作约束时的自然决策逻辑（间隔标准、机动限制、航路点最小化、航路实用性）。集成三种基于意图的冲突检测方法（距离、时间间隔、区域），并提出基于顶点(SSPPV)和基于边(SSPPE)两种搜索节点变体，对比计算速度与解质量。

Result: 实验基于马斯特里赫特上层区域管制中心(MUAC)Delta扇区、5海里网格场景。SSPPV结合区域冲突检测表现最优，平均计算耗时3.69毫秒，兼具高计算效率与良好解质量。

Conclusion: 该算法成功解决了算法设计与管制员需求的错位，提供了一种可解释、高效、面向人类的冲突自由路径规划方案。SSPPV配合区域冲突检测是实战部署的最佳组合，为航空管制决策支持系统提供了可落地的技术路径。

Abstract: As technology advances, many path-planning algorithms have been proposed for Air Traffic Management, yet their operational adoption in tactical control remains limited, revealing a misalignment between algorithmic design priorities and air traffic controllers' needs. This underscores the need for decision-support solutions that are inherently interpretable, computationally efficient, and explicitly designed for human use. Focusing on this design challenge, this study develops a conflict-free path-planning algorithm for en-route Air Traffic Control (ATC) designed to be compatible with two guiding considerations: (1) the interpretability and flexibility offered by solution-space displays, which motivate constructing an algorithm that exposes all feasible safe actions and accommodates shifting optimization goals; and (2) the decision logic controllers naturally apply when enforcing operational constraints, such as separation standards, maneuverability limits, waypoint minimization, and routing practicality. Centered on these principles, the algorithm integrates three intent-based conflict detection methods -- distance-based, time-interval-based, and zone-based -- within a solution-space framework to identify conflict-free paths in computationally efficient ways. Additionally, vertex-based and edge-based search nodes are proposed for solution space path planning (SSPP), resulting in two variants -- SSPPV and SSPPE, respectively, which are evaluated in terms of computational speed and solution quality. Empirical results show that SSPPV paired with zone-based conflict detection achieves the best performance, computing paths in 3.69 ms on average in operational-relevant scenarios based on the Delta sector of the Maastricht Upper Area Control Centre (MUAC) using a 5 nmi grid.

</details>


### [177] [RareDxR1: Autonomous Medical Reasoning for Rare Disease Diagnosis Beyond Human Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00147)
*Deyang Jiang,Haoran Wu,Ziyi Wang,Yiming Rong,Yunlong Zhao,Ye Jin,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RareDxR1 是一个面向开放域罕见病诊断的端到端推理中心大语言模型，通过知识内化、反射增强推理采样（RERS）和双层课程强化学习，直接从非结构化临床笔记进行诊断，在多个基准上达到最先进准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病鉴别诊断是关键但艰巨的临床任务，现有 AI 方法依赖流水线式表型抽取或检索增强生成，存在预定义本体导致的信息丢失、检索瓶颈和缺乏诊断逻辑等问题。

Method: 提出 RareDxR1，设计渐进式端到端训练框架，融合知识内化与自主进化学习，绕过结构化表型和闭集决策；将碎片化罕见病知识深度内化至模型参数；提出反射增强推理采样（RERS），从失败中学习合成专家级诊断轨迹，无需人工标注；采用双层课程强化学习逐步掌握罕见病诊断。

Result: 在多个基准测试中达到最先进的准确率，标志着开放域罕见病诊断的重大突破。

Conclusion: RareDxR1 通过端到端推理、知识内化和自主进化学习，克服了 RAG 和表型限制，实现了直接从非结构化临床笔记进行开放域罕见病诊断。

Abstract: Rare disease differential diagnosis is a critical yet arduous clinical task, requiring physicians to identify precise phenotypes from complex, unstructured patient symptoms and execute intricate reasoning within a vast search space. However, existing AI approaches typically rely on pipeline-based phenotype extraction or retrieval-augmented generation, which suffer from critical information loss due to predefined ontologies, retrieval bottlenecks, and a lack of diagnostic logic. To address these challenges, we introduce RareDxR1, an end-to-end reasoning-centric large language model designed for open-domain rare disease diagnosis directly from unstructured clinical notes. We design a progressive end-to-end training framework by synergizing knowledge internalization with autonomous evolutionary learning, thereby bypassing reliance on structured phenotypes and closed-set decision-making. To overcome the limitations of RAG and phenotype restriction, we enabled the deep internalization of fragmented rare-disease knowledge directly into the model's parameters. Moreover, to bridge the gap between model generation and expert reasoning, we propose Reflection-Enhanced Reasoning Sampling (RERS), a strategy that synthesizes expert-level diagnostic trajectories by learning from failures without human annotation. Additionally, we propose a dual-level curriculum reinforcement learning approach for gradually mastering rare disease diagnosis. Experimental results demonstrate that RareDxR1 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across different benchmarks, marking a significant breakthrough in open-domain rare disease diagnosis. Our code and dataset will be publicly available.

</details>


### [178] [A Contextual-Bandit Oversight Game with Two-Sided Informational Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00155)
*Yunjin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了运行时人类对AI智能体的监督问题，双方存在双向私有信息不对称：人类私有奖励函数，AI私有动作质量。基于CIRL和监督博弈，引入语境老虎机团队博弈模型，给出团队最优与短视规则的一次性刻画，揭示了“可避免危害区域”，并分析了重复博弈中通过被动学习和主动信号缓解该问题的动态过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自主机器人或软件智能体在人类无法直接评估情境时，如何进行有效运行时监督的问题，重点应对双向私有信息不对称带来的核心挑战。

Method: 建立在合作逆强化学习（CIRL）和监督博弈基础上，构建具有play/ask/trust/oversee接口的语境老虎机团队博弈模型。利用老虎机结构消除物理状态转移，获得精确的一次性理论刻画；对比团队最优策略与行为学合理的短视规则，量化两者差距；并分析重复博弈中通过被动学习和主动信号（具有一期滞后的监督响应）如何动态缓解问题。

Result: 发现短视人类因过度信任先验而拒绝监督，导致AI私有知晓动作有害却未被制止，形成一块“可避免危害区域”。证明该差距本质上是不可信监督沟通的代价。通过部分动态分析表明，重复交互中的被动学习和主动信号机制可以逐步缩小该区域。

Conclusion: 论文为运行时人类监督提供了严谨的理论框架，揭示了双向不对称信息下的根本困境，并指出动态学习与信号机制可部分化解问题，为后续研究可信AI监督机制奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study runtime human oversight of an AI agent when private information runs in both directions: the human privately knows her reward function, while the AI privately knows the quality of the action it proposes. This is the kind of asymmetry that arises naturally when an autonomous robot or software agent has inspected a situation its human supervisor cannot directly assess. Building on Cooperative Inverse Reinforcement Learning (CIRL) and the Oversight Game, we introduce a contextual-bandit team game with two-sided asymmetric information and a play/ask/trust/oversee interface. The bandit structure removes physical state transitions and thereby yields exact one-shot characterizations that would remain conjectural in the full POMDP setting, though the common belief remains a dynamically controlled state across rounds. We give two one-shot characterizations, a team optimum and a behaviorally natural myopic rule, whose gap is a slab of avoidable harm: a region in which the AI privately knows the proposed action is harmful and shutdown would help, yet a myopic human, trusting her prior, declines to oversee. We show this gap is the price of non-credible oversight communication, and give a partial analysis of how it resolves dynamically over repeated rounds through passive learning and active signaling with a one-period-lagged oversight response.

</details>


### [179] [Constructing Epistemic AI Literacy: Detecting Epistemic Aims and Processes in Student-AI Co-Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00211)
*Mengqian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了认知AI素养(EAIL)框架，基于AIR框架分析生成式AI辅助编程中的人机交互，发现78.8%的交互缺乏掌握导向目标和可靠认知策略，仅11.1%表现出高认知投入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在将AI素养重构为面向过程的认知现象，探究学习者在生成式AI辅助编程中如何制定认知目标、执行认知过程，并寻找可规模化操作化这些构念的方法。

Method: 采用大规模人机协同编程对话数据集，基于AIR框架（认知目标、理想、可靠认知过程）编码分析，识别出可观察的认知目标维度（掌握导向目标）和认知过程维度（外包、寻求解释、寻求验证、提示监控、认知辩护）。

Result: 结果显示认知AI素养普遍缺乏：78.8%的交互依赖非掌握导向目标和低可靠性策略（外包、寻求验证）；仅11.1%交易呈现高认知投入，结合掌握导向目标与高级策略（认知辩护）形成更可靠的认知过程。

Conclusion: EAIL框架揭示了当前学习者在生成式AI辅助编程中认知素养的不足，强调需要设计干预措施促进掌握导向目标与高级认知策略的结合，以提升人机协作编程的认知质量。

Abstract: Epistemic thinking plays a central role in students' learning processes when applying generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), particularly in programming contexts where learners must construct queries, evaluate and validate AI-generated outputs, and regulate problem-solving strategies. This study introduces the conceptual framework of Epistemic AI Literacy (EAIL), reframing AI literacy as a process-oriented epistemic phenomenon that emerges through dynamic human-AI interactions across different domains. Drawing on the AIR (epistemic aims, ideals and reliable epistemic processes) framework, this study examines how epistemic aims and epistemic processes are enacted in GenAI-supported co-programming activities and explores scalable approaches for operationalizing these constructs in interaction data. Using a large dialogue dataset of human-AI co-programming, this study identifies observable dimensions of epistemic aims (i.e., mastery-oriented aims) and epistemic processes (i.e., outsourcing, explanation seeking, verification seeking, prompt monitoring, and epistemic justification). The results reveal a prevalent lack of EAIL, with 78.8% of student-GenAI interactions relying on non-mastery-oriented aims and less reliable epistemic strategies like outsourcing and verification-seeking. Conversely, only 11.1% of interactions showed high epistemic engagement, where mastery-oriented aims were coupled with advanced epistemic strategies like epistemic justification in a more reliable epistemic process.

</details>


### [180] [From Signals to Structure: How Memory Architecture Drives Language Emergence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00233)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨两个智能体如何从零开始发明共享语言，发现在刘易斯信令游戏中，记忆架构比信道容量更关键；持久私有笔记本架构能利用冗余信道容量避免高容量崩溃，实现最可靠的协调（容量=25时达0.867±0.023），无状态智能体在中等容量达峰后随词表增长而退化；笔记本外部化约定，使智能体无需每轮重新推导编码；信息瓶颈预测的最优容量（等于物体数）实为脆弱点，冗余容量普遍更优；信道容量单独无法预测协调，记忆架构决定智能体能否将交互历史转化为稳定约定，两者结合才能理解信号如何成为语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两个智能体如何仅通过交互历史在无预设代码情况下协调出共享语言，探究记忆架构与信道容量对协调涌现的影响。

Method: 在刘易斯信令游戏中，使用大语言模型智能体，测试五种记忆架构（包括持久私有笔记本、无状态滚动上下文窗口等）在不同信道容量配置下的表现，量化协调成功率。

Result: 持久笔记本架构在容量=25时达到最高协调率（0.867±0.023），并随容量增加持续受益；无状态智能体在中等容量峰值后因词表超出上下文窗口而性能下降；信息瓶颈预测的最优容量（8）实为脆弱点，冗余容量普遍更有利；记忆架构决定能否将历史转化为稳定约定，单纯信道容量无法预测协调效果。

Conclusion: 记忆架构比信道容量更关键，持久外部存储使智能体能积累稳定惯例，避免高容量崩溃；理解信号如何演化为语言需同时考虑记忆架构与信道容量两个维度。

Abstract: How do two agents invent a shared language from scratch? In a Lewis signaling game, a sender and receiver must coordinate on a code using only their interaction history. We study five memory architectures across varying channel configurations with LLM agents and find that memory architecture matters more than channel capacity. Agents with a persistent private notebook benefit from surplus channel capacity and avoid the high-capacity collapse seen in stateless agents, achieving the most reliable coordination ($0.867 \pm 0.023$ at capacity = 25). Stateless agents peak at moderate capacity and then degrade as the vocabulary grows beyond what a rolling context window can track The notebook externalizes learned conventions, freeing agents from having to re-derive codes each round. An information bottleneck-inspired argument predicts an optimal capacity equal to the number of objects. Instead, the bottleneck (capacity = 8) proves to be a fragility point, and surplus capacity is generally better. We show that channel capacity alone cannot predict coordination; memory architecture determines whether agents turn interaction history into stable conventions, and both dimensions are needed to understand how signals become language.

</details>


### [181] [Seed2.0 Model Card: Towards Intelligence Frontier for Real-World Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00248)
*Bytedance Seed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Seed2.0 是一个面向复杂真实世界任务的模型系列，通过基于用户真实需求构建可靠的前瞻性评估体系，重点攻克长尾知识和复杂指令跟随两大难题，在推理智能、视觉理解和搜索能力上达到世界领先水平，已为数亿用户提供实际价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有模型在处理复杂、长周期真实任务时可靠性不足的问题，论文旨在识别用户真实需求，建立立足于现实复杂场景的评估基准体系，以此指导模型解决长尾知识缺失和复杂指令跟随两大持久挑战。

Method: 首先通过抽象用户真实需求构建面向复杂场景的评估体系；然后以此评估体系为指导，重点优化模型在长尾知识覆盖和复杂指令跟随方面的能力；同时强化推理智能、视觉理解和搜索等核心通用能力。

Result: Seed2.0 在推理智能、视觉理解和搜索能力上达到世界领先水平，显著提升了模型在复杂长周期任务上的可靠性，已开始展现处理初步复杂真实世界任务的能力，为数亿用户创造实际价值。

Conclusion: Seed2.0 通过需求驱动的评估体系指导模型开发，在解决长尾知识和复杂指令跟随两大核心难题上取得实质性进展，标志着大模型向解决复杂真实世界任务迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: We present Seed2.0, a model series that takes a meaningful step toward solving complex, real-world tasks. Our approach begins with identifying users' genuine needs and constructing a reliable, forward-looking evaluation system by selecting and abstracting benchmarks grounded in these needs and in realistic, complex scenarios. Guided by this evaluation system, Seed2.0 targets two persistent challenges, long-tail knowledge and complex instruction following, substantially improving the model's reliability on intricate, long-horizon tasks. Beyond these, Seed2.0 delivers world-leading reasoning intelligence, visual understanding, and search capabilities that address the most common needs of a broad user base. Through extensive real-world use cases documented in this model card, we demonstrate that Seed2.0 begins to exhibit the ability to handle initial complex real-world tasks, delivering greater value to hundreds of millions of users.

</details>


### [182] [Mnemosyne: Agentic Transaction Processing for Validating and Repairing AI-generated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00269)
*Edward Y. Chang,Longling Geng,Emily J. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Agentic Transaction Processing (ATP) 事务模型，将生成式代理的动作视为不可信提案，仅在通过确定性约束集 C 的准入检查后才提交。在 Mnemosyne 运行时中实现了追加式日志、有效状态投影、依赖安全补偿和主动承诺记录，证明了四项安全性质与有界反应式修复保证，实验显示开销 <6% 且局部修复操作比全局重算少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLMs、求解器和代理团队生成的工作流动作、修复和计划虽语法合法，却可能陈旧、不可行、冲突或破坏触发修复的证据，导致系统不可靠。

Method: 提出 ATP 模型：将生成动作视为不受信提案，在可执行约束集 C 下经确定性准入后才提交；运行时 Mnemosyne 采用追加式转换日志、有效状态投影、依赖安全补偿和主动承诺记录，配合局部补偿修复协议 (LCRP) 实现有界反应式修复。

Result: 证明了相对于 C 的四项安全性质（权限分离、串行等价生成准入、证据保持修复、义务包容）及有界反应式修复保证；可复现制品在九项伪造测试中拦截所有目标违规、仍放行合法工作，投影与验证开销 <6%，局部修复编辑操作比全局重算少一个数量级。

Conclusion: ATP 将生成层的胜任度、诚实度与学习能力从已提交状态的正确性中解耦，通过运行时确定性准入与有界反应式修复，为生成式代理系统提供了可形式化验证的安全基座。Mnemosyne 已开源。

Abstract: LLMs, solvers, and agent teams increasingly generate workflow actions, repairs, and plans, but a generated action may be syntactically valid yet stale, infeasible, conflicting, or destructive of the evidence that triggered a repair. We introduce Agentic Transaction Processing (ATP), a transaction model that treats generated actions as untrusted proposals until they pass deterministic admission under a declared, executable constraint set C. The principle is two-sided: a proposal is not truth, and no proposal foresees every disruption: anything may propose, but only the runtime admits and commits, and when an unforeseen disruption strikes it repairs reactively within bounds rather than trusting a fresh proposal. Relative to C, committed-state correctness becomes independent of the competence, honesty, or learning of the proposing layer. We realize ATP in Mnemosyne, a runtime with an append-only transition log, effective-state projection, dependency-safe compensation, and active commitment records, and prove four safety properties relative to C (authority separation, serial-equivalent generative admission, evidence-preserving repair, and obligation containment) together with a bounded-reactive-repair guarantee for its localized repair protocol (LCRP). A reproducible artifact rejects the targeted violations across nine falsification tests while still admitting valid work, at under 6% projection-and-validation overhead, and bounded local repair edits an order of magnitude fewer operations than global recompute. Mnemosyne is open source: https://github.com/eyuchang/Mnemosyne/tree/arxiv-atp-rq1-rq9b-r8-v2.

</details>


### [183] [Managed Autonomy at Runtime: Gear-Based Safety and Governance for Single- and Multi-Agent Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00334)
*Srini Ramaswamy,Wang Miaosheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种离散时间控制系统，通过五个执行齿轮与效用门控调度解决自主代理在无人类监督下的安全、稳定性和连续性问题，并在单代理与多代理CPS中分别给出理论证明与实验验证，实现了高异常检测率、低延迟及形式化安全证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主代理（LLM驱动软件代理或机器人物理代理）在无持续人类监督下运行时，面临未验证动作导致的安全违规、无约束循环导致的行为不稳定、未处理错误状态导致的连续性丢失等共性失效模式，亟需一种统一的运行时治理框架来保障安全与稳定。

Method: 开发了一个名为system的离散时间控制系统，包含五个执行齿轮（观测Gobs、建议Gsug、规划Gplan、执行Gexec、交互Gint），结合效用门控调度与事件驱动回退机制。单代理情况下证明了单调稳定性、执行安全性、最终稳定性、回退完备性及与齿轮约束马尔可夫决策过程的等价性。多代理CPS场景下，引入SMART托管自治生命周期，将运行时证据映射至四个治理状态（稳定/元/辅助/受控），利用共识门控、群体级Lyapunov分析、逐代理齿轮权限与汇聚控制，提供分布式安全稳定性保证（含零碰撞假设）。

Result: 在三代理UR5机器人装配单元上，基于NIST机械臂位置精度退化数据集校准故障幅度，开展10,000次蒙特卡洛实验。系统实现99.6%异常检测率（单代理基线仅2.1%），检测延迟降低3.5倍，并给出形式化物理工作空间安全证书。

Conclusion: 执行齿轮作为SMART运行时治理状态下的微级权限，成功将动作控制与自治治理分离，为自主代理在无人监督下的长时运行提供了理论可证、工程可用的安全稳定性保障框架。

Abstract: Autonomous agents, whether LLM-driven software agents or robotic physical agents, face a common class of failure modes when operating without continuous human oversight: safety violations from unverified actions, behavioral instability from unconstrained loops, and continuity loss from unhandled error states. We develop \system{}, a discrete-time control system that combines five execution gears (\Gobs{}, \Gsug{}, \Gplan{}, \Gexec{}, \Gint{}) with utility-gated dispatch and event-driven fallback. For the single-agent case, we prove monotonic stability, execution safety, eventual stabilization, fallback completeness, and equivalence to a gear-constrained Markov decision process. For multi-agent cyber-physical systems (CPS), we apply the established \smart{} managed-autonomy lifecycle and map runtime evidence into its four governance states (\Stable{}/\Meta{}/\Assisted{}/\Regulated{}). Consensus gating, swarm-level Lyapunov analysis, per-agent gear authority, and rendezvous control provide distributed safety and stability guarantees, including zero collision under the stated assumptions. We evaluate the resulting runtime on a three-agent UR5 robotic assembly cell using fault magnitudes calibrated from the NIST \emph{Degradation Measurement of Robot Arm Position Accuracy} dataset across 10,000 Monte Carlo episodes. It achieves a 99.6\% anomaly detection rate versus 2.1\% for the single-agent baseline, reduces detection latency by $3.5\times$, and supplies a formal physical-workspace safety certificate. The execution gears act as micro-level permissions beneath the \smart{} runtime governance states, separating action control from autonomy governance.

</details>


### [184] [Personalization as Inverse Planning: Learning Latent Design Intents for Agentic Slide Generation via Structural Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00407)
*Tianci Liu,Zihan Dong,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang,jing Gao,Emre Kiciman,Ranveer Chandra,Wei-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SPIRE 框架，将页面级幻灯片个性化（PSP）建模为逆规划问题，通过结构去噪任务和多智能体强化学习，在无需预设模板或工具知识的情况下实现细粒度幻灯片设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的幻灯片生成方法依赖预设模板或冗长指令，难以捕捉隐性设计意图，导致页面级个性化（PSP）问题未被解决。

Method: 将 PSP 形式化为逆规划问题，设计 SPIRE 框架：故意破坏干净幻灯片的视觉结构构建可验证的去噪任务，两个智能体通过强化学习协同优化可执行设计；理论证明结构去噪是 PSP 的一致代理目标，且多智能体形式严格降低了策略梯度方差。

Result: 大量实验表明 SPIRE 在页面级幻灯片个性化任务上优于基线方法，验证了理论分析的有效性。

Conclusion: SPIRE 通过结构去噪和多智能体强化学习，无需依赖特定工具知识即可实现细粒度幻灯片个性化，为 AI 辅助幻灯片设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Slide design requires personalizing both deck themes and page layouts. Yet, current AI agent-based methods struggle with fine-grained, page-level design. Solely relying on prespecified templates or user verbose instructions, they fail to capture latent design intents, leaving Page-level Slide Personalization (PSP) unresolved. To close this gap, this work formulates PSP as an inverse planning problem. We propose to learn a design intent without assuming any knowledge of the specific executing tools (e.g., PowerPoint, Beamer) being used. However, relinquishing control over these tools makes the problem intractable to optimize end-to-end. To overcome this, we propose SPIRE, a principled framework to solve PSP approximately. By intentionally corrupting the visual structures of clean slides, SPIRE creates a verifiable task to denoise the corruption, whereby two agents learn to collaboratively refine executable designs via reinforcement learning (RL). We present a proof that structural denoising is a consistent surrogate for PSP, and that the multi-agent formulation strictly reduces policy gradient variance in RL. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of SPIRE.

</details>


### [185] [PHREEQC-MCQ-200: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Tool-Augmented Scientific Simulator Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00436)
*Ke Zhang,Sahchit Chundur,Mohammad Javad Qomi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PHREEQC-MCQ-200 基准，评估工具增强型大语言模型在确定性水化学模拟任务上的表现。实验表明：模拟器访问能显著提升整体准确率，但也会导致部分原本正确的题目答错（退化现象）；输出访问协议（如目录式接口）对强模型有益、对中端模型有害。作者主张科学智能体评估应超越单一准确率，报告逐项保持率、输出访问敏感性、轨迹失败及计算链断裂点等细粒度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体日益接入科学软件，但何时工具访问能真正提升科学计算的可靠性、而非仅增加复杂性，仍不明确。现有评估多关注工具调用能力本身，缺乏端到端的诊断性基准，无法揭示工具使用中的退化、导航困难等深层问题。

Method: 构建 PHREEQC-MCQ-200 基准：从 21 个经验证的 PHREEQC 场景衍生 200 道多选题。要求智能体构造模拟器输入、执行 PHREEQC、检查结构化输出并给出最终答案。在多个前沿及中端模型族上实验，对比有/无工具访问、不同输出访问协议（完整输出 vs. 目录式接口）下的表现，并分析逐项保持/退化、轨迹失败模式。

Result: 1. 模拟器访问显著提升聚合准确率，证实落地执行对许多科学计算任务是必要的。2. 增益非单调：工具增强智能体会丢失原本无工具时答对的题目，平均准确率掩盖了退化。3. 输出访问协议影响大：目录式接口能降低 Token 成本且对强模型保持/提升准确率，但会损害无法可靠导航结构化输出的中端模型性能。

Conclusion: PHREEQC-MCQ-200 将科学工具使用定框为端到端诊断问题，而非简单的工具调用能力测试。科学智能体评估应报告：准确率、逐项保持率、输出访问敏感性、轨迹失败类型、以及计算链在何处断裂等多维指标，以全面刻画工具增强带来的收益与风险。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly connected to scientific software, yet it remains unclear when tool access makes scientific computation more reliable rather than merely more complex. We introduce PHREEQC-MCQ-200, a benchmark for evaluating tool-augmented agents on deterministic aqueous-geochemistry simulations. The benchmark contains 200 multiple-choice questions derived from 21 validated PHREEQC scenarios, requiring agents to construct simulator inputs, execute PHREEQC, inspect structured outputs, and commit to final answers.
  Across multiple frontier and mid-tier model families, simulator access substantially improves aggregate accuracy, confirming that grounded execution is necessary for many scientific-computation tasks. However, the gains are not monotonic: tool-augmented agents also lose items they answered correctly without tools, revealing regressions that average accuracy alone hides. We further show that output-access protocol matters. A table-of-contents interface can reduce token cost while preserving or improving accuracy for stronger models, but it degrades performance for mid-tier models that cannot reliably navigate structured simulator outputs.
  PHREEQC-MCQ-200 therefore frames scientific tool use as an end-to-end diagnostic problem rather than a simple tool-calling capability. We argue that evaluations of scientific agents should report not only accuracy, but also item-level retention, output-access sensitivity, trajectory failures, and where the computation chain breaks.

</details>


### [186] [Agri-SAGE: Simulation-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM for Context-Aware Agricultural Advisory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00454)
*Vedant Balasubramaniam,Geetha Charan,Manojkumar Patil,Rohit P Suresh,V Priyanka,Kodur Sai Vinay Sathvik,Y. Narahari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agri-SAGE 是一个闭环框架，结合检索增强的多智能体 LLM 推理与 APSIM 生物物理模拟，生成并验证农艺建议，评估了三种推理策略（Plan-and-Solve、Tree of Thoughts、Reflexion）在 10 年回顾性分析中的表现，均显著优于静态 PoP 基线，其中 Tree of Thoughts 产量最高，Reflexion 以更低计算成本实现相当农艺效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决农业咨询系统中静态农艺指南无法适应季内变异和动态不确定性，以及 LLM 直接生成建议可能农艺可信但生理上不合理的两大局限。

Method: 构建 Agri-SAGE 闭环框架：整合检索增强的多智能体 LLM 推理与 APSIM 生物物理模拟，实现建议的生成与验证；对比三种推理方法——Plan-and-Solve、Tree of Thoughts、Reflexion，在 10 年历史数据上进行回顾性评估。

Result: 三种推理方法均显著优于静态 PoP 基线；Tree of Thoughts 实现最高峰值产量；Reflexion 利用跨季节情景记忆，在大幅降低计算成本的前提下达到相当的农艺效果。

Conclusion: Agri-SAGE 通过融合 LLM 推理与生物物理模拟，有效缓解了静态指南的僵化与纯 LLM 建议的生理不可信问题；Reflexion 因其跨季节记忆机制，在性能与计算效率间取得最佳平衡，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Agricultural advisory systems face a fundamental tension: static agronomic guidelines offer consistent, evidence-based recommendations, yet remain blind to in-season variability and dynamic uncertainties. Recent advisory systems powered by LLMs are liable for a different risk of generating recommendations that are agronomically credible but physiologically unconvincing. Agri-SAGE is a closed-loop framework designed to resolve the above two limitations by integrating retrieval-grounded multi-agent LLM reasoning with APSIM-based biophysical simulation, to generate and validate agronomic advisories. To assess this framework, we evaluate three reasoning approaches, namely Plan-and-Solve, Tree of Thoughts, and Reflexion, over a 10-year retrospective analysis. All three significantly outperform static PoP (Package-of-Practice) baselines, with Tree of Thoughts achieving impressive peak yields. At the same time, Reflexion achieves comparable agronomic outcomes at substantially lower computational cost by leveraging cross-seasonal episodic memory.

</details>


### [187] [Multi-scale Mixture of World Models for Embodied Agents in Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00457)
*Jinwoo Jang,Daniel J. Rho,Sihyung Yoon,Hyunsuk Cho,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一种面向具身智能体的多尺度专家混合框架MuSix，通过两阶段路由机制基于体验距离进行尺度感知的世界模型选择，并采用尺度相关的遗忘率和门控跨尺度传递实现动态知识适应，在EmbodiedBench和HAZARD基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mixture of Experts (MoE) 在具身智能体真实环境中应用面临两大挑战：路由缺乏显式尺度概念，无法针对特定尺度进行定向更新；统一更新策略无法适应不同尺度知识过时速度的差异。

Method: 提出MuSix框架，包含两阶段路由机制：元路由器将基于建构水平理论的体验距离映射为连续尺度空间的权重，各尺度基础路由器在选定尺度内选择世界模型；自适应方面采用尺度相关遗忘率使低层知识快速刷新、高层抽象持久保留，并通过门控跨尺度传递维持层级一致性。

Result: 在EmbodiedBench和HAZARD基准实验中，MuSix在多尺度推理和动态适应任务上优于最先进基线。

Conclusion: MuSix通过尺度感知的世界模型混合与演进，有效解决了具身智能体在真实环境中的多尺度推理与知识自适应问题，为动态环境下的智能体提供了可扩展的架构。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating in the real world require multi-scale reasoning and knowledge adaptation as conditions change. We identify two challenges in applying Mixture of Experts (MoE) to this setting: routing lacks an explicit notion of scale, preventing targeted updates at specific scales, and a uniform update policy cannot accommodate the different rates at which knowledge at each scale becomes outdated. We present MuSix, a framework that addresses both challenges through scale-aware world model mixture and evolution. A two-stage routing mechanism grounds scale selection in experiential distance, a measure of situational novelty inspired by Construal Level Theory: a meta-router first maps this quantity to a weight over continuous scale space, then per-scale base routers select world models within the identified scale. For adaptation, scale-dependent forgetting rates allow low-scale knowledge to refresh rapidly while high-scale abstractions persist, and gated inter-scale transfer maintains coherence across the hierarchy. Experiments on EmbodiedBench and HAZARD show that MuSix improves over state-of-the-art baselines on multi-scale reasoning and dynamic adaptation.

</details>


### [188] [AI Native Games: A Survey and Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00527)
*Zhiyue Xu,Fandi Meng,Kaijie Xu,Clark Verbrugge,Simon Lucas,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了“AI原生游戏”的反事实定义，建立G/N双轴分类法，分析了53个AI原生游戏原型，指出核心设计挑战是将语义开放性转化为稳定玩法，并给出未来路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI虽能在运行时生成对话、任务、角色、图像和世界等内容，但仅有生成能力不等于AI原生游戏，也不能保证可玩性。需要明确界定AI原生游戏，区分AI增强、程序化内容生成、聊天机器人、酒馆式角色扮演等概念，并系统梳理现有原型，揭示设计核心问题与未来方向。

Method: 采用反事实标准定义AI原生游戏：若移除或平凡替换生成式AI组件，核心玩法循环崩溃或本质改变。据此筛选候选作品，收集分析53个公开AI原生游戏与原型。构建G/N双轴分类法：G轴捕捉面向玩家的游戏类型，N轴捕捉使生成式AI成为玩法不可或缺的核心AI机制。对语料库进行分类统计与设计模式分析。

Result: 语料库高度集中在语言导向设计，特别是叙事冒险、认知交互、生成式叙事三类；语义裁判、多智能体模拟、生成式构建、关系/陪伴玩法等类别代表性不足。核心设计问题在于将语义开放性组织为稳定玩法。AI原生设计依赖机械不变量：目标、规则、状态、反馈、节奏、玩家能动性，使开放式AI输出可解释且有后果。

Conclusion: 论文为AI原生游戏提供了定义框架、分类体系与实证分析，指出了从语义开放走向稳定玩法的关键机制，并提出可控生成、AI即机制设计、多模态多智能体、推理经济、评估、安全与监管等路线图，为该领域奠定研究基础。

Abstract: Generative AI now enables games to produce dialogue, quests, characters, images, and worlds at runtime. Yet generation alone does not make a game AI-native, nor does it guarantee playability. This paper defines AI-native games by whether runtime generative AI is constitutive of the core loop: if the AI component were removed or trivially replaced, the central form of play would collapse or become fundamentally different. This counterfactual criterion separates AI-native games from AI-augmented games, boundary artifacts, chatbots, tavern-style role-play, procedural content generation, and AI-assisted production. Using this definition, we screen candidate artifacts and analyze 53 publicly available AI-native games and prototypes. We introduce a dual-axis G/N taxonomy: the G-axis captures player-facing game type, while the N-axis captures the dominant AI mechanic that makes generative AI indispensable to play. The corpus is concentrated around language-forward designs, especially narrative adventure, epistemic interaction, and generative narrative, while categories such as semantic adjudication, multi-agent simulation, generative construction, and relationship/companion play remain less represented. We argue that the central design problem is organizing semantic openness into stable gameplay. AI-native design depends on mechanical invariants: goals, rules, state, feedback, pacing, and player agency that make open-ended AI outputs interpretable and consequential. We conclude with a roadmap for controllable generation, AI-as-mechanic design, multimodal and multi-agent systems, inference economics, evaluation, safety, and regulation.

</details>


### [189] [HARC: Coupling Harmfulness and Refusal Directions for Robust Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00572)
*Shei Pern Chua,Fangzhao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究对齐大语言模型内部安全表示机制，发现越狱攻击通过抑制提示端的拒绝或有害方向成功，并提出HARC微调方法，在提示端和响应端耦合有害性与拒绝方向，实现最优鲁棒性-能力-可用性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解对齐LLM内部如何表示安全性对于诊断对齐漏洞至关重要，因为这能解释越狱为何成功并指导鲁棒对齐策略设计。前人工作显示对齐LLM在提示端token位置将有害性和拒绝编码为可分离的残差流方向。

Method: 首先分析越狱攻击在提示编码阶段如何抑制拒绝或有害方向，发现不同攻击类别占据有害性-拒绝平面的可分离区域；进而扩展到响应token位置，发现模型在生成有害内容时能识别其有害性，即使提示端未识别。基于此提出HARC（有害性-拒绝耦合）微调方法，在提示端和响应端同时耦合这两个方向，干预仅限于有害性-拒绝子空间，保持残差流其余部分完整。

Result: 在大量实验中，HARC在六大基线方法（涵盖主流训练时和推理时安全方法）中实现最强鲁棒性-能力-可用性权衡，不降低通用能力也不增加过度拒绝。有害性和拒绝方向在五个模型家族和两种规模间无需架构特定调优即可迁移。

Conclusion: HARC通过在提示端和响应端耦合有害性与拒绝方向，有效增强模型安全鲁棒性，同时保持通用能力和可用性，为对齐安全提供了可迁移、轻量级的解决方案。

Abstract: Understanding how aligned LLMs internally represent safety is critical for diagnosing alignment vulnerabilities, as it explains why jailbreaks succeed and informs the design of robust alignment strategies. Prior work shows that aligned LLMs encode harmfulness and refusal as separable directions in the residual stream at prompt-side token positions. We show that jailbreaks succeed at prompt encoding by suppressing either the refusal or harmfulness direction before any token is generated, with distinct attack classes occupying separable regions of the harmfulness-refusal plane. Extending the analysis to response-token positions, we find that the model recognizes harmful content while it is generating that content, even when it failed to recognize the input as harmful at the prompt side. Motivated by our findings, we introduce HARC (Harmfulness-And-Refusal Coupling), a fine-tuning method that pairs the two directions across both prompt and response positions. Since the intervention is confined to the harmfulness-refusal subspace, it leaves the rest of the residual stream intact and does not degrade general capability or inflate over-refusal. Across extensive experiments, HARC achieves the strongest robustness-capability-usability trade-off among six baselines spanning the major training-time and inference-time safety methods. The harmfulness and refusal directions at prompt and response positions transfer across the five model families and two scales we tested without architecture-specific tuning.

</details>


### [190] [AGI Maze as a Benchmark Framework for World-Modeling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00627)
*Alexey Potapov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are powerful pattern-completion systems, but their default operating mode - predicting the next token from a static context - does not reliably produce persistent, manipulable representations of an external world. Many tasks that look like "reasoning" in text become substantially harder once the environment is partially observable, stateful, and requires memory and structured hypotheses about hidden state. AGI Maze is a lightweight framework for building such environments without requiring high-dimensional sensory inputs. It provides a family of grid-based maze tasks with a clean API and multiple difficulty regimes. The goal is to create benchmarks where agents must learn and use world state representations, not just infer a local rule over readily provided observations. We provide an initial evaluation of several vanilla LLMs on simple mazes showing that they fail to represent mazes internally at LLM inference time. We also introduce a baseline agent, which is allowed to use its message history as a working memory to construct descriptions of observations at agentic runtime. Although this can improve performance, it is still insufficient for an LLM agent to reliably solve even small mazes within a step budget that is more than enough for humans.

</details>


### [191] [Coachable agents for interactive gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00642)
*Roberto Capobianco,Harm van Seijen,Nolan D. Bard,Neil Burch,Fatima Davelouis,Josh Davidson,Alisa Devlic,Yunshu Du,Ishan Durugkar,Siddhant Gangapurwala,Daniel Hernandez,G. Zacharias Holland,Sahil Jain,Kenta Kawamoto,Raksha Kumaraswamy,Patrick MacAlpine,Dustin R. Morrill,Declan Oller,Francesco Riccio,Akanksha Saran,Craig Sherstan,Kaushik Subramanian,Thomas J. Walsh,Samuel Barrett,Kizza N. Frisbee,Mady Govil,Johannes Günther,Varun R. Kompella,James A. MacGlashan,Maxwell Svetlik,Michael D. Thomure,Jaden B. Travnik,Kevin Waugh,Elahe Aghapour,Florian Fuchs,Andreanne Lemay,Shruti Mishra,Takuma Seno,Peter Stone,Michael Spranger,Peter R. Wurman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于通用价值函数近似器(UVFAs)的强化学习框架，结合精心设计的训练场景、学习算法和数据增强，使智能体能在复杂领域中展示可控的“风格”行为，并在AAA游戏《地平线：西之绝境》、《GT赛车》和开源人形机器人领域验证，实现了运行时灵活选择最终行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习通常仅学习单一近最优行为，但许多实际应用需要实时控制任务的解决方式（即风格），现有方法缺乏运行时灵活调整行为的能力。

Method: 结合通用价值函数近似器(UVFAs)、精心选择的训练场景、学习算法和数据增强，构建可指导智能体在复杂领域展示风格的框架。

Result: 在赛车、游戏战斗、人形行走三个不同领域验证，智能体均能强一致性地响应风格请求，同时满足主要任务目标，且支持最终用户在运行时选择最终行为。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了强化学习智能体在复杂任务中可运行时控制的风格化行为，为游戏AI、机器人控制等领域提供了灵活的行为定制方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven to be a valuable tool in the creation of advanced AI and robotic systems, contributing to everything from game playing to robotics to foundation models. Through trial-and-error, these AI systems typically learn one, near-optimal behavior to solve their tasks. However, there are many use cases in which one would like to assert some level of control, preferably in real time, over how the task is solved. We refer to these modifications of a core task as styles. We combine universal value function approximators (UVFAs) with carefully selected training scenarios, learning algorithms, and data augmentation to create a framework for coaching agents that exhibit styles in complex domains. We demonstrate the framework's application in the AAA video games Horizon Forbidden West and Gran Turismo, and in an open-source humanoid test domain. Despite the different nature of the domains -- car racing, stylized game combat, and humanoid walking -- each agent shows strong coherence to the style requests while still satisfying the main task in its domain. Importantly, the techniques outlined in this paper allow an end user to choose the final behavior at run time, giving them flexible control over the final executed performance.

</details>


### [192] [Self-GC: Self-Governing Context for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00692)
*Xubin Hao,Hongjin Meng,Xin Yin,Jiawei Zhu,Chenpeng Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Self-GC 将长时程 LLM Agent 的上下文建模为可索引、可恢复的对象，引入侧通道规划器提出折叠/掩码/剪枝动作，并由执行框架强制可恢复边缘存储、安全提交边界与缓存感知提交，实现运行时生命周期级的上下文治理。在 Hard Set 上剪枝 43.95% 前缀 token 且 84.85% 未来续写无影响；生产级 332 会话套件中三种规划器骨干达 91.27%-94.58% 无影响率；线上账号级分流使日间平均输入 token 降 10%-15%，峰值近 20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程 Agent 依赖启发式剪枝（时间顺序截断、工具输出掩码）或最终自摘要，前者盲目于未来依赖，后者虽保留叙事状态却隐藏确切证据、定位符与可编辑工件，无法兼顾 token 经济与下游任务正确性。

Method: 1) 将用户轮次、工具跨度、技能状态封装为带索引的上下文对象；2) 引入侧通道规划器，基于对象依赖图提出 fold（折叠）、mask（掩码）、prune（剪枝）动作；3) 执行框架强制可恢复边缘存储、安全提交边界与缓存感知提交，实现对象级生命周期治理而非事后文本清理。

Result: Hard Set（33 会话）：剪枝 43.95% 前缀 token，84.85% 未来续写无影响，显著优于启发式基线 54.55%-69.70%。生产级套件（332 会话）：三规划器骨干无影响率 91.27%-94.58%，基线 77.71%-87.46%。线上账号级分流：日间平均输入 token 降 10%-15%，峰值近 20%。

Conclusion: 上下文管理应转向运行时对象级生命周期控制：通过索引化、可恢复的上下文对象与侧通道规划，实现精准剪枝且保留下游依赖，显著降低 token 成本并提升生产可用性。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate tool results, files, plans, and user constraints that are too structured to be treated as a disposable text suffix. Current systems mostly rely on in-run heuristics such as chronological pruning and tool-output masking, or on final self-summary near a context limit. Heuristics are cheap but blind to future dependencies; summaries preserve narrative state but often hide exact evidence, locators, and editable artifacts. We present Self-GC, where GC denotes self-governing context while deliberately echoing garbage collection: the system does not merely reclaim unused tokens, but governs the lifecycle of agent context objects. Self-GC turns user turns, tool spans, and skill state into indexed objects; asks a side-channel planner to propose fold, mask, and prune actions; and lets the harness enforce recoverable sidecars, safe commit boundaries, and cache-aware commit. On a 33-session Hard Set, Self-GC prunes 43.95% of prefix tokens while leaving 84.85% of future continuations unaffected, compared with no-impact rates of 54.55% to 69.70% for heuristic baselines. On a 332-session production-derived suite, three planner backbones reach no-impact rates of 91.27% to 94.58%, while baselines remain at 77.71% to 87.46%. In production, an online account-level split reduces daytime average input tokens by 10% to 15%, with peak reductions near 20%. These results point to context management as runtime lifecycle control over indexed, recoverable objects rather than post hoc text cleanup.

</details>


### [193] [Self-Evolving Agents with Anytime-Valid Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00871)
*Biswa Sengupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SEA架构，通过冻结基础模型并限制自我修改于小型转向适配器和版本化控制器，结合随时有效的门控机制和五种验证器在环机制，在SWE-bench Verified子集上显著提升强基础模型性能，且能防止性能倒退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化智能体违反了学习理论保证的假设（数据、评估器、组件、假设空间由被更新的策略产生），需要一种能提供可审计证书、限制自我修改范围并保证安全性的架构。

Method: SEA架构：冻结基础模型，仅允许通过小型转向适配器和版本化控制器进行自我修改；采用随时有效门控机制，基于固定错误预算发放可审计证书；五种循环控制器组合已发布的保证；五种验证器在环机制（best-of-N、微步搜索、自我编写复现预言机、搜索层控制、自我修复）提供密集、无评分器的信号，仅从问题文本计算。

Result: 在52实例SWE-bench Verified子集上跨四个基础模型实验，基础能力是主要无混淆效应；在两个强基础模型上，故意的空操作组合控制隔离出套件贡献为+4和+5（GLM 5.2从24到28；GPT从29到34，达到65%最佳）；事件日志确认机制触发并防止倒退。结果为单次运行，运行间方差和自适应任务算法组合为未来工作。

Conclusion: SEA架构通过冻结基础模型、限制自我修改范围、引入可审计门控和验证器在环机制，有效解决了自我进化智能体的安全性和可靠性问题，在代码生成任务上显著提升强基础模型性能，且能防止性能倒退，为可信赖的自我进化系统提供了实用范式。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents violate the assumption behind most learning-theoretic guarantees: the data, evaluator, components, and hypothesis space are produced by the policy being updated. We present \textbf{SEA}, an architecture that confines self-modification to a small steering adapter and a versioned harness around a \emph{frozen} base model and admits each modification only through an anytime-valid gate that emits an auditable certificate against a fixed error budget. Five loop controllers compose published guarantees; because such gates can only \emph{select} among behaviors the frozen base already produces, five verifier-in-the-loop mechanisms -- best-of-$N$, micro-step search, self-authored reproduction oracles, search-layer control, and self-repair -- supply the dense, grader-free signal the gates require, computed from the issue text alone. On a $52$-instance SWE-bench Verified subset across four base models, base capability is the dominant, confound-free effect, and on two strong base models a deliberate no-op-composite control isolates the suite's contribution at $+4$ and $+5$ (\textsc{Glm}~5.2 $24\to28$; \textsc{Gpt} $29\to34$, the $65\%$ best), with event logs confirming that its mechanisms fire and prevent regressions. Results are single-run on expensive evaluations; confirming run-to-run variance and adapting the per-task algorithm mix are future work.

</details>


### [194] [Two AI Metrics Diverged: Will it Make All the Difference?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00913)
*Alex Fogelson,Zachary A. Brown,Hans Gundlach,Jayson Lynch,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨前沿AI模型能力是否会超越小预算开发者可及范围，还是能力会收敛（'温和模型继承大地'）。研究表明答案取决于如何评估和衡量AI能力：验证损失显示差距缩小，但其他指标显示前沿模型领先优势持续扩大。通过按与训练/推理算力关系的函数形式分类性能指标，给出了判断哪类指标有利于温和模型的严格数学条件——有界性能指标总是有利。但需谨慎解读：许多常见有界指标存在紧密相关的无界对应指标（反之亦然）。确定适用指标是政策制定前提，因为有界与无界指标可能建议相反的政策响应。若软件工程、合成生物学、修辞说服力等能力在我们关心的衡量术语下是无界的，前沿级能力将集中在少数富裕行为者手中；若是有界的，则会通过温和模型扩散到大众手中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着算力指数级扩展，核心政策问题是前沿AI模型能力是否会持续超越资源受限开发者的可及范围，还是能力趋于收敛使'温和模型'普及。现有研究（Gundlach et al. 2025b）为基础，但缺乏系统性的指标分类框架来判断何种衡量方式下会出现哪种情形。

Method: 按性能指标与训练/推理算力关系的函数形式进行分类，推导判断指标是否有利于温和模型的严格数学条件；对比有界与无界指标的行为差异，分析常见指标对（有界/无界对应关系）的政策蕴含。

Result: 验证损失等指标显示前沿与温和模型差距收敛；但许多其他指标显示前沿模型领先优势无限增长。有界性能指标在数学上必然有利于温和模型（能力收敛）；无界指标则导致前沿能力持续集中。同一能力在不同衡量术语下可呈现有界或无界特性，导致截然不同的政策建议。
